### [1] [Seeing without Looking: Do Vision-Language Benchmarks Really Test Vision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22903)
*Zixuan Lan,Luzhe Sun,Matthew R. Walter,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLMs）在标准幻觉基准上即使移除大量图像token也仅轻微下降，说明当前基准无法可靠评估VLMs的细粒度视觉理解能力。作者通过多粒度行为分析和表征层面分析揭示了这一不匹配现象。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准准确率常被默认反映VLMs对视觉证据的依赖程度，但作者发现移除大量图像token后模型性能仅轻微下降，这促使他们系统探究基准准确率与真实视觉理解之间的不匹配问题。

Method: 采用多粒度分析方法：(1) 全局视觉退化、局部遮挡、问题重述、答案空间扩展等行为层面分析；(2) 超越标准准确率的决策层面分析；(3) 视觉token几何的逐层表征分析。

Result: VLMs虽整合视觉输入，但其预测对细粒度视觉证据缺失的敏感度低于标准准确率所暗示的水平；即使最终预测不变，模型内部对正确答案的支持可能已被削弱；深层视觉token的相似性增加为上述发现提供了可能的解释。

Conclusion: 当前基准不足以可靠评估VLMs的细粒度视觉基础能力。

Original Abstract: Benchmark accuracy is often implicitly assumed to reflect grounded visual understanding in vision-language models (VLMs), yet it remains unclear to what extent such scores truly reflect reliance on visual evidence. Motivated by a surprising observation that removing a substantial fraction of image tokens only degrades model performance very slightly on a widely used hallucination benchmark, we systematically investigate this mismatch in a set of open-source VLMs. Our analysis spans multiple levels of granularity, spanning global visual degradation, localized occlusion, question reformulation, answer-space expansion, and decision-level analyses beyond standard accuracy. We further complement these behavioral results with a layer-wise analysis of vision-token geometry. Throughout the experiments, we find that although VLMs do incorporate visual input, their predictions are less sensitive to the loss of fine-grained visual evidence that standard accuracy should have suggested. Even when the final prediction remains unchanged, the model's internal support for the correct answer may already be weakened. We further complement a representation-level analysis, which shows increasing similarity among visual tokens in deeper layers, providing a possible explanation for our findings. Together, these results suggest that current benchmarks are not sufficient to reliably evaluate fine-grained visual grounding in VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 基准准确率常被默认反映视觉语言模型（VLMs）对视觉证据的依赖程度，但这类分数在多大程度上真正体现对视觉证据的依赖仍不清楚。受一个令人惊讶的现象——在一个广泛使用的幻觉基准上，移除大量图像token仅使模型性能轻微下降——的启发，我们在一组开源VLM中系统研究了这一不匹配问题。我们的分析涵盖多个粒度层级：全局视觉退化、局部遮挡、问题重述、答案空间扩展，以及超越标准准确率的决策层面分析。我们进一步用视觉token几何的逐层分析来补充这些行为结果。在整个实验中，我们发现，尽管VLMs确实整合了视觉输入，但其预测对细粒度视觉证据缺失的敏感度低于标准准确率所暗示的水平。即使最终预测保持不变，模型内部对正确答案的支持可能已被削弱。我们还补充了表征层面的分析，显示深层视觉token之间的相似性增加，这为我们的发现提供了可能的解释。综合来看，这些结果表明当前基准不足以可靠评估VLMs的细粒度视觉基础能力。

</details>


### [2] [Suicide Risk Assessment from AI-powered Video Surveillance: An Interpretable Framework for Prevention in Metro Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22904)
*Safwen Naimi,Wassim Bouachir,Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau,Brian Mishara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次正式定义了地铁站自杀风险评估（SRA）任务，并提出了一个可解释的框架，通过整合人物追踪、活动识别、站台语义分割和轨迹驱动的风险热图建模，在真实监控数据上达到83.2%的ROC-AUC。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地铁站监控视频中的自杀风险评估对于早期识别高风险情况、实现及时干预至关重要。现有方法要么关注孤立的子任务，要么试图直接推断意图，缺乏从累积证据中综合评估自杀风险的可解释框架。

Method: 提出一个可解释的SRA框架，包含四个关键模块：人物追踪（person tracking）、活动识别（activity recognition）、站台语义分割（semantic segmentation of the platform）以及轨迹驱动的风险热图建模（trajectory-driven risk heatmap modeling）。该框架通过整合这些异构行为线索，从累积证据中评估自杀风险。

Result: 在真实监控数据上，完整操作流程达到了83.2%的ROC-AUC。

Conclusion: 本文通过将SRA形式化为一个独立任务并建立完整的操作基线，揭示了自杀风险评估的复杂性，并为面向社会公益的可解释AI系统研究开辟了新方向。

Original Abstract: Understanding and monitoring human behavior in metro stations play an important role in supporting suicide prevention efforts, where early identification of high-risk situations can enable timely intervention. This requires assessing suicide risk from a surveillance video by jointly reasoning about the behavior of each passenger, his/her spatial context, and temporal dynamics. However, this assessment using videos captured by surveillance cameras is challenging, as it demands accurate perception of human motion, understanding of platform geometry, and aggregation of heterogeneous behavioral cues over time. In this work, we formalize the task of Suicide Risk Assessment (SRA) in metro stations and introduce the first interpretable framework that addresses this challenge. Unlike approaches that focus on isolated subtasks or attempt to infer intent directly, our formulation assesses suicide risk from accumulated evidence by incorporating person tracking, activity recognition, semantic segmentation of the platform, and trajectory-driven risk heatmap modeling. By formalizing SRA as a distinct task and benchmarking a complete operational pipeline achieving 83.2% ROC-AUC on real surveillance data, this work highlights the complexity of suicide risk assessment and opens new directions for research on interpretable AI systems for social good.

中文摘要翻译: 理解和监控地铁站中的人类行为对于支持自杀预防工作至关重要，早期识别高风险情况可实现及时干预。这需要从监控视频中通过联合推理每位乘客的行为、其空间上下文以及时间动态来评估自杀风险。然而，利用监控摄像头捕获的视频进行评估具有挑战性，因为它要求准确感知人体运动、理解站台几何结构，并随时间聚合异构行为线索。在这项工作中，我们正式定义了地铁站自杀风险评估（SRA）任务，并引入了第一个应对该挑战的可解释框架。与关注孤立子任务或试图直接推断意图的方法不同，我们的公式通过整合人物追踪、活动识别、站台语义分割以及轨迹驱动的风险热图建模，从累积证据中评估自杀风险。通过将SRA形式化为一个独立任务，并在真实监控数据上建立一个达到83.2% ROC-AUC的完整操作基线，本工作凸显了自杀风险评估的复杂性，并为面向社会公益的可解释AI系统研究开辟了新方向。

</details>


### [3] [VideoOdyssey: A Benchmark for Ultra-Long-Context and Omni-Modal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22907)
*Haichen He,Jiayi Zhou,Sifeng Shang,Yihan Hu,Yuanhan Zhang,Kaiyang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoOdyssey 是一个针对超长视频理解设计的基准测试，通过引入“连续证明长度”指标，评估模型在极端时长（平均109分钟）视频上的持续追踪、信息整合和记忆能力。实验表明当前多模态大模型不仅在简单检索上存在瓶颈，更在跨上下文长度的连续推理、细粒度感知和非语言全模态理解方面表现不佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频理解基准虽然提升了视频时长，但其评估任务通常只需理解短暂、孤立的视频片段，无法真正衡量超长上下文推理所需的高认知负荷。因此，需要设计一个能捕捉持续认知负荷的基准，推动模型在极端时长视频下的理解能力。

Method: 引入“连续证明长度”指标，定义为人类为准确回答问题必须连续观看的视频长度。基于此构建 VideoOdyssey 基准，包含三个关键特征：1）极端视频时长与多样性（11个领域54个子类，平均109分钟）；2）两个子集（VideoOdyssey-V 用于视觉理解，VideoOdyssey-AV 用于音视频同步理解）；3）从秒到小时共5个粒度级别的超长连续证明（视频子集平均16分钟，音视频子集平均12.8分钟）。

Result: 当前多模态大模型的瓶颈不仅限于简单检索，还包括跨不同上下文长度的连续推理困难、细粒度感知能力不足以及非语言全模态理解能力欠缺。

Conclusion: 视频Odyssey 能够有效诊断模型在不同上下文长度和认知负荷下的能力，揭示了现有模型在超长视频理解方面的系统性不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Original Abstract: Real-world long video understanding requires models to perform continuous tracking, information integration and memory retention over massive temporal spans within extreme video durations. Mastering this intense cognitive load constitutes the fundamental bottleneck in long video understanding. While existing benchmarks have driven progress by scaling up video duration, their evaluation tasks often require comprehending only short and isolated video segments, falling short of capturing the challenge of ultra-long-context reasoning. To measure this cognitive load, we emphasize continuous certificate length, defined as the video length a human must continuously watch to definitively answer a given question. Driven by this metric, we introduce VideoOdyssey, a benchmark specifically designed for ultra-long-context and omni-modal video understanding. VideoOdyssey is characterized by three key features: 1) Extreme video duration and diversity: spanning 11 domains and 54 subcategories with an average video duration of 109 minutes; 2) Comprehensive evaluation scenarios: offering two subsets to address different research focuses, i.e., VideoOdyssey-V for probing the limits of visual understanding in MLLMs, and VideoOdyssey-AV for evaluating synchronized audio-visual understanding for omni-modal models; 3) Ultra-long and multi-level continuous certificates: extending the average continuous certificate to 16 minutes for VideoOdyssey-V and 12.8 minutes for VideoOdyssey-AV. Crucially, we design 5 granular levels from seconds to hours, providing a comprehensive diagnostic tool to evaluate models across varying context lengths and cognitive loads. Extensive evaluations show that bottlenecks of current MLLMs extend beyond simple retrieval to include struggles with continuous reasoning across varying context lengths, fine-grained perception, and non-verbal omni-modal understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界中的长视频理解要求模型在极端视频时长内对海量时间跨度进行持续追踪、信息整合和记忆保持。掌握这种高强度的认知负荷是长视频理解的根本瓶颈。虽然现有基准通过扩展视频时长推动了进展，但其评估任务往往仅需理解短暂且孤立的视频片段，未能捕获超长上下文推理的挑战。为衡量这种认知负荷，我们强调连续证明长度，定义为人类为准确回答给定问题必须连续观看的视频长度。基于这一指标，我们引入 VideoOdyssey，一个专为超长上下文和全模态视频理解设计的基准。VideoOdyssey 具有三个关键特征：1）极端视频时长与多样性：涵盖11个领域和54个子类别，平均视频时长为109分钟；2）全面的评估场景：提供两个子集以应对不同的研究重点，即 VideoOdyssey-V 用于探索多模态大语言模型的视觉理解极限，以及 VideoOdyssey-AV 用于评估全模态模型的同步音视频理解能力；3）超长且多层次的连续证明：将 VideoOdyssey-V 的平均连续证明长度延长至16分钟，VideoOdyssey-AV 为12.8分钟。关键的是，我们设计了从秒到小时共5个粒度级别，为评估模型在不同上下文长度和认知负荷下的表现提供了全面的诊断工具。大量评估表明，当前多模态大语言模型的瓶颈不仅限于简单检索，还包括在跨不同上下文长度的连续推理、细粒度感知和非语言全模态理解方面的困难。

</details>


### [4] [GazeBehavior Annotation Toolkit (GBAT): AI-powered toolkit for automatic annotation of egocentric eye-tracking and video data of child-caregiver interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22962)
*Iba Baig,Kevin Li,Yanbin Xu,Seiji Cattelain,Marie Hallo,Hayato Ono,Sho Tsuji,Ming Bo Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于深度学习的注视行为标注工具包（GazeBehavior Annotation Toolkit），用于自动化处理儿童-看护者互动视频数据中的多摄像头同步、注视目标分类以及姿态和手部动作标注，从而提升数据预处理和特征提取的效率和可扩展性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 儿童-看护者互动视频数据的手动标注非常耗时，限制了大规模和纵向研究的发展，亟需自动化的工具来提升数据预处理和特征提取的效率与可扩展性。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度学习的注视行为标注工具包（GazeBehavior Annotation Toolkit），主要包含三个模块：多视频事后同步、注视目标类别的半自动标注、以及参与者姿态和手部动作的分类。

Result: 该工具包能够有效提升从人类自我中心眼动跟踪和视频数据中提取特征的效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工具包对于支持人类早期发展中注意动态和自然行为的大规模纵向研究具有重要意义。

Original Abstract: Video recordings of child-caregiver interactions enable investigation of attentional dynamics during naturalistic behavior. Such multimodal recording also allows researchers to examine how attention interacts with action and language use in real time. However, manual annotation of such data is time-consuming. Here, we introduce GazeBehavior Annotation Toolkit, a deep-learning-based toolkit designed to facilitate three key processes in data preprocessing and feature extraction: post-hoc synchronization across multiple videos, semi-automatic annotation of gaze target categories, and categorization of participants' poses and hand actions. This toolkit improves the efficiency and scalability of feature extraction from human egocentric eye-tracking and video data. Such improvement is critical in supporting large-scale and longitudinal investigations of attentional dynamics and naturalistic behavior in human early development.

中文摘要翻译: 儿童-看护者互动的视频记录使得研究者能够在自然行为过程中探究注意动态。这种多模态记录还允许研究者实时考察注意力如何与动作和语言使用相互作用。然而，对这类数据进行手动标注非常耗时。本文介绍了一种基于深度学习的注视行为标注工具包（GazeBehavior Annotation Toolkit），旨在促进数据预处理和特征提取中的三个关键过程：多视频事后同步、注视目标类别的半自动标注，以及参与者姿态和手部动作的分类。该工具包提升了从人类自我中心眼动跟踪和视频数据中提取特征的效率和可扩展性。这种改进对于支持人类早期发展中注意动态和自然行为的大规模纵向研究至关重要。

</details>


### [5] [Improved Vision-to-Chart Buoy Association with Learned World-to-Image Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22942)
*Borja Carrillo-Perez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对MaCVi 2026视觉到图表数据关联挑战，提出一种轻量级改进：在DETR融合转换器基线基础上，训练专用MLP（QueryMLP）显式预测浮标在水线处的图像接触点坐标，并将其附加到解码器查询向量中，为每个浮标提供直接的空间先验，降低转换器解码器的几何推理负担。该方法在测试集上取得Overall=0.7386, F1=0.8055, mIoU=0.6718，位列第二。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基线方法将世界空间距离和方位编码为查询向量，迫使转换器隐式学习从世界坐标到图像像素的复杂几何投影，几何推理负担过重。本文旨在通过显式预测图像坐标来减轻这一负担。

Method: 在DETR融合转换器基线基础上，额外训练一个专用MLP（QueryMLP），该MLP利用图表测量数据和IMU姿态数据，显式预测浮标在水线处的图像接触点坐标，然后将预测的像素坐标附加到基线解码器的查询向量中，为每个浮标提供直接的空间先验信息。

Result: 在保留测试集上，Overall分数为0.7386，F1=0.8055，mIoU=0.6718，在所有提交中排名第二。

Conclusion: 通过QueryMLP显式预测图像坐标并作为空间先验注入查询向量，可以有效降低转换器解码器的几何推理负担，提升DETR融合转换器在视觉到图表数据关联任务上的性能。

Original Abstract: This report presents a lightweight modification to the DETR-based fusion transformer baseline for the MaCVi 2026 Vision-to-Chart data association challenge. The challenge baseline decoder receives per-buoy queries encoding world-space distance and bearing, forcing the transformer to implicitly learn the complex geometric projection from world coordinates to image pixels. Instead, this work trains an additional dedicated MLP, QueryMLP, to explicitly predict the buoy's waterline contact point in the image from chart measurements and IMU orientation data. The predicted pixel coordinates are appended to the baseline decoder query vector, providing a direct spatial prior per buoy and reducing the geometric reasoning burden on the transformer decoder. On the challenge leaderboard, the presented approach achieves an Overall score of 0.7386, with F1 = 0.8055 and mIoU = 0.6718, on the held-out test set, placing second among all submissions.

中文摘要翻译: 本报告提出了一种针对DETR融合转换器基线的轻量级改进方法，用于MaCVi 2026视觉到图表数据关联挑战。挑战基线解码器接收编码了世界空间距离和方位的逐浮标查询，迫使转换器隐式学习从世界坐标到图像像素的复杂几何投影。本文则额外训练一个专用MLP（QueryMLP），利用图表测量数据和IMU姿态数据，显式预测浮标在水线处的图像接触点坐标。将预测的像素坐标附加到基线解码器的查询向量中，为每个浮标提供直接的空间先验信息，从而降低转换器解码器的几何推理负担。在挑战排行榜的保留测试集上，所提方法取得了Overall分数0.7386，F1=0.8055，mIoU=0.6718，在所有提交中位列第二。

</details>


### [6] [CoMoGen: COntrollable MOtion Dynamics and Interactions with Mask-Guided Video GENeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22996)
*Adil Meric,Lin Geng Foo,Mert Kiray,Benjamin Busam,Rishabh Dabral,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoMoGen是一个可控视频生成框架，通过二进制掩码序列和输入图像生成真实交互动态。它利用MaskAdapter编码掩码序列，并在MMDiT中通过余弦加权调度注入，使用LoRA微调识别出的"运动层"，在无需改变架构的情况下实现高效精确的运动控制，在运动保真度和感知真实性上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的可控视频生成方法多基于UNet架构的层次化由粗到细设计，而MMDiT（多模态扩散Transformer）由统一的Transformer块序列组成，难以识别哪些层负责运动生成。因此，需要一种新方法来定位MMDiT中的运动相关层，并实现高效的微调控制。

Method: (1) 提出轻量级MaskAdapter，将二进制掩码序列编码为潜在残差信号，通过余弦加权调度注入到MMDiT中；(2) 提出在MMDiT注意力空间中确定"运动层"的新方法；(3) 对识别出的运动层使用LoRA进行选择性微调，无需改变MMDiT架构。

Result: 在多个数据集上的综合实验表明，CoMoGen在运动保真度和感知真实性上持续优于先前的可控视频生成方法，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CoMoGen通过简单而高效的设计，实现了精确的主体运动和逼真的场景交互，并且计算成本更低，验证了在MMDiT中选择性适应运动关键组件的有效性。

Original Abstract: We present CoMoGen, a controllable video generation framework that generates realistic interactive dynamics from a single binary mask sequence conditioned on an input image. CoMoGen introduces a lightweight MaskAdapter that encodes binary mask sequences into a latent residual signal, injected into the Multi Modal Diffusion Transformer (MMDiT) model through a cosine-weighted schedule. Unlike the hierarchical coarse-to-fine design of UNet architectures, MMDiT operates as a sequence of uniform transformer blocks, making it difficult to identify which layers are responsible for the motion generation. Therefore, we propose a novel way to determine "Motion Layers" operating in the attention space of MMDiT. We fine-tune the model by using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to the Motion Layers, without requiring any architecture change in the MMDiT. This selective adaptation enables our method to focus on motion-critical components, yielding reduced computational cost. Despite its simplicity, CoMoGen enables precise subject motion and plausible interactions with surrounding humans, objects, and scenes. Comprehensive experiments on different datasets show that CoMoGen consistently outperforms prior controllable video generation methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance in motion fidelity and perceptual realism. Project page: mericadil.github.io/CoMoGen.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了CoMoGen，一个可控视频生成框架，能够从单一二进制掩码序列结合输入图像条件，生成真实的交互动态。CoMoGen引入了一个轻量级的MaskAdapter，将二进制掩码序列编码为潜在残差信号，通过余弦加权调度注入到多模态扩散Transformer（MMDiT）模型中。与UNet架构的层次化由粗到细设计不同，MMDiT由一系列统一的Transformer块组成，因此难以识别哪些层负责运动生成。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的方法，在MMDiT的注意力空间中确定"运动层"。我们使用低秩适配（LoRA）对运动层进行微调，无需对MMDiT进行任何架构修改。这种选择性适应使我们的方法能够聚焦于运动关键组件，从而降低计算成本。尽管设计简洁，CoMoGen能够实现精确的主体运动以及与周围人物、物体和场景的合理交互。在不同数据集上的综合实验表明，CoMoGen在运动保真度和感知真实性上持续优于先前的可控视频生成方法，达到了最先进的性能。项目页面：mericadil.github.io/CoMoGen。

</details>


### [7] [Scene Reconstruction as Mapping Priors for 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22997)
*Yang Fu,Yuliang Zou,Hao Xiang,Xin Huang,Yijing Bai,Chen Song,Weijing Shi,Govind Thattai,Dragomir Anguelov,Mingxing Tan,Yingwei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的解决方案，通过自动构建稠密地图先验并将其有效集成到3D检测框架中，显著提升了自动驾驶中的3D目标检测性能，在Waymo Open Dataset上达到新SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地图在自动驾驶中主要用于运动规划，但在3D目标检测等感知任务中未被充分利用。虽然地图能提供静态环境的强结构先验以解决传感器噪声和稀疏性问题，但传统高清地图构建和维护成本高昂。因此，需要一种可扩展的方式来利用地图先验提升3D检测。

Method: 提出MPA3D框架：1）构建自动管道，从聚合的传感器数据中密集重建地图先验，无需人工标注；2）设计有效的融合机制，将地图先验与不同传感器模态（如激光雷达、相机）集成，增强3D检测特征表示。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset上的大量实验表明，该方法达到了新的SOTA结果，验证了可扩展重建场景先验在提升3D检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过自动化构建稠密地图先验并将其与多模态传感器数据融合，可以有效提升3D目标检测性能，且无需昂贵的人工标注，具备大规模部署的潜力。

Original Abstract: In autonomous driving, mapping is critical for motion planning but remains an under-utilized resource for perception tasks such as 3D object detection. Maps can provide robust structural priors of the static environment, helping resolve ambiguities and correct for sensor data sparsity or noise, especially for distant objects or under adverse weather conditions. However, conventional High-Definition (HD) maps are resource-intensive to obtain and maintain, which presents a challenge for efficient, large-scale deployment. In this paper, we propose a scalable solution to systematically leverage mapping to improve 3D detection by overcoming two primary challenges. First, we introduce a pipeline to automatically build dense mapping priors from aggregated sensor data, eliminating the need for human labeling. Second, we design a novel Mapping Priors Augmented 3D Detection (MPA3D) framework to effectively integrate mapping priors with different sensor modalities. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset demonstrate that our approach achieves new state-of-the-art results, proving the effectiveness of scalable reconstructed scene priors for enhancing 3D detection.

中文摘要翻译: 在自动驾驶中，地图绘制对于运动规划至关重要，但在三维目标检测等感知任务中尚未被充分利用。地图可以提供静态环境的强结构先验，帮助解决歧义并纠正传感器数据的稀疏性或噪声，尤其是对于远距离目标或在恶劣天气条件下。然而，传统高清（HD）地图的获取和维护成本高昂，这给高效大规模部署带来了挑战。本文提出了一种可扩展的解决方案，通过克服两个主要挑战，系统性地利用地图来改进三维检测。首先，我们引入了一个从聚合传感器数据中自动构建稠密地图先验的流程，消除了人工标注的需求。其次，我们设计了一种新颖的MPA3D（地图先验增强的三维检测）框架，以有效集成地图先验与不同传感器模态。在Waymo Open Dataset上的大量实验表明，我们的方法达到了新的最先进结果，验证了可扩展重建场景先验在增强三维检测方面的有效性。

</details>


### [8] [The TIME Machine: On The Power of Motion for Efficient Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23045)
*Mantas Skackauskas,Xinyue Hao,Laura Sevilla-Lara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TIME（Temporally Informed Motion Embedding），一种仅使用合成运动数据、以运动为核心模态的视频表示学习方法。通过点轨迹掩码自编码器进行自监督学习，在零样本测试中，使用少4个数量级的训练数据即可达到与SOTA相当的性能，有效解决了视频模型规模成本高和语言依赖限制时间理解两个核心问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频表示学习面临两大局限：一是视频模型规模化成本高昂，二是依赖语言进行对比学习限制了可学习概念的范围（仅限于字幕中的概念），导致视频模型在时间理解方面仍然表现不佳。作者希望突破这些限制，寻求一种更高效、不依赖语言标注的视频表示学习方法。

Method: 以视频中的点轨迹（point-tracks）形式表示运动，将其作为核心模态；采用掩码自编码器（Masked Autoencoder），随机遮蔽部分轨迹，训练自编码器重建缺失的轨迹，从而实现自监督学习。整个模型仅使用合成运动数据进行训练，生成名为TIME（Temporally Informed Motion Embedding）的嵌入表示。

Result: 在零样本（zero-shot）测试中，TIME嵌入在多种任务上的表现与最先进模型相当，但使用的训练数据量减少了多达4个数量级（即10,000倍）。这表明运动作为中心模态可以大幅降低数据需求，同时提升细粒度时间理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向视频模型新范式的垫脚石——这一新范式下的视频模型既具有更强的时间感知能力，又具有更好的可扩展性。运动作为核心模态能够同时解决视频技术中的规模成本问题和语言依赖问题。

Original Abstract: Video representation learning has seen tremendous progress in recent years. This has been driven by many factors, including the scale of training and the success of visual models trained contrastively with language. While these factors have pushed the boundaries of what video models can do, they also introduce their own set of limitations: first, scaling video models can reach prohibitive costs and second, learning from language restricts the range of concepts that can be learned to those in captions. As a result, video models still struggle with temporal understanding. In this paper we propose a novel approach that uses motion as the central modality for video representation. In particular, given the motion in a video in the form of point-tracks, we use a masked-autoencoder to mask some of the tracks and train the autoencoder to reconstruct the missing tracks. This allows us to learn a representation in a self-supervised manner. We show that using motion to represent videos actually addresses both of the core limitations of video technology. First, it allows us to massively reduce the scale of training data, as motion is inherently appearance-independent and hence needs fewer examples to generalize well. Second, motion allows us to bypass the language-dependent training paradigm, learning better fine-grained concepts. The result is an embedding that we call TIME (Temporally Informed Motion Embedding), a representation trained exclusively on synthetic motion data. We test this embedding on a wide set of tasks in a zero-shot manner. We observe that without bells and whistles, performance is on par with state-of-the-art models using up to 4 orders of magnitude less training data. This is a stepping stone towards a new paradigm of video models that are both more temporally aware as well as more scalable.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，视频表示学习取得了巨大进展。这得益于多种因素，包括训练规模的扩大以及通过语言对比训练成功的视觉模型。虽然这些因素推动了视频模型能力的边界，但它们也带来了自身的局限性：首先，扩展视频模型可能达到令人望而却步的成本；其次，从语言中学习将可学习概念的范围限制在字幕所涵盖的范围内。因此，视频模型在时间理解方面仍然存在困难。本文提出了一种新颖的方法，将运动作为视频表示的核心模态。具体而言，给定视频中以点轨迹形式存在的运动信息，我们使用掩码自编码器遮蔽部分轨迹，并训练自编码器重建缺失的轨迹。这使得我们能够以自监督的方式学习表示。我们证明，使用运动来表示视频实际上解决了视频技术的两个核心局限性。首先，它能够大幅减少训练数据的规模，因为运动本质上是与外观无关的（appearance-independent），因此需要更少的样本即可实现良好的泛化。其次，运动使我们能够绕过依赖语言的训练范式，学习更好的细粒度概念。由此得到的嵌入称为TIME（Temporally Informed Motion Embedding, 时间感知运动嵌入），这是一种完全在合成运动数据上训练得到的表示。我们在零样本（zero-shot）方式下对多种任务进行了测试。我们发现，无需任何额外修饰，其性能与使用多达4个数量级更多训练数据的最先进模型相当。这是迈向视频模型新范式的垫脚石——这一新范式下的视频模型既具有更强的时间感知能力，又具有更好的可扩展性。

</details>


### [9] [RoboSurg-VQA: A Multimodal Benchmark for Surgical Segmentation-Aware Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23068)
*Chengyi Zhang,Zi Ye,Ziyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoboSurg-VQA是一个面向机器人辅助和微创手术的、基于分割感知的视觉问答基准数据集，通过对公开手术分割数据集进行重新标注和Schema统一构建，用于评估模型在手术影像中回答临床相关问题的能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在临床实践中，仅靠准确的分割掩码不足以满足需求，医生需要模型能够像语言问答一样理解手术过程中的上下文、可见性、伪影、解剖结构和手术器械等信息，尤其是在遮挡、烟雾、出血和高光等退化条件下。现有数据集缺乏这类结构化问答能力。

Method: 利用公开手术分割数据集，在统一Schema下重新标注；每个帧配对一组固定且临床动机明确的问题（涵盖过程上下文、解剖、模态/视角、手术伪影、图像质量、可见性与空间属性），采用封闭答案集以保证一致评估；通过约束提示生成候选答案并辅以自动有效性和一致性检查，再经人工审核提升合理性与标签一致性。

Result: 给出了基准数据集统计信息、合理性基线和常见评估挑战，特别是在困难手术条件下的表现。

Conclusion: RoboSurg-VQA为手术视觉问答提供了标准化基准，有助于推动模型在真实临床场景中回答结构化问题能力的发展。

Original Abstract: Reliable visual understanding in robot-assisted and minimally invasive surgery (RMIS/MIS) demands more than accurate masks: in clinical practice, clinicians pose language-like questions about procedural context, visibility, artefacts, and the presence of anatomical structures and surgical instruments, often under degraded views caused by occlusion, smoke, bleeding, and specular highlights. We present \textbf{RoboSurg-VQA}, a segmentation-aware visual question answering (VQA) benchmark built by repurposing public surgical segmentation datasets under a shared schema. Each frame is paired with a fixed set of clinically motivated questions spanning procedure context, anatomy (including region), imaging modality/view, surgical artefacts, image quality, and basic visibility and spatial attributes, with closed answer sets to enable consistent evaluation. To scale annotation, we generate candidate answers via constrained prompting with automatic validity and consistency checks, followed by human auditing to improve plausibility and label consistency. We report benchmark statistics, sanity baselines, and common evaluation challenges under challenging surgical conditions. The code will be available on https://github.com/ziyangwang007/Robosurg-VQA.

中文摘要翻译: 在机器人辅助和微创手术中，可靠的视觉理解不仅仅需要精确的分割掩码：在临床实践中，医生会就手术过程上下文、可见性、伪影以及解剖结构和手术器械的存在提出类似语言的问题，且这些问题往往在遮挡、烟雾、出血和高光反射等退化视图下提出。本文提出了\textbf{RoboSurg-VQA}，一个基于分割感知的视觉问答基准数据集，通过在一个统一Schema下重新利用公开手术分割数据集构建而成。每个帧与一组固定的临床驱动问题配对，涵盖手术过程上下文、解剖结构（包括区域）、成像模态/视角、手术伪影、图像质量以及基本的可见性和空间属性，并采用封闭答案集以实现一致的评估。为了扩展标注规模，我们通过约束提示生成候选答案，辅以自动有效性和一致性检查，再经人工审核以提升合理性和标签一致性。我们报告了该基准数据集的统计信息、合理性基线以及在困难手术条件下的常见评估挑战。代码将在https://github.com/ziyangwang007/Robosurg-VQA上开源。

</details>


### [10] [Flow Mismatching: Unsupervised Anomaly Detection via Velocity Discrepancies in Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23070)
*Shengzhe Chen,Mehrdad Moradi,Kamran Paynabar,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Flow Mismatching，一种基于流匹配的无监督异常检测方法，通过比较生成模型预测速度与几何速度之间的差异来检测异常，无需重建、无测试时优化，在MVTec-AD和VisA上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于重建的异常检测方法存在局限性，且流匹配模型在异常检测中的应用尚未充分探索。本文希望利用流匹配的几何动力学特性，通过模型预测速度与几何速度的失配来定位异常，避免重建范式的固有缺陷。

Method: 训练一个仅在正常图像上的流匹配模型；在从高斯噪声到目标图像的仿射路径上，对比模型预测速度(遵循正常生成动力学)与朝向目标的几何速度；异常区域会导致两者产生局部失配；跨时间步和多路径聚合失配得到像素级热图和图像级分数。

Result: 理论分析揭示了失配可分解为不可约去噪项和Fisher散度项，后者对应正常与测试路径得分函数之间的差异，是驱动异常分离的关键。在MVTec-AD和VisA数据集上，该方法优于SOTA重建方法和近期流匹配方法。

Conclusion: Flow Mismatching通过无重建、无测试时优化的范式实现了卓越的异常检测性能，其理论框架解释了异常分离的机制，路径聚合策略的有效性也得到了验证。

Original Abstract: We propose Flow Mismatching, an unsupervised anomaly detection method that deliberately avoids reconstruction-based paradigms. Instead, we treat flow matching as geometric dynamics and leverage a key insight: anomalies occur at places where the learned normal flow disagrees with the geometric path toward a test image.
  Given a flow matching model trained only on normal images, we probe its learned velocity field along affine paths from Gaussian noise to a target image. Along each path, we compare the model-predicted velocity, which follows normal generative dynamics, with the geometric velocity toward the target, which includes any anomalous content. Anomalies induce strong local disagreement between these velocities. Aggregating the mismatch over different time steps and multiple paths yields pixel-wise heatmaps and image-level scores without test-time optimization, feature memories, or additional calibration.
  Our analysis shows that the population mismatch decomposes into an irreducible denoising term and a Fisher-divergence term between the test-path and normal-path score functions, which identifies the score-gap component that drives anomaly separation and explains the effectiveness of robust path aggregation. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD and VisA demonstrate superior performance compared with SOTA reconstruction-based and recent flow matching-based approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出Flow Mismatching，一种无监督异常检测方法，它刻意避开了基于重建的范式。相反，我们将流匹配视为几何动力学，并利用一个关键洞见：异常发生在学习到的正常流与指向测试图像的几何路径不一致的位置。给定一个仅在正常图像上训练的流匹配模型，我们沿着从高斯噪声到目标图像的仿射路径探测其学习到的速度场。在每条路径上，我们比较模型预测的速度（遵循正常生成动力学）与指向目标图像的几何速度（包含任何异常内容）。异常会导致这些速度之间出现强烈的局部失配。聚合不同时间步和多条路径上的失配，无需测试时优化、特征记忆或额外校准，即可生成像素级热图和图像级分数。我们的分析表明，总体失配可分解为不可约去噪项和测试路径与正常路径得分函数之间的Fisher散度项，后者识别出驱动异常分离的得分差距成分，并解释了鲁棒路径聚合的有效性。在MVTec-AD和VisA上的大量实验表明，与SOTA重建方法和近期流匹配方法相比，该方法性能更优。

</details>


### [11] [Inconsistency-aware Multimodal Schrödinger Bridge for Deepfake Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23113)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IaMSB框架，利用薛定谔桥统一跨模态一致性估计与区间级音视频深度伪造定位，通过非对称步分配和瓶颈交互抑制噪声传播，在严格IoU边界上提升AP@0.95达3%~10%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音视频深度伪造定位方法采用对称融合策略，在面对单侧伪造或异步伪造时，对称融合会传播跨模态噪声，导致高精度定位性能退化。

Method: 提出IaMSB框架，基于薛定谔桥(Schrödinger Bridge)统一一致性估计、跨模态信息选择和桥步调度。先由轻量粗桥提出候选区间并估计跨模态一致性，据此选择跨模态证人信号并异分配桥步；再由精炼桥进行步调融合，输出精调的时间对齐区间。

Result: 在多个基准上，IaMSB稳定了严格IoU边界精度，AP@0.95提升3%~10%，尤其对单侧伪造场景表现优异。

Conclusion: IaMSB通过非对称步分配和瓶颈跨模态交互有效抑制噪声传递、避免不必要的迭代，为音视频深度伪造定位提供了高精度解决方案。

Original Abstract: Audio-visual deepfake localization demands interval-level outputs that serve as temporal evidence. Despite recent progress, symmetric fusion under single-sided or asynchronous forgeries propagates cross-modal noise, degrading high-precision localization. We present IaMSB, an inconsistency-aware multimodal Schrödinger Bridge (SB) that jointly estimates cross-modal consistency and performs interval-level localization. Unlike diffusion models, SB minimizes path-distribution discrepancy and yields consistency scores without explicit noise injection or denoising. With the Schrödinger Bridge (SB), IaMSB unifies consistency estimation, cross-modal information selection, and bridge-step scheduling in one framework. Specifically, a lightweight coarse bridge first proposes candidate intervals and estimates cross-modal consistency; these statistics select cross-modal witness signals and allocate bridge steps asymmetrically across modalities. A refinement bridge then performs step-tuned fusion and outputs refined, time-aligned intervals. IaMSB anticipates single-sided and asynchronous forgeries and, using bottlenecked cross-modal interaction with step allocation, suppresses noise transfer, avoids unnecessary iterations. Across benchmarks, IaMSB stabilizes strict-IoU boundary precision, raising AP@0.95 by 3%~10%, and yields improved high-precision localization, particularly for single-sided forgeries.

中文摘要翻译: 音视频深度伪造定位需要输出作为时间证据的区间级结果。尽管近期研究取得了进展，但在单侧或异步伪造条件下，对称融合会传播跨模态噪声，导致高精度定位性能退化。本文提出IaMSB，一种基于不一致性感知的多模态薛定谔桥(Schrödinger Bridge, SB)框架，可联合估计跨模态一致性并执行区间级定位。与扩散模型不同，SB通过最小化路径分布差异直接输出一致性分数，无需显式注入噪声或去噪。借助SB，IaMSB将一致性估计、跨模态信息选择和桥步调度统一于单一框架中。具体而言，轻量粗桥首先提议候选区间并估计跨模态一致性；这些统计量用于选择跨模态证人信号，并在不同模态间不对称地分配桥步。精炼桥随后执行步调融合，输出精调的时间对齐区间。IaMSB能够预判单侧和异步伪造，并通过瓶颈化的跨模态交互与步分配机制抑制噪声传递、避免不必要的迭代。在多个基准上，IaMSB稳定了严格IoU边界精度，将AP@0.95提升了3%~10%，特别是在单侧伪造场景下实现了更优的高精度定位。

</details>


### [12] [CoReVAD: A Contextual Reasoning Framework for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23116)
*Hyeongmuk Lim,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoReVAD，一种无需训练的视频异常检测框架，基于单个冻结的视觉-语言模型（VLM），直接生成异常分数和时序描述，并通过局部响应清洗、softmax优化、高斯平滑和位置加权来抑制生成噪声、融入全局时序上下文，在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence上取得了与免训练方法可比的性能，同时提供可解释的推理结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VAD方法依赖任务特定训练，域依赖性强且训练成本高；多数方法仅输出标量异常分数，缺乏可解释性；VLM-based方法仍需额外训练或外部LLM，引入额外开销。因此，作者希望设计一种无需额外训练、仅用单个冻结VLM即可实现异常检测与可解释推理的框架。

Method: CoReVAD以单个冻结VLM为核心，直接生成异常分数与时序描述；设计局部响应清洗（LRC）模块，基于局部视觉-文本对齐抑制生成噪声；通过softmax优化、高斯平滑和位置加权引入全局时序上下文与进展信息。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence数据集上，CoReVAD在免训练方法中取得了有竞争力的性能，同时提供了可靠且可解释的异常解释。

Conclusion: CoReVAD通过单个冻结VLM实现了无需额外训练的视频异常检测与可解释推理，在保持性能的同时大幅降低了训练成本和推理开销，为VAD提供了一种高效、可解释的新范式。

Original Abstract: Existing Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) methods typically rely on task-specific training, leading to strong domain dependency and high training costs. Moreover, most existing methods output only scalar anomaly scores, providing limited insight into why specific events are considered abnormal. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled both anomaly detection and human-interpretable reasoning. However, many VLM-based approaches still require additional training steps (e.g., instruction tuning or verbalized learning) or external Large Language Models (LLMs), incurring further training costs and inference overhead. To address these challenges, we propose CoReVAD, a contextual reasoning framework for training-free video anomaly detection that operates with a single frozen VLM. CoReVAD directly generates anomaly scores and temporal descriptions from the VLM. To mitigate noise in generative outputs, we introduce a Local Response Cleaning (LRC) module based on local vision-text alignment. Furthermore, global temporal context and progression are incorporated through softmax-based refinement, Gaussian smoothing, and position weighting. Experiments on UCF-Crime and XD-Violence demonstrate that CoReVAD achieves competitive performance among training-free methods while providing reliable and interpretable explanations. Our official code is available at: https://github.com/Muk-00/CoReVAD

中文摘要翻译: 现有的视频异常检测（VAD）方法通常依赖于特定任务的训练，导致强领域依赖性和高训练成本。此外，大多数现有方法仅输出标量异常分数，对于特定事件为何被视为异常提供的解释十分有限。近期视觉-语言模型（VLM）的进展使得同时实现异常检测和人类可解释的推理成为可能。然而，许多基于VLM的方法仍需额外的训练步骤（如指令微调或口头化学习）或外部大语言模型（LLM），从而引入进一步的训练成本和推理开销。为应对这些挑战，我们提出CoReVAD，一种面向免训练视频异常检测的上下文推理框架，仅需单个冻结的VLM即可运行。CoReVAD直接从VLM生成异常分数和时序描述。为抑制生成输出中的噪声，我们引入基于局部视觉-文本对齐的局部响应清洗（LRC）模块。此外，通过基于softmax的精炼、高斯平滑和位置加权，融入全局时序上下文与进展信息。在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence上的实验表明，CoReVAD在免训练方法中取得了有竞争力的性能，同时提供了可靠且可解释的解释。我们的官方代码已开源：https://github.com/Muk-00/CoReVAD

</details>


### [13] [Exploiting Longitudinal Context in Clinician-Verified Interactive Lesion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23118)
*Yannick Kirchhoff,Maximilian Rokuss,Daniel Philipp Mertens,David Füller,Benjamin Hamm,Andreas Schreyer,Oliver Ritter,Klaus Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"验证追踪"（Verified Tracking）的新范式，通过结合配准提议的提示与基线病灶外观，实现CT序列中肿瘤病灶的半自动化追踪，在自动化与临床安全控制之间取得平衡，并在MICCAI autoPET IV挑战赛中取得第一名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法存在根本性权衡：端到端追踪器自动化程度高但无法纠正静默失败，而解耦的配准-分割流水线允许用户验证但丢弃了病灶先验外观，在模糊病例中精度受限。因此需要一种既允许医生验证又能利用历史病灶信息的方法。

Method: 提出统一框架，结合早期空间提示融合（early spatial prompt fusion）与潜在时序差异加权（latent temporal difference weighting），实现纵向信息感知的分割。采用大规模合成数据预训练以解决数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在MICCAI autoPET IV挑战赛中获得第一名；合成预训练相比从头训练可提升最多4.5个Dice点；在自建PanTrack胰腺癌纵向基准上实现全自动和验证追踪两种设置下均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为临床提供了一种介于全自动与完全人工控制之间的安全中间地带，兼顾自动化效率和临床验证需求。

Original Abstract: Tracking tumor lesions across serial CT scans is essential for oncological response assessment. Existing automated methods face a fundamental trade-off: end-to-end trackers achieve high automation but offer no opportunity to correct silent tracking failures, while decoupled registration-segmentation pipelines permit user verification yet discard the lesion's prior appearance, limiting accuracy in ambiguous cases. In this work, we propose a Verified Tracking paradigm: a clinician verifies a registration-proposed prompt, which the model leverages alongside the baseline lesion appearance to resolve segmentation ambiguities. We present a unified framework combining early spatial prompt fusion with latent temporal difference weighting for longitudinally-informed segmentation. To address data scarcity, we leverage large-scale synthetic pretraining, proving essential for exploiting longitudinal context, improving performance by up to 4.5 Dice points over training from scratch. Our approach secured first place in the MICCAI autoPET IV challenge. We further curate and release PanTrack, a new longitudinal pancreatic cancer benchmark, to assess out-of-distribution generalization. Experiments show that our model outperforms prior work in both fully automatic and the proposed verified tracking setting offering a clinically safe middle ground between automation and control. Code, model and dataset will be released at https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/LongiSeg

中文摘要翻译: 跟踪连续CT扫描中的肿瘤病灶对于肿瘤学疗效评估至关重要。现有的自动化方法面临一个根本性的权衡：端到端追踪器实现了高度自动化，但无法纠正静默追踪失败；而解耦的配准-分割流水线允许用户验证，却丢弃了病灶的先验外观，在模糊病例中限制了精度。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种"验证追踪"范式：临床医生验证配准所提议的提示，模型结合该提示与基线病灶外观来消除分割歧义。我们提出了一个统一框架，将早期空间提示融合与潜在时序差异加权相结合，实现纵向信息感知的分割。为解决数据稀缺问题，我们利用大规模合成预训练，并证明该方法对于利用纵向上下文至关重要，相比从头训练可提升最多4.5个Dice点。我们的方法在MICCAI autoPET IV挑战赛中获得了第一名。我们进一步整理并发布了PanTrack——一个新的纵向胰腺癌基准数据集，用于评估分布外泛化能力。实验表明，我们的模型在全自动和所提出的验证追踪两种设置下均优于先前工作，为临床提供了介于自动化与控制之间的安全中间地带。代码、模型和数据集将发布在 https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/LongiSeg

</details>


### [14] [VisAnalog: A Diagnostic Suite for Visual Concept Transfer on Natural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23141)
*Zhaonan Li,Kyle R. Chickering,Bangzheng Li,Jacob Dineen,Xiao Ye,Zhikun Xu,Shijie Lu,Yuxi Huang,Ming Shen,Bach Nguyen,Jaya Adithya Pavuluri,Mau Son Nguyen,Sanika Chavan,Ngoc Minh Thu Le,Muhao Chen,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisAnalog是一个用于测试视觉类比推理能力的基准，包含617个人工验证的多步视觉变换问题。实验发现，当前最先进的视觉语言模型在此任务上表现远低于人类，主要瓶颈在于从A→B中推断视觉关系的能力不足，且随变换步数增加性能急剧下降。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉概念学习测试仅关注模型能否在单张图像中识别概念，而忽略了模型在变换下保持和迁移概念级属性的能力。作者希望系统性地评估视觉语言模型在视觉类比推理（A:B::C:?）任务上的表现。

Method: 构建VisAnalog基准，包含617个人工验证的A:B::C:?多选问题。每个问题中，B和隐藏目标D由相同的确定性变换序列分别应用于A和C得到。变换包括缩放、象限交换、旋转、翻转和色相旋转，步数从1到4步。模型需根据A、B、C从选项中选出D。此外还设计了程序条件评估方法，将关系推理和变换应用两个子能力分离测试。

Result: 1) 强封闭源和开源VLM的端到端准确率显著低于直接展示D时的oracle准确率；2) 随着变换深度增加，模型性能急剧下降，而人类表现接近天花板；3) 程序条件评估显示，从A→B推断视觉关系是主要瓶颈，在难度更高的多步案例中还会出现额外的变换应用错误。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在视觉类比推理任务上远未达到人类水平，特别是在关系推理方面存在显著短板。VisAnalog为评估和提升模型的视觉概念学习与推理能力提供了一个有价值的基准。

Original Abstract: A useful test of visual concept learning is not just whether a model can recognize a concept in a single image, but whether it can preserve and manipulate concept-level properties under transformation and transfer them to new scenes. We introduce VisAnalog, a controlled suite for this setting on natural images. Each example instantiates $A\!:\!B::C\!:\,?$: images $B$ and a hidden target image $D$ are produced by applying the same deterministic transformation sequence to source images $A$ and $C$. Given $A$, $B$, and $C$, a model must answer a multiple-choice question about $D$. The benchmark contains 617 human-validated questions spanning one- to four-step transformations such as zoom, quadrant swap, rotation, flip, and hue rotation. Across strong proprietary and open-source VLMs, end-to-end accuracy is substantially lower than oracle accuracy when $D$ is directly shown, and degrades sharply as transformation depth increases, while human performance remains near the ceiling. A program-conditioned evaluation further separates failures of relation inference from failures of transformation application, showing that inferring the visual relation from $A \rightarrow B$ is the dominant bottleneck, with additional application errors emerging on harder multi-step cases. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/zli99/VisAnalog.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉概念学习的一个有效测试不仅在于模型能否在单张图像中识别概念，更在于它能否在变换下保持并操作概念级属性，并将其迁移到新场景中。我们提出了VisAnalog，一个针对自然图像中这一场景的受控测试集。每个示例实例化A:B::C:?：图像B和隐藏目标图像D通过将相同的确定性变换序列分别应用于源图像A和C生成。给定A、B和C，模型必须回答关于D的多选题。该基准包含617个人工验证的问题，涵盖1到4步的变换，如缩放、象限交换、旋转、翻转和色相旋转。在强封闭源和开源视觉语言模型上，端到端准确率显著低于直接展示D时的oracle准确率，并且随着变换深度的增加而急剧下降，而人类表现始终接近天花板。程序条件评估进一步分离了关系推理失败和变换应用失败，表明从A→B推断视觉关系是主要瓶颈，在更难的多步案例中还会出现额外的应用错误。该数据集已公开在https://huggingface.co/datasets/zli99/VisAnalog。

</details>


### [15] [SLIP-RS: Structured-Attribute Language-Image Pre-Training for Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23144)
*Chenxu Wang,Yuxuan Li,Yunheng Li,Xiang Li,Jingyuan Xia,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLIP-RS提出了一种结构化属性解耦范式，通过将开放类别空间映射到有限属性空间，结合结构化属性对比学习和保形属性可靠性引擎，解决了遥感目标检测中细粒度表示学习依赖穷举标注的问题，在大规模数据集上实现了先进的细粒度检测和跨域泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感目标检测的语言-图像预训练方法受限于单一标签学习（Monolithic Label Learning），需要通过黑盒数据穷举枚举开放类别来获取细粒度表示，这种依赖性与遥感领域固有的数据稀缺性不兼容。

Method: (1) 结构化属性对比学习（Structured-Attribute Contrastive Learning）：通过组合属性增强来强制学习解耦的内在视觉逻辑；(2) 保形属性可靠性引擎（Conformal Attribute Reliability Engine）：利用保形预测理论从噪声源中严格提取高保真监督信号，构建了包含超过1500万属性标注的最大数据集RS-Attribute-15M。

Result: SLIP-RS在细粒度检测和跨域泛化任务上取得了前所未有的性能，验证了结构化属性作为遥感基础模型的关键要素。

Conclusion: 结构化属性是遥感视觉基础模型的重要基石，所提出的属性解耦范式能够有效突破数据稀缺瓶颈，实现开放场景下的细粒度判别能力。

Original Abstract: Existing language-image pre-training for remote sensing object detection is constrained by Monolithic Label Learning, which relies on exhaustively enumerating open-set categories via black-box data to acquire fine-grained representations, creating a dependency incompatible with the domain's inherent data scarcity. To transcend this bottleneck, we propose SLIP-RS, establishing a Structured-Attribute Decoupling Paradigm that maps the open-ended category space into a finite, physically meaningful attribute space, unlocking fine-grained discriminability via explicit structural logic. This paradigm is realized via two technical pillars: (1) Structured-Attribute Contrastive Learning, which enforces the learning of decoupled intrinsic visual logic via combinatorial attribute augmentation; and (2) Conformal Attribute Reliability Engine, which leverages conformal prediction theory to rigorously distill high-fidelity supervision from noisy sources, yielding RS-Attribute-15M, the largest dataset with over 15 million attribute annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SLIP-RS establishes unprecedented performance in fine-grained detection and cross-domain generalization, validating structured attributes as a vital foundation for remote sensing. Code: https://github.com/facias914/SLIP-RS.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的遥感目标检测语言-图像预训练方法受限于单一标签学习范式，该范式需要通过黑盒数据穷举枚举开放类别来获取细粒度表示，这种依赖性与此领域固有的数据稀缺性不兼容。为了突破这一瓶颈，我们提出SLIP-RS，建立了一种结构化属性解耦范式，将开放的类别空间映射到有限的、具有物理意义的属性空间，通过显式的结构逻辑解锁细粒度判别能力。该范式通过两个技术支柱实现：（1）结构化属性对比学习，通过组合属性增强强制学习解耦的内在视觉逻辑；（2）保形属性可靠性引擎，利用保形预测理论从噪声源中严格提取高保真监督信号，构建了RS-Attribute-15M——迄今为止最大的、包含超过1500万属性标注的数据集。大量实验表明，SLIP-RS在细粒度检测和跨域泛化中建立了前所未有的性能，验证了结构化属性作为遥感基础的重要基石。代码：https://github.com/facias914/SLIP-RS。

</details>


### [16] [LQ-rPPG: A Label-Quantized Coarse-to-Fine Learning Framework for Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23174)
*Jun Seong Lee,Samyeul Noh,Changki Sung,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LQ-rPPG框架，通过标签量化和粗到细学习解决rPPG训练标签噪声问题，在降低88%参数和29%计算量的同时提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于深度学习的rPPG方法忽视了训练标签质量对模型学习的影响。接触式PPG信号作为训练标签，常因运动伪迹、传感器接触不一致和形态畸变而包含噪声和变异，导致模型过拟合到标签噪声，泛化性能下降。

Method: 提出LQ-rPPG框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 标签量化模块，将连续PPG信号转换为多比特量化伪标签，降低噪声和变异；(2) 粗到细的rPPG估计模型，在多比特伪标签的分层监督下逐步细化rPPG信号，避免过拟合到标签特定变异，学习结构化和一致的表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的内数据集和跨数据集评估中均取得强性能，同时参数量减少88%，乘累加操作减少29%，吞吐量提升191%。

Conclusion: LQ-rPPG通过标签量化和粗到细学习有效缓解了标签噪声问题，实现了鲁棒且泛化性强的rPPG估计，并且在模型效率上有显著提升。

Original Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact measurement of physiological signals from facial videos, offering strong potential for remote healthcare and daily health monitoring. Driven by this potential, various deep learning-based rPPG methods have been proposed to improve rPPG estimation. However, previous deep learning-based rPPG methods have paid little attention to the quality of training labels and their impact on model learning. Contact-based PPG signals used as training labels often contain noise and variability caused by motion artifacts, inconsistent sensor contact, and morphological distortions. Such label inconsistency can lead models to overfit to the label noise and variability and consequently degrade generalization performance. To address this issue, we propose LQ-rPPG, a label-quantized coarse-to-fine learning framework for robust rPPG estimation. LQ-rPPG consists of a label quantization module and a coarse-to-fine rPPG estimation model. The label quantization module transforms continuous PPG signals into multi-bit quantized pseudo labels with reduced noise and variability. The coarse-to-fine estimation model progressively refines rPPG signals under hierarchical supervision guided by the multi-bit pseudo labels. This design alleviates overfitting to label-specific variations and enables the model to learn structured and consistent representations. As a result, LQ-rPPG achieves robust and generalizable rPPG estimation even under challenging conditions. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that LQ-rPPG achieves strong performance in both intra- and cross-dataset evaluations, while reducing parameters and multiply-accumulate operations by 88% and 29%, respectively, and increasing throughput by 191%. The code is available at https://github.com/Anonymous-repo-code/LQ-rPPG.

中文摘要翻译: 远程光电容积描记术(rPPG)能够从面部视频中非接触式测量生理信号，在远程医疗和日常健康监测方面具有巨大潜力。受此潜力驱动，研究人员提出了多种基于深度学习的rPPG方法来改进rPPG估计。然而，以往的深度学习方法很少关注训练标签的质量及其对模型学习的影响。用作训练标签的接触式PPG信号常因运动伪迹、传感器接触不一致和形态畸变而包含噪声和变异。这种标签不一致性会导致模型过拟合到标签噪声和变异，从而降低泛化性能。为解决该问题，我们提出了LQ-rPPG，一种基于标签量化的粗到细学习框架，用于鲁棒的rPPG估计。LQ-rPPG包含标签量化模块和粗到细的rPPG估计模型。标签量化模块将连续PPG信号转换为降低噪声和变异的多比特量化伪标签。粗到细估计模型在多比特伪标签的分层监督下逐步细化rPPG信号。该设计缓解了过拟合到标签特定变异的问题，使模型能够学习结构化和一致的表示。最终，LQ-rPPG即使在挑战性条件下也能实现鲁棒且可泛化的rPPG估计。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，LQ-rPPG在内数据集和跨数据集评估中均取得强性能，同时参数量和乘累加操作分别减少88%和29%，吞吐量提升191%。代码已在https://github.com/Anonymous-repo-code/LQ-rPPG公开。

</details>


### [17] [DRIVESPATIAL: A Benchmark for Spatiotemporal Intelligence in VLMs for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23176)
*Hao Vo,Khoa Vo,Phu Loc Nguyen,Sieu Tran,Duc Minh Nguyen,Ngo Xuan Cuong,Gladys Gawugah,Sreevenkata Anjani Tishita Godavarthi,Chase Rainwater,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Anh Nguyen,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DriveSpatial基准，包含15.6K人工验证的QA对，用于评估自动驾驶场景中VLM的时空智能。实验表明最强模型与人类差距达28.4分，认知场景构建是关键瓶颈，语言提示不足而显式BEV定位可提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶视觉语言基准大多局限于单视角、静态、自中心或单源问答，无法判断当前VLM是否真正具备对动态驾驶场景进行构建和推理的能力。因此需要一个新的基准来系统评估VLM在时空驾驶智能方面的真实能力。

Method: 构建了DriveSpatial基准，基于动态多关系场景图（编码对象状态、空间关系、交互、相机可见性及时间对应关系）生成15.6K人工验证的QA对，覆盖五个大规模AD数据集的20个任务，评估认知场景构建、多视角关系理解、时间推理和泛化四种能力。

Result: 评估15个代表性VLM，最强模型与人类差距达28.4分；认知场景构建是主要瓶颈；仅用语言提示效果不足，显式BEV定位可一致提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前VLM缺乏可靠的场景构建能力，难以实现可靠的时空驾驶智能。DriveSpatial及其构建流程将开源以支持未来研究。

Original Abstract: Spatiotemporal intelligence in autonomous driving (AD) requires an agent to integrate multi-view observations into a coherent scene representation, maintain object continuity across viewpoints and time, and reason about spatial relations, interactions, and future dynamics. However, existing AD vision-language benchmarks largely focus on single-view, static, ego-centric, or single-source question answering, leaving it unclear whether current Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can truly construct and reason over dynamic driving scenes. We introduce DriveSpatial, a benchmark of 15.6K human-verified QA pairs across 20 tasks from five large-scale AD datasets. DriveSpatial evaluates four abilities: Cognitive Scene Construction, Multi-view Relational Understanding, Temporal Reasoning, and Generalization. Unlike prior benchmarks, DriveSpatial is generated from a dynamic multi-relational scene graph that encodes object states, spatial relations, interactions, camera visibility, and temporal correspondences, enabling QA pairs that enforce genuine cross-view and spatiotemporal reasoning. Evaluating 15 representative VLMs reveals a substantial human-model gap: the strongest model trails humans by 28.4 points, with Cognitive Scene Construction emerging as the key bottleneck. Further diagnostics show that language-only prompting is insufficient, while explicit BEV grounding consistently improves performance. These results suggest that current VLMs lack the scene-construction ability needed for reliable spatiotemporal driving intelligence. DriveSpatial and its construction pipeline will be released to support future research.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶中的时空智能要求智能体将多视角观测整合为连贯的场景表示，跨视角和时间维持对象连续性，并推理空间关系、交互和未来动态。然而，现有的自动驾驶视觉语言基准主要集中于单视角、静态、自中心或单源问答，难以判断当前的视觉语言模型（VLM）是否能够真正构建和推理动态驾驶场景。我们提出了DriveSpatial基准，包含来自五个大规模AD数据集的20个任务中的15.6K人工验证的QA对。DriveSpatial评估四种能力：认知场景构建、多视角关系理解、时间推理和泛化。与先前基准不同，DriveSpatial基于动态多关系场景图生成，该图编码对象状态、空间关系、交互、相机可见性和时间对应关系，从而产生强制进行真正的跨视角和时空推理的QA对。对15个代表性VLM的评估揭示了显著的人机差距：最强模型落后人类28.4分，其中认知场景构建成为关键瓶颈。进一步的诊断表明，仅依赖语言提示不够，而显式的BEV（鸟瞰图）定位一致地提升了性能。这些结果表明，当前VLM缺乏实现可靠时空驾驶智能所需的场景构建能力。DriveSpatial及其构建流程将开源以支持未来研究。

</details>


### [18] [IntentionNav: A Benchmark for Intent-Driven Object Navigation from Implicit Human Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23187)
*Lin Qian,Shijie Li,Sihao Lin,Xuan Zhang,Bangya Liu,Yanran Li,Hujun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了意图驱动目标导航任务IntentionNav，智能体需从隐式人类指令（如“我需要加热食物”）推断目标物体并完成导航。基准包含500条意图、176个场景和64个目标类别，采用四种指令风格和四种意图模式进行控制实验。评估显示VLM模型在间接意图理解、视觉验证和终端定位方面存在显著瓶颈，总体终止成功率仅24.9%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有目标导航基准通常直接告知智能体目标类别（如微波炉），但真实人机交互中指令往往是隐式的（如“我需要加热食物”），智能体需推断隐含目标、找到场景中的实例并判断任务是否完成。本文旨在研究这一更具挑战性的意图驱动目标导航问题。

Method: 构建IntentionNav诊断基准：包含500条自然语言意图、176个Isaac Sim场景和64个目标类别；每条意图以四种受控指令风格重写，并标注四种意图模式（事件脚本、物理状态、功能用途等）；采用固定主动导航智能体评估三种VLM模型在目标推断、语言鲁棒性、邻域可达性和终止成功率等方面的性能。

Result: 模型在48.3%的回合中识别出预期目标，68.7%进入目标2米邻域，但仅24.9%成功终止，1米接地成功率仅5.5%。事件脚本意图成功率最高（28.7%），物理状态（19.2%）和功能用途意图（18.5%）较低，表明间接人类意图仍是目标选择、视觉验证和终端定位的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 间接人类意图理解在主动具身搜索中仍是关键瓶颈，当前VLM模型在目标推断、视觉验证和精确终端定位方面表现不足，尤其在物理状态和功能用途等隐含意图模式下差距更大。

Original Abstract: Existing object navigation benchmarks usually tell an embodied agent which object category to find, such as microwave or chair. Human-facing embodied AI is often asked something less direct: "I need something to warm this food" or "the room feels stuffy." The agent must infer the object that can satisfy the need, find a scene-grounded instance, and decide whether the goal has been reached. We study this setting as intent-driven object navigation and introduce IntentionNav, a diagnostic benchmark for active object search from implicit human instructions. Each episode provides a free-text intent, RGB-D observations, and pose, but withholds the target object name. IntentionNav contains 500 intents over 176 Isaac Sim scenes and 64 target categories. Each intent is rewritten in four controlled instruction styles and annotated with one of four intent modes, separating surface phrasing from semantic cue type under matched geometry. This paired design supports analysis of target inference, language robustness, neighborhood reachability, and terminal success rather than only aggregate success. We evaluated three VLMs using a fixed active-navigation agent. Models identify the intended target in 48.3 percent of episodes and enter its 2 m neighborhood in 68.7 percent, but terminate successfully in only 24.9 percent and achieve grounded 1 m success in 5.5 percent. Success is highest for event-script intents (28.7 percent) and lower for physical-state and affordance intents (19.2 percent and 18.5 percent), showing that indirect human intent remains a bottleneck for target selection, visual verification, and terminal localization in active embodied search.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的物体导航基准通常告诉具身智能体需要寻找哪一类物体，例如微波炉或椅子。面向人类的具身AI常常面临不那么直接的指令："我需要加热食物"或"房间感觉很闷"。智能体必须推断出能够满足需求的物体，找到场景中具体的实例，并判断目标是否已达成。本文将这一设定研究为意图驱动目标导航，并引入IntentionNav，一个面向隐式人类指令的主动物体搜索诊断基准。每个回合提供自由文本形式的意图、RGB-D观测和位姿信息，但不给出目标物体名称。IntentionNav包含覆盖176个Isaac Sim场景和64个目标类别的500条意图。每条意图以四种受控指令风格重写，并标注四种意图模式之一，在匹配几何结构的前提下将表面措辞与语义线索类型分离。这种配对设计支持对目标推断、语言鲁棒性、邻域可达性和终止成功率（而非仅聚合成功率）进行分析。我们使用一个固定主动导航智能体评估了三种视觉语言模型。模型在48.3%的回合中识别出预期目标，在68.7%的回合中进入目标2米邻域，但仅24.9%成功终止，接地1米成功率仅5.5%。事件脚本意图的成功率最高（28.7%），物理状态和功能用途意图的成功率较低（分别为19.2%和18.5%），这表明间接人类意图在主动具身搜索中仍是目标选择、视觉验证和终端定位的瓶颈。

</details>


### [19] [Occlusion-Aware Physics-Semantic Keyframe Selection for Robust Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23192)
*Lin Liu,Zhihan Xiao,Haohang Xu,Rong Cong,Zhibo Zhang,Xiaopeng Zhang,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种遮挡感知的物理-语义关键帧选择框架，通过自动选择最优锚定帧并双向传播生成时空掩码，解决了扩散模型视频编辑在遮挡、视角变化和快速运动下定位不准、时序闪烁和不一致编辑的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的视频编辑方法在遮挡、视角变化和快速运动场景下表现不佳，根本原因在于缺乏可靠的视觉锚点。作者将遮挡鲁棒视频编辑的瓶颈归结为缺乏可靠的视觉锚点，而非传统的显式重建问题。

Method: 提出一个遮挡感知的物理-语义关键帧选择框架，从三个互补角度评估候选帧：结构完整性（避免截断观测）、循环一致性跟踪稳定性（衡量物理可靠性）、视觉-语言属性可见性（确保语义清晰）。选中的关键帧通过双向跟踪生成密集时空掩码，作为扩散视频编辑骨干的辅助监督信号。

Result: 在具有挑战性的视频编辑基准上进行了大量实验，证明了该方法的有效性和高质量性能。

Conclusion: 通过将遮挡处理从显式重建转化为可靠的锚点选择，该框架无需手动标注即可实现精确且时序一致的视频编辑。

Original Abstract: Video editing has recently achieved remarkable progress with diffusion-based generative models, enabling diverse object-level manipulations from natural language instructions. However, existing methods often struggle under occlusion, viewpoint changes, and fast object motion, where unreliable visual observations lead to inaccurate localization, temporal flickering, and inconsistent edits. In this work, we identify the absence of reliable visual anchors as a fundamental bottleneck in occlusion-robust video editing. To address this issue, we propose an occlusion-aware physics-semantic keyframe selection framework that automatically identifies an optimal anchor frame for downstream editing. Specifically, our method evaluates candidate frames from three complementary perspectives: structural completeness for avoiding truncated observations, cycle-consistent tracking stability for measuring physical reliability, and vision-language-based attribute visibility for ensuring semantic clarity. The selected keyframe is then propagated through bidirectional tracking to generate dense spatiotemporal masks, which are used as auxiliary supervision for a diffusion-based video editing backbone. By transforming occlusion handling from explicit reconstruction into reliable anchor selection, our framework enables precise and temporally consistent editing without requiring manual annotations. Extensive experiments on challenging video editing benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and high-quality performance of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散生成模型的视频编辑近年来取得了显著进展，能够根据自然语言指令实现多样化的物体级操作。然而，现有方法在遮挡、视角变化和快速运动场景下常常表现不佳，不可靠的视觉观测会导致定位不准、时序闪烁以及不一致的编辑。在这项工作中，我们指出现有方法缺乏可靠的视觉锚点是实现遮挡鲁棒视频编辑的根本瓶颈。为解决该问题，我们提出了一种遮挡感知的物理-语义关键帧选择框架，能够自动识别最优锚定帧用于后续编辑。具体而言，我们的方法从三个互补角度评估候选帧：结构完整性——避免截断观测；循环一致性跟踪稳定性——衡量物理可靠性；以及基于视觉-语言模型的属性可见性——确保语义清晰。选中的关键帧通过双向跟踪传播生成密集的时空掩码，作为扩散视频编辑骨干的辅助监督信号。通过将遮挡处理从显式重建转化为可靠的锚点选择，我们的框架无需手动标注即可实现精确且时序一致的编辑。在具有挑战性的视频编辑基准上进行的广泛实验证明了我们方法的有效性和高质量性能。

</details>


### [20] [CaST-Bench: Benchmarking Causal Chain-Grounded Spatio-Temporal Reasoning for Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23216)
*Mingfang Zhang,Jingjing Pan,Ashutosh Kumar,Rajat Saini,Mustafa Erdogan,Hsuan-Kung Yang,Caixin Kang,Yifei Huang,Yoichi Sato,Quan Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CaST-Bench，一个用于评估视频视觉语言模型因果链时空推理能力的基准，包含2066个问题和1015个视频，并设计了新的评估指标。实验表明现有VLM在因果推理上表现不佳，主要由于构建精确因果证据链的能力有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频推理基准缺乏细粒度、可定位的因果证据，难以严格评估VLM在视频中的因果推理能力，尤其是需要识别和定位多个时空证据链的复杂因果问题。

Method: 通过人机协作流程构建高质量数据集，包含2066个问题、1015个视频，因果链由时间片段和边界框轨迹标注；设计综合评估套件，包含新指标评估答案正确性和视觉证据接地推理能力。

Result: 实验表明当前VLM在因果问题上表现不佳，主要原因是它们构建精确且可接地的因果证据链的能力有限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在因果链时空推理上存在显著不足，提高模型构建精确因果证据链的能力是未来改进的重要方向。

Original Abstract: Cause-and-effect reasoning in video is a significant challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), as it requires going beyond surface-level perception to a deeper understanding of causal mechanisms. However, existing benchmarks rarely provide the fine-grained, grounded evidence needed to rigorously evaluate this capability. To address this gap, we introduce CaST-Bench, a benchmark for Causal Chain-Grounded Spatio-Temporal Video Reasoning. CaST-Bench presents complex causal questions that require models to identify and localize a chain of multiple spatio-temporal evidences. Through a human-AI collaborative pipeline, we construct a high-quality dataset of 2,066 questions over 1,015 videos, with causal chains annotated by temporal segments and bounding-box tracks. Furthermore, we design a comprehensive evaluation suite with novel metrics that assess not only answer correctness but also the capability for visual evidence grounded reasoning. This grounding is crucial for improving accuracy by mitigating spurious correlations and for enhancing user trust by making models more transparent. Our experiments show that current VLMs struggle with causal questions, largely due to their limited ability to construct precise and grounded causal chains. This highlights an important direction for improving future VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 视频中的因果推理对视觉语言模型（VLM）来说是一项重大挑战，因为它要求模型超越表面感知，深入理解因果机制。然而，现有基准很少提供严格评估这一能力所需的细粒度、可接地的证据。为填补这一空白，我们提出了CaST-Bench——一个用于因果链接地时空视频推理的基准。CaST-Bench提出复杂的因果问题，要求模型识别并定位多个时空证据构成的因果链。通过人机协作流程，我们构建了一个高质量数据集，包含1,015个视频上的2,066个问题，因果链由时间片段和边界框轨迹进行标注。此外，我们设计了一套综合评估套件，包含新颖的指标，不仅评估答案的正确性，还评估视觉证据接地推理的能力。这种接地对于通过减轻虚假相关性来提高准确率以及通过增强模型透明度来提升用户信任至关重要。我们的实验表明，当前VLM在因果问题上表现不佳，主要原因是它们构建精确且接地的因果链的能力有限。这为改进未来VLM指明了一个重要方向。

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Normal References: Discriminative Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23231)
*Huan Wang,Jun Shen,Jun Yan,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IDEAL框架，通过内在偏差学习解决判别式少样本异常检测中异常参考利用不足和过拟合的问题，将学习分解为正常变化擦除器和内在偏差编码器，在多个真实数据集上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少样本异常检测方法仅依赖正常参考进行正态性匹配，忽略了异常参考中的判别性线索；而直接同时拟合正常和异常参考又会导致对已见异常的过拟合，无法泛化到未见异常。

Method: IDEAL包含两个核心组件：1) 正常变化擦除器，抑制干扰性的正常变化，突出异常相关偏差表示；2) 内在偏差编码器，将去噪后的偏差表示分解为最具判别性的正交内在偏差向量。推理时通过查询样本在内在偏差向量上的投影来评分。

Result: 在8个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，IDEAL能有效泛化到未见异常，且持续优于现有最先进的少样本异常检测方法。

Conclusion: IDEAL通过内在偏差学习框架有效利用正常和异常两种参考类型，学习可泛化的异常模式，在判别式少样本异常检测任务中取得了显著优势。

Original Abstract: This paper considers a practical few-shot anomaly detection (FSAD) setting, termed discriminative FSAD, where a limited number of both normal and anomalous examples are available as references during inference. Existing FSAD methods rely on normal-only references through normality matching, ignoring the discriminative clues in anomalous references, while directly fitting both references can overfit to the seen anomalies. We introduce IDEAL, an intrinsic deviation learning framework that leverages both reference types to learn intrinsic deviation patterns characterizing generalizable abnormality as deviations from normality. IDEAL decomposes the learning process into two novel components: 1) a Normal Variation Eraser to suppress nuisance normal variations that may lead to noisy deviations from normality, thereby highlighting anomaly-relevant deviation representations; 2) an Intrinsic Deviation Encoder to decompose these denoised deviation representations into intrinsic deviation vectors capturing the most discriminative orthogonal deviation directions. At inference, IDEAL scores query-to-normal deviations preserved after projection onto the learned intrinsic deviation vectors, enabling generalization for both seen and unseen anomalies. Extensive experiments on eight real-world datasets show that IDEAL generalizes effectively to unseen anomalies and consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art FSAD methods. Code and data will be available at \href{https://github.com/mala-lab/IDEAL}{https://github.com/mala-lab/IDEAL}.

中文摘要翻译: 本文考虑一种实际的少样本异常检测（FSAD）设置，称为判别式FSAD，其中推理时可获得有限数量的正常和异常样本作为参考。现有的FSAD方法仅依赖正常参考进行正态性匹配，忽略了异常参考中的判别性线索，而直接同时拟合两种参考又会导致对已见异常的过拟合。我们提出IDEAL，一种内在偏差学习框架，利用两种参考类型来学习表征可泛化异常（即偏离正常模式）的内在偏差模式。IDEAL将学习过程分解为两个新颖组件：1) 正常变化擦除器，用于抑制可能导致噪声化偏差的干扰性正常变化，从而突出与异常相关的偏差表示；2) 内在偏差编码器，将这些去噪后的偏差表示分解为捕获最具判别性正交偏差方向的内在偏差向量。推理时，IDEAL对查询样本到投影到所学内在偏差向量上的正常性偏差进行评分，从而实现对已见和未见异常的泛化。在八个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，IDEAL能有效泛化到未见异常，并持续优于现有最先进的少样本异常检测方法。代码和数据将在https://github.com/mala-lab/IDEAL上提供。

</details>


### [22] [StereoGenBench: A Synthetic Multi-Camera Benchmark for Stereo Generation under Controlled Baseline Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23237)
*Yangzhi Cui,Feng Qiao,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了StereoGenBench，一个基于Unreal Engine的合成基准数据集，用于立体图像/视频生成、立体几何估计和条件控制视图合成。该数据集提供了多基线、校准的右视图真值，包含内参、密集度量深度和逐帧位姿，弥补了现有立体资源在这些变量上的缺失。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有立体资源无法同时提供场景配对、校准的多基线右视图真值、内参、密集度量深度和逐帧位姿，导致立体生成评估中基线灵敏度、目标相机一致性等关键指标无法被精确测量。

Method: 使用Unreal Engine合成场景，通过刚性六相机横向阵列渲染每个场景，生成最多15个校准视图对；相邻基线从瞳孔间距离到宽基线范围采样；焦距独立采样；每个视图提供RGB、度量深度、内参、每对基线和逐帧位姿。

Result: 数据集包含窄基线、宽基线两个评估族和一个全配对训练族；发布了数据集、评估代码、参考结果、Croissant元数据和扩展用的生成代码/配置。

Conclusion: StereoGenBench为立体生成任务提供了首个场景配对、多基线校准、完整标注（内参、深度、位姿）的合成基准，使基线敏感性分析和目标相机一致性测量成为可能。

Original Abstract: Stereo image and video generation, stereo geometry estimation, and condition-controlled view synthesis require paired data in which the variables that determine binocular geometry -- camera baseline, intrinsics, scene depth, and camera motion -- are known and controllable. Existing stereo resources provide subsets of these variables, but resources commonly used for stereo generation evaluation do not, to our knowledge, provide scene-paired, calibrated multi-baseline right-view ground truth with jointly recorded intrinsics, dense metric depth, and per-frame poses in a single controlled source. We introduce StereoGenBench, a synthetic Unreal Engine benchmark designed to make baseline-regime sensitivity and target-camera consistency measurable under matched scene content. Each scene is rendered with a rigid six-camera lateral array, yielding up to 15 calibrated view pairs; adjacent baselines are sampled from inter-pupillary to wide-baseline regimes; focal length is sampled independently; and every view is released with RGB, metric depth, intrinsics, per-pair baselines, and per-frame poses. The splits include two evaluation families for narrow and wide baseline regimes and a train-only family for broader all-pairs coverage. We release the dataset, evaluation code, reference results, Croissant metadata, and generation code/configuration for extension with compatible assets. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/stereo-dataset/stereo-dataset

中文摘要翻译: 立体图像与视频生成、立体几何估计以及条件控制视图合成任务需要配对数据，其中决定双目几何的变量——相机基线、内参、场景深度和相机运动——是已知且可控的。现有的立体资源提供了这些变量的子集，但据我们所知，常用于立体生成评估的资源并未在单一受控源中提供场景配对的、校准的多基线右视图真值，并同时包含内参、密集度量深度和逐帧位姿。我们提出了StereoGenBench，这是一个基于Unreal Engine的合成基准，旨在使基线区间敏感性和目标相机一致性能够在匹配的场景内容下被可测量地评估。每个场景使用刚性六相机横向阵列渲染，生成最多15个校准视图对；相邻基线从瞳孔间距离到宽基线区间采样；焦距独立采样；每个视图均提供RGB、度量深度、内参、每对基线和逐帧位姿。数据集划分包括窄基线和宽基线两个评估族，以及一个覆盖更广泛全配对范围的训练族。我们发布了数据集、评估代码、参考结果、Croissant元数据，以及用于扩展兼容资产的生成代码/配置。数据集可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/stereo-dataset/stereo-dataset获取。

</details>


### [23] [SimInsert: Seamless Video Object Insertion via Regional Sparse Attention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23245)
*Xinyu Chen,Yuyi Qian,Jiang Lin,Shenyi Wang,Gao Wang,Zhiqiu Zhang,Jizhi Zhang,Mingjie Wang,Qiang Tang,Qian Wang,Song Wu,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimInsert是一种无需训练的视频对象插入方法，通过解耦为单帧编辑和语义运动描述，利用图像到视频扩散模型实现背景不变、文本驱动的时空一致插入，在PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频对象插入方法依赖显式运动工程或资源密集的重新训练，灵活性和泛化能力受限。

Method: 将任务解耦为直观的单帧编辑和语义运动描述，利用图像到视频扩散模型的生成先验进行时间传播，采用非侵入式引导机制保持结构一致性、实现边界无缝融合并抵消去噪过程中的保真度漂移。

Result: PSNR提升18.8%，SSIM提升20.1%，LPIPS降低44.1%，全面超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SimInsert提供了一种流线型、无需训练的高保真视频编辑方案，有效解决了视频对象插入中时空一致性与交互真实性的挑战。

Original Abstract: Video object insertion requires ensuring spatio-temporal coherence and interactive realism, extending far beyond simple content placement. However, current approaches are often hindered by a reliance on explicit motion engineering or resource-intensive retraining, restricting their flexibility and generalization. To bridge this gap, we present \textit{SimInsert}, a training-free paradigm that efficiently decouples the task into intuitive single-frame editing and semantic motion description. By harnessing the robust generative priors of image-to-video diffusion models, SimInsert propagates edits temporally, strictly preserving background invariance while enabling plausible, text-driven interactions between the inserted object and the dynamic environment. Our approach hinges on non-invasive guidance mechanisms that enforce structural consistency, facilitate seamless boundary fusion, and counteract the fidelity drift that typically accumulates during the denoising trajectory. Extensive quantitative experiments validate our efficacy: SimInsert surpasses state-of-the-art methods with an 18.8\% gain in PSNR, 20.1\% in SSIM, and a 44.1\% decrease in LPIPS, offering a streamlined solution for high-fidelity video editing.

中文摘要翻译: 视频对象插入需要确保时空一致性和交互真实性，远不止简单的内容放置。然而，当前方法通常受限于对显式运动工程或资源密集型重新训练的依赖，限制了其灵活性和泛化能力。为弥补这一差距，我们提出\textit{SimInsert}——一种无需训练的新范式，该范式将任务高效解耦为直观的单帧编辑和语义运动描述。通过利用图像到视频扩散模型的强大生成先验，SimInsert在时间上传播编辑结果，严格保持背景不变性，同时实现插入对象与动态环境之间合理的、文本驱动的交互。我们的方法依赖于非侵入式引导机制，该机制强化结构一致性、促进边界无缝融合，并抵消在去噪轨迹中通常累积的保真度漂移。广泛的定量实验验证了我们的有效性：SimInsert在PSNR上超越现有最先进方法18.8%，在SSIM上超越20.1%，在LPIPS上降低44.1%，为高保真视频编辑提供了一种流线型解决方案。

</details>


### [24] [CARE: Class-Adaptive Expert Consensus for Reliable Learning with Long-Tailed Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23254)
*Mengke Li,Haiquan Ling,Lihao Chen,Yang Lu,Yiqun Zhang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对长尾分布和噪声标注的复合挑战，提出CARE框架，利用视觉语言模型的三种互补监督源，通过类自适应专家共识机制，对尾部类施加更严格的共识约束、对头部类施加更宽松的约束，从而有效纠正噪声标签并重校准类分布，在合成和真实基准上均取得最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法未能解决标签噪声在不同类别上的非均匀影响，导致对尾部类的纠正不足和对头部类的过度正则化。

Method: CARE框架利用视觉语言模型的三种监督源（观测噪声标签、VLM文本嵌入、视觉特征），引入类自适应专家共识机制，根据类别频率对尾部类施加更严格的共识、对头部类允许更宽松的共识，通过聚合高置信度预测来过滤不可靠信号并重校准类分布。

Result: 在合成和真实长尾基准上，CARE一致优于现有方法，性能提升最高达3.0%。

Conclusion: 类自适应的专家共识机制能有效应对长尾分布下的噪声标注问题，为参数高效的鲁棒学习提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Learning from real-world data is frequently hindered by the compound challenge of long-tailed class distributions and noisy annotations. Existing methods partially address these issues but typically ignore the non-uniform impact of label noise across classes, resulting in ineffective correction for tail classes and over-regularization for head classes. To address this issue, we propose Class-Adaptive Rectification with Experts (CARE), a parameter-efficient framework that leverages three complementary supervision sources from vision-language models (VLM): observed noisy labels, VLM text embeddings, and visual features. CARE introduces a class-adaptive expert consensus mechanism that enforces stricter agreement for tail classes and more permissive agreement for head classes based on class frequency. By aggregating high-confidence predictions across these sources, CARE filters unreliable signals and recalibrates class distributions, yielding more reliable rectification under long-tailed distributions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that CARE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving up to 3.0\% performance gains. The source code is available at https://github.com/qwq123-study/CARE.

中文摘要翻译: 从真实世界数据中学习常面临长尾类分布和噪声标注的双重挑战。现有方法部分解决了这些问题，但通常忽略了标签噪声在各类别上的非均匀影响，导致对尾部类的纠正不足、对头部类的过度正则化。为解决该问题，我们提出CARE（类自适应专家纠正）框架——一个参数高效的框架，利用来自视觉语言模型（VLM）的三种互补监督源：观测到的噪声标签、VLM文本嵌入和视觉特征。CARE引入了一种类自适应专家共识机制，根据类频率对尾部类施加更严格的共识约束，对头部类允许更宽松的共识。通过聚合这些监督源中的高置信度预测，CARE过滤不可靠信号并重校准类分布，从而在长尾分布下实现更可靠的纠正。在合成和真实基准上的大量实验表明，CARE一致优于最先进方法，性能提升最高达3.0%。源代码已公开于https://github.com/qwq123-study/CARE。

</details>


### [25] [Coloring the Noise: Adversarial Sobolev Alignment for Faithful Image Super Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23264)
*Hongbo Wang,Huaibo Huang,Pin Wang,Jinhua Hao,Chao Zhou,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASASR框架，通过将生成流重构成Sobolev诱导的黎曼几何，利用Riesz表示定理驱动的参数化对抗样本，在图像超分辨率中实现了光谱一致性和结构保真度的提升，有效减少了生成先验带来的幻觉伪影。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 图像超分辨率中生成先验常在忠实恢复上妥协，根本原因在于各向同性目标与自然图像流形之间存在光谱错位；现有直接偏好优化依赖光谱平坦的高斯噪声，无法区分真实高频细节与幻觉。

Method: 提出ASASR框架，将生成流重构成Sobolev诱导的黎曼几何，通过着色噪声转移核以镜像自然光谱衰减；基于Riesz表示定理集成参数化对抗方法，合成等价于最坏情况Sobolev梯度的针对性负样本，沿可行结构失效的切空间引导优化。

Result: 广泛评估表明ASASR优于领先的生成基线，特别是在保持光谱一致性和结构保真度方面，有效减轻了伪影。

Conclusion: ASASR通过几何对齐的理论框架，为图像超分辨率中的光谱一致性和结构保真度提供了鲁棒解决方案，有效缓解了生成先验带来的伪影问题。

Original Abstract: Generative priors in Image Super-Resolution (SR) often compromise faithful restoration, we attribute this limitation to a fundamental spectral misalignment between isotropic objectives and the intrinsic natural image manifold. While Direct Preference Optimization offers a path to alignment, its reliance on spectrally flat Gaussian noise fails to distinguish authentic high-frequency details from hallucinations. To bridge this geometric gap, we propose ASASR, a theoretically grounded framework that recasts the generative flow into a Sobolev-induced Riemannian geometry by explicitly coloring the noise transition kernel to mirror natural spectral decay. Driving this geometric alignment, we integrate a parametric adversary grounded in the Riesz Representation Theorem, which synthesizes targeted negative samples equivalent to worst-case Sobolev gradients to direct optimization along the tangent space of plausible structural failures. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that ASASR outperforms leading generative baselines, particularly in preserving spectral consistency and structural fidelity, offering a robust solution that effectively mitigates artifacts.

中文摘要翻译: 图像超分辨率中的生成先验常常在忠实恢复上做出妥协，我们将这一局限性归因于各向同性目标与自然图像流形之间存在根本性的光谱错位。尽管直接偏好优化提供了一条对齐路径，但其对光谱平坦的高斯噪声的依赖无法区分真实的高频细节与幻觉。为弥合这一几何鸿沟，我们提出了ASASR，一个具有理论基础的框架。该框架通过显式地对噪声转移核进行着色以镜像自然光谱衰减，将生成流重构成Sobolev诱导的黎曼几何。为推动这一几何对齐，我们集成了一个基于Riesz表示定理的参数化对抗方法，该方法合成等价的针对性负样本，作为最坏情况下的Sobolev梯度，沿可行结构失效的切空间引导优化。广泛评估表明，ASASR优于领先的生成基线，特别是在保持光谱一致性和结构保真度方面，提供了一种有效减轻伪影的鲁棒解决方案。

</details>


### [26] [ChainFlow-VLA: Causal Flow Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23270)
*Xiyang Wang,Xinlin Wang,Tingguang Zhou,Gong Chen,Xingtai Gui,Zhi Xu,Xiaolei Wu,Feiyang Tan,Hangning Zhou,Mu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChainFlow-VLA框架，通过自回归因果模式生成与扩散残差精炼的统一概率框架，解决端到端自动驾驶中时序因果推理与全局轨迹一致性的矛盾，在NAVSIM v1榜单达到SOTA（94.85），匹配人类水平（94.8）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将因果建模与全局优化视为分离的范式：自回归模型捕捉因果依赖但存在误差累积和次优全局结构，扩散模型优化全局轨迹但缺乏显式因果约束。两者缺乏在单一轨迹分布内统一因果生成与全局精炼的原则性方法。

Method: 提出ChainFlow-VLA，将规划建模为自回归诱导模式的混合分布，并学习VLM条件下的残差分布。自回归生成器（Chain）生成离散因果轨迹模式，扩散精炼器（Flow）利用VLM隐藏状态作为语义先验，在残差空间中进行模式条件校正，同时保留因果结构。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1榜单上取得94.85的SOTA分数，匹配人类表现水平（94.8），在模糊和长尾场景中实现鲁棒规划。

Conclusion: 通过将自回归因果生成与扩散全局精炼统一在单一概率框架中，有效弥合了时序因果推理与全局轨迹一致性之间的鸿沟，实现了高性能自动驾驶规划。

Original Abstract: Current end-to-end autonomous driving systems are fundamentally limited by a mismatch between temporal causal reasoning and global trajectory consistency. Autoregressive (AR) models capture interaction-aware temporal dependencies via causal factorization, but their step-wise decoding leads to error accumulation and suboptimal global structure. In contrast, diffusion models optimize trajectories globally but lack explicit causal constraints, making them unreliable in interactive and safety-critical scenarios. This dichotomy reveals a deeper issue: existing methods treat causal modeling and global optimization as separate paradigms, without a principled way to unify them within a single trajectory distribution. To address this, we propose ChainFlow-VLA, which unifies causal generation and global refinement within a unified probabilistic framework. We formulate planning as a mixture over AR-induced modes and learn Vision-Language Model (VLM)-conditioned residual distributions over these modes. An autoregressive generator (Chain) produces a discrete set of causal trajectory modes, followed by a diffusion-based refiner (Flow) that leverages VLM hidden states as semantic priors to perform mode-conditioned correction in residual space while preserving causal structure. This straightforward conditioning seamlessly injects high-level scene understanding into fine-grained trajectory adjustments. Experiments demonstrate that ChainFlow-VLA achieves robust planning in ambiguous and long-tail scenarios, achieving a state-of-the-art score of 94.85 on the NAVSIM v1 leaderboard, matching human-level performance (94.8). Code will be available at https://github.com/AFARI-Research/ChainFlow-VLA.

中文摘要翻译: 当前端到端自动驾驶系统从根本上受限于时序因果推理与全局轨迹一致性之间的不匹配。自回归（AR）模型通过因果分解捕捉交互感知的时序依赖，但其逐步解码导致误差累积和次优全局结构。相比之下，扩散模型在全局层面优化轨迹，但缺乏显式因果约束，在交互型和安全关键场景中不可靠。这种二分法揭示了一个更深层次的问题：现有方法将因果建模和全局优化视为分离的范式，缺乏在单一轨迹分布内统一它们的原理性方法。为解决这一问题，我们提出ChainFlow-VLA，它在统一的概率框架内统一了因果生成与全局精炼。我们将规划建模为自回归诱导模式的混合分布，并学习视觉语言模型（VLM）条件下的残差分布。自回归生成器（Chain）产生一组离散的因果轨迹模式，随后基于扩散的精炼器（Flow）利用VLM隐藏状态作为语义先验，在残差空间中进行模式条件的校正，同时保留因果结构。这种直接的条件注入方式将高层场景理解无缝地融入细粒度轨迹调整中。实验表明，ChainFlow-VLA在模糊和长尾场景中实现了鲁棒规划，在NAVSIM v1榜单上取得了94.85的SOTA分数，匹配人类表现水平（94.8）。代码将开源在 https://github.com/AFARI-Research/ChainFlow-VLA。

</details>


### [27] [DepthAgent: Towards Better Universal Depth Estimation via Sample-wise Expert Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23281)
*Jie Zhu,Girish Chandar Ganesan,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DepthAgent，一个视觉-语言智能体，通过多轮工具调用自适应选择/融合多个单目深度估计专家模型，利用不同模型在不同相机几何下的互补性，在透视、鱼眼和全景图像上取得优于单个专家和固定融合策略的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的单目深度估计方法通常依赖单一模型，忽略了不同模型在不同相机设置（透视、鱼眼、全景）下的互补性——模型偏好与相机几何高度相关，且多模型融合在困难样本上收益最大。

Method: 提出DepthAgent，一个视觉-语言智能体：将已有的深度模型视为冻结工具，通过学习分析场景和相机线索，进行多轮工具调用来选择合适的专家，并对各专家预测进行选择或融合。采用多奖励强化微调方案，联合优化工具执行、场景分析、专家选择质量和推理效率。

Result: 在透视、鱼眼和全景基准上，DepthAgent一致优于单个专家、固定模型融合及不同选择策略，在困难样本上提升显著，验证了专家选择与融合的关键作用。

Conclusion: 多模型专家之间存在样本级互补性，通过智能体进行自适应专家选择和融合可以显著提升跨相机设置的单目深度估计鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Monocular metric depth estimation has achieved strong progress with large-scale training and universal-camera modeling, yet robust deployment across diverse camera settings, such as perspective, fisheye, and panoramic images, remains challenging. Existing methods typically rely on a single depth estimator, overlooking that different models encode different camera assumptions and perform best under different input domains. In this paper, we show that depth experts exhibit strong sample-wise complementarity: model preference is highly correlated with camera geometry, and multi-model fusion brings the largest gains on difficult samples where individual experts are unreliable. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{\ours}, a vision-language agent for adaptive monocular depth estimation. DepthAgent treats existing depth models as frozen tools and learns to analyze scene and camera cues, invoke suitable experts through multi-turn tool utilization, and select or fuse their predictions for each input. To optimize such discrete decision-making toward dense geometric quality, we design a multi-reward reinforcement fine-tuning scheme that jointly encourages valid tool execution, camera/scene analysis, expert-selection quality, and inference efficiency. Extensive experiments across perspective, fisheye, and panoramic benchmarks show that \ours consistently outperforms individual experts, fixed model fusion, and different selection strategies, with strong improvements on challenging samples, highlighting the critical role of expert selection and fusion. The code and model will be released upon publication.

中文摘要翻译: 单目度量深度估计通过大规模训练和通用相机建模取得了显著进展，但在不同相机设置（如透视、鱼眼和全景图像）下的鲁棒部署仍然具有挑战性。现有方法通常依赖单一的深度估计器，忽略了不同模型编码了不同的相机假设并在不同输入域下表现最佳的事实。本文表明，深度专家模型展现出显著的样本级互补性：模型偏好与相机几何高度相关，且多模型融合在单个专家不可靠的困难样本上带来最大收益。受这些观察启发，我们提出DepthAgent，一个用于自适应单目深度估计的视觉-语言智能体。DepthAgent将已有的深度模型视为冻结工具，学习分析场景和相机线索，通过多轮工具调用选择合适的专家，并对每个输入的预测结果进行选择或融合。为了将这种离散决策优化为密集的几何质量，我们设计了一种多奖励强化微调方案，联合鼓励有效的工具执行、场景/相机分析、专家选择质量和推理效率。在透视、鱼眼和全景基准上的大量实验表明，DepthAgent一致优于单个专家、固定模型融合以及不同的选择策略，在困难样本上提升显著，突显了专家选择与融合的关键作用。代码和模型将在发表后公开。

</details>


### [28] [LangFlash: Feed-forward 3D Language Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23287)
*Yilong Liu,Wanhua Li,Chen Zhu-Tian,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LangFlash 是一种前馈式3D语言高斯溅射框架，能从稀疏无位姿的多视图图像中直接预测3D几何和语义特征，实现低延迟重建和语义一致的场景理解，无需位姿估计或逐场景优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于优化的3D语义场景重建方法需要位姿信息和逐场景优化，计算开销大、延迟高，难以扩展到大规模应用。作者希望提出一种无需位姿、单次前向传播即可同时预测几何与语义的高效框架。

Method: 1) 提出前馈式3D语言高斯溅射框架，直接从稀疏无位姿多视图图像预测高斯原语的几何和语言对齐语义特征；2) 为RealEstate10k数据集添加密集语义标注用于3D语义监督；3) 提出稀疏语义编码方案，结合全局语义字典与逐原语局部权重，降低表示复杂度同时保留高层语义信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，LangFlash在 novel view synthesis 和语义一致性方面均优于以往方法。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种无位姿、语言引导的3D场景重建新范式，推动了可泛化3D视觉和多模态场景理解的发展。

Original Abstract: We present LangFlash, a feed-forward framework for 3D Language Gaussian Splatting that reconstructs 3D scenes parameterized by Gaussian primitives enriched with language-aligned semantic features from sparse unposed multi-view images. Unlike optimization-based 3D methods, LangFlash directly predicts the geometry and semantics in a single forward pass, enabling low-latency 3D reconstruction and language-consistent scene understanding. To support large-scale training, we enriched the RealEstate10k dataset with coherent and dense semantic information for 3D semantic supervision. Furthermore, we propose a sparse semantic encoding scheme that combines a global semantic dictionary with locally varying per-primitive weights, preserving high-level linguistic information, while reducing representation complexity. Experimental results show that LangFlash achieves superior novel view synthesis and semantic consistency compared with previous methods. This study establishes a new paradigm for pose-free, language-grounded 3D scene reconstruction, advancing generalizable 3D vision and multimodal scene understanding. Demo is available at https://liylo.github.io/langflash.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出LangFlash，一种前馈式3D语言高斯溅射框架，能够从稀疏的、无位姿的多视图图像中重建由高斯原语参数化的3D场景，并为这些原语赋予与语言对齐的语义特征。与基于优化的3D方法不同，LangFlash在单次前向传播中直接预测几何和语义，实现了低延迟的3D重建和语言一致的场景理解。为了支持大规模训练，我们为RealEstate10k数据集丰富了连贯且密集的语义信息，用于3D语义监督。此外，我们提出了一种稀疏语义编码方案，将全局语义字典与局部变化的逐原语权重相结合，在保留高层语言信息的同时降低表示复杂度。实验结果表明，与以往方法相比，LangFlash在新视角合成和语义一致性方面均达到了更优的性能。本研究建立了一种无位姿、语言引导的3D场景重建新范式，推动了可泛化3D视觉和多模态场景理解的发展。演示可在https://liylo.github.io/langflash.github.io/获取。

</details>


### [29] [Spatio-Temporal Similarity Volume Aggregation for Open-Vocabulary Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23288)
*Yerim So,Jiyeong Kim,Jiwon Yoon,Dongbo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SimVA框架，通过构建稠密4D时空相似度体积来保持局部视觉-文本对应关系，从而将CLIP有效迁移到视频动作识别任务中。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放词汇动作识别方法通常将视觉特征聚合为全局表示后再计算文本对齐，这一过程会丢失局部patches信息和细粒度时空线索。

Method: SimVA框架主要包括：(1) 构建基于patch级视觉-文本相似度的稠密4D时空相似度体积；(2) 采用类别采样确保对大词表的可扩展性；(3) 空间聚合细化局部相似度模式以提升帧内一致性；(4) 运动感知调制注入帧间变化线索，突出动态变化区域；(5) 基于Mamba的时序聚合建模跨帧的类别条件相似度演化。

Result: 在零样本、少样本和基类到新类三个基准测试中均取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过保持稠密的视觉-文本对应关系，SimVA能够有效将CLIP迁移到视频动作识别任务中。

Original Abstract: Recent Open-Vocabulary Action Recognition (OVAR) methods typically aggregate visual features into a global representation before computing text alignment, a process that obscures local patch information and fine-grained spatio-temporal cues. We propose Similarity Volume Aggregation (SimVA), a framework that constructs a dense 4D spatio-temporal similarity volume from patch-level visual-text similarities. SimVA constructs a spatio-temporal similarity volume over local video tokens and action classes, and employs class sampling to ensure similarity aggregation scalable to large vocabularies. The similarity volume is refined by spatial aggregation, which contextualizes local similarity patterns to improve intra-frame consistency. Motion-aware modulation further injects inter-frame variation cues, highlighting dynamically changing regions. Mamba-based temporal aggregation then models the evolution of class-conditioned similarity patterns across frames. By maintaining dense visual-text correspondence, SimVA effectively transfers CLIP to video action recognition, achieving competitive performance across zero-shot, few-shot, and base-to-novel benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的开放词汇动作识别方法通常先将视觉特征聚合成全局表示，再计算文本对齐，这一过程会掩盖局部patch信息和细粒度的时空线索。本文提出相似度体积聚合框架SimVA，该框架从patch级别的视觉-文本相似度构建稠密的4D时空相似度体积。SimVA在局部视频token和动作类别上构建时空相似度体积，并采用类别采样确保相似度聚合可扩展到大规模词表。通过空间聚合细化相似度体积，将局部相似度模式语境化以提升帧内一致性。运动感知调制进一步注入帧间变化线索，突出动态变化的区域。基于Mamba的时序聚合则建模跨帧的类别条件相似度演化。通过保持稠密的视觉-文本对应关系，SimVA有效地将CLIP迁移到视频动作识别中，在零样本、少样本和基类到新类基准测试中均取得了具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [30] [General Hazard Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23304)
*Stephanie Ng,CP Lim,SueJen Looi,Hendrik Zurlinden,David Nguyen,Lei Wei,Saeid Nahavandi,Hailing Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CompliVision数据集，首个面向规则合规评估的通用危险检测数据集，通过将危险概念与图像解耦、基于语言规则表达安全要求，并结合主动学习框架与LLaVA视觉推理，解决了抽象安全概念检测在数据稀疏、定义动态变化和泛化性差三方面的挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有危险检测系统依赖预定义类别和大量标注示例，面临三大根本挑战：训练数据噪声大且稀疏、危险定义随上下文和时间动态变化、对未见或新场景泛化能力有限。因此需要一种不依赖具体图像示例、基于规则表达的通用危险检测方法。

Method: (1) 构建CompliVision数据集：包含3,006张涵盖交通、建筑和仓库环境的图像，每张图像针对特定安全规则进行合规性标注，并附有自然语言解释；关键创新是将危险概念与图像示例解耦，通过基于语言的安全规则来表达要求，并依据权威领域法规和ISO标准定义危险概念。(2) 提出主动学习框架，引导和优化视觉-语言模型的危险合规评估能力。(3) 结合LLaVA的视觉推理与人机交互反馈，构建通用危险检测框架。

Result: 实验表明，当前最先进的视觉-语言模型在细粒度、上下文依赖的危险合规评估中表现不足，需要更精细的解释能力；而本文提出的主动学习框架和LLaVA+人机交互方案能够有效提升危险检测的准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 将危险概念与具体图像示例解耦、采用语言规则表达安全要求，结合主动学习与视觉-语言模型的交互式推理，是解决抽象安全概念检测中数据稀疏、定义动态变化和泛化性差问题的有效路径。

Original Abstract: Hazard, as an abstract concept, is typically defined through cognitive-level logical reasoning rather than concrete examples. In contrast, existing hazard detection systems rely on predefined hazard categories and require intensive collection of labelled examples within detection or classification architectures. This approach faces three fundamental challenges when addressing abstract safety concepts: (1) noisy and sparse training data, (2) dynamically evolving definitions that change across contexts and time, and (3) limited generalisation to unseen or novel scenarios. To address these limitations, we present the CompliVision dataset, the first general-purpose hazard dataset designed for rule-based compliance assessment, along with a baseline framework for hazard evaluation. Our key innovation is decoupling the hazard concept from image-based examples by expressing safety requirements through language-based rules. We ground our approach in authoritative domain regulations and ISO standards to define diverse hazard concepts across multiple domains. The CompliVision dataset comprises 3,006 images spanning traffic, construction, and warehouse environments, with each image annotated for compliance against specific safety rules, accompanied by natural language explanations highlighting the supporting visual evidence.
