To achieve robust generalisation, we develop an active learning framework to more effectively guide and refine vision-language models in assessing hazard compliance. While state-of-the-art VLMs demonstrate strong capabilities, they struggle with the fine-grained, context-dependent interpretation required for accurate safety assessment. We proposed a general hazard detection framework to address this limitation which combines LLaVA-based visual reasoning with with human-in-the-loop feedback.

中文摘要翻译: 危险作为一个抽象概念，通常通过认知层面的逻辑推理而非具体示例来定义。相比之下，现有的危险检测系统依赖于预定义的危险类别，并需要在检测或分类架构中大量收集标注示例。这种方法在应对抽象安全概念时面临三个根本性挑战：(1) 训练数据噪声大且稀疏，(2) 定义随上下文和时间动态演变，(3) 对未见或新场景的泛化能力有限。为应对这些局限，我们提出了CompliVision数据集——首个面向规则合规评估的通用危险数据集，并配套搭建了危险评估的基线框架。我们的核心创新在于，通过基于语言的规则来表达安全要求，从而将危险概念与基于图像的具体示例解耦。我们将方法建立在权威领域法规和ISO标准之上，跨多个领域定义了多样化的危险概念。CompliVision数据集包含3,006张图像，涵盖交通、建筑和仓库环境，每张图像均针对特定安全规则进行了合规性标注，并附有突出显示相关视觉证据的自然语言解释。为实现鲁棒的泛化能力，我们开发了一个主动学习框架，以更有效地引导和优化视觉-语言模型在危险合规评估中的表现。尽管当前最先进的视觉-语言模型展现了强大的能力，但它们在准确安全评估所需的细粒度、上下文依赖的解释方面仍存在困难。为此，我们提出了一个通用危险检测框架，将基于LLaVA的视觉推理与人机交互反馈相结合，以解决这一局限。

</details>


### [31] [GFSR: Geometric Fidelity and Spatial Refinement for Reliable Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23327)
*Tiancheng Wang,Zhaolu Ding,Richeng Xu,Tianhui Zheng,Hui Liu,Hanyu Xuan,Zhiliang Wu,Guanghui Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GFSR框架，通过LaneIoU引导的置信度校准（LCC）和自适应门控位置细化（AGLR）两个模块，分别解决车道检测中分类置信度与几何质量脱节、以及回归模块削弱采样点关联导致复杂车道欠拟合的问题。在CULane和CurveLanes上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有车道检测方法存在两个主要局限：(1) 分类置信度仅表征车道候选的类别存在性，与几何质量缺乏强相关性，导致基于置信度的阈值过滤和NMS可能保留高置信度但几何质量差的先验，而丢弃低置信度但几何表现更优的先验；(2) 现有回归模块削弱了采样点之间的相关性，阻碍了对远距离、高曲率和复杂拓扑车道的细粒度优化，导致欠拟合。

Method: 提出GFSR框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) LaneIoU引导的置信度校准（LCC）：采用LaneIoU作为软监督信号，显式估计车道先验的几何保真度，再与分类置信度融合构建协同可靠性指标（CRI），用于指导阈值过滤和NMS；(2) 自适应门控位置细化（AGLR）：在每个细化阶段与回归头协作，预测采样点横向偏移量并采用门控机制自适应调节修正幅度，增强点间相关性，提升模型对复杂车道场景的适应性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在CULane上，F1@50和F1@75分别达到81.46%和65.01%；在CurveLanes上，F1@50达到87.35%，均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GFSR框架有效解决了车道检测中置信度与几何质量脱节以及复杂车道回归欠拟合两大关键问题，通过LCC和AGLR两个模块的协同作用，显著提升了模型在复杂场景下的车道检测性能。

Original Abstract: Lane detection stands as a crucial perception task in autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. However, existing methods still degrade in complex real scenarios due to two major limitations. First, classification confidence only characterizes the categorical existence of lane candidates and has no strong correlation with geometric quality. If threshold filtering and NMS are conducted merely based on this confidence, the model tends to retain lane priors with high confidence while eliminating those with lower confidence but superior geometric representation. Secondly, existing regression modules weaken correlations among sampling points, hindering fine-grained optimization of distant, high-curvature and complex-topology lanes and causing underfitting. To address these issues, we propose Geometric Fidelity and Spatial Refinement (GFSR), a framework consisting of LaneIoU-guided Confidence Calibration (LCC) and Adaptive Gated Location Refinement (AGLR). Specifically, LCC adopts LaneIoU as soft supervision to explicitly estimate geometric fidelity of lane priors, which is further fused with classification confidence to construct the collaborative reliability index (CRI). This index guides threshold filtering and NMS, effectively retaining lane priors with high classification confidence and favorable geometric quality. Meanwhile, cooperating with regression heads in each refinement stage, AGLR predicts sampling point lateral offsets and adopts a gating mechanism to adaptively regulate correction magnitude, strengthen inter-point correlations and boost model adaptability as well as robustness toward complex lane scenarios. Extensive experiments on CULane and CurveLanes demonstrate that our GFSR achieves state-of-the-art performance on CULane, with F1@50 and F1@75 scores of 81.46% and 65.01%, and reaches 87.35% F1@50 on CurveLanes.

中文摘要翻译: 车道检测是自动驾驶和高级驾驶辅助系统中的一项关键感知任务。然而，现有方法在复杂真实场景中仍会出现性能下降，原因在于两个主要局限。首先，分类置信度仅表征车道候选的类别存在性，与几何质量缺乏强相关性。若仅基于该置信度进行阈值过滤和非极大值抑制，模型倾向于保留高置信度的车道先验，同时丢弃那些置信度较低但几何表示更优的先验。其次，现有回归模块削弱了采样点之间的相关性，阻碍了对远距离、高曲率和复杂拓扑车道的细粒度优化，导致欠拟合。为解决这些问题，我们提出了几何保真度与空间细化（GFSR）框架，包含LaneIoU引导的置信度校准（LCC）和自适应门控位置细化（AGLR）。具体而言，LCC采用LaneIoU作为软监督信号，显式估计车道先验的几何保真度，再与分类置信度融合构建协同可靠性指标（CRI）。该指标指导阈值过滤和NMS，有效保留具有高分类置信度和良好几何质量的车道先验。同时，AGLR在每个细化阶段与回归头协作，预测采样点横向偏移量并采用门控机制自适应调节修正幅度，增强点间相关性，提升模型对复杂车道场景的适应性和鲁棒性。在CULane和CurveLanes上的大量实验表明，我们的GFSR在CULane上取得了SOTA性能，F1@50和F1@75分数分别为81.46%和65.01%，在CurveLanes上F1@50达到87.35%。

</details>


### [32] [CHASD: Language Increment-Calibrated Contrastive Decoding against Hallucination in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23344)
*Xiaoyi Huang,Kejia Zhang,Zhiming Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHASD，一种基于不确定性驱动的按需对比解码框架，通过注意力引导的局部扰动和置信度门控，在减少计算开销的同时有效缓解大视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对比解码方法存在两个问题：一是全局扰动可能破坏有用的视觉证据，二是每个解码步骤都需要额外调用负分支，计算成本高。观察到幻觉风险是瞬态且与特定token相关的，因此希望实现“按需校准”。

Method: 提出CHASD框架：1）使用不确定性驱动的置信度门控，仅在最大概率低于阈值时激活对比分支；2）通过注意力引导的局部扰动，只扰动当前关注的视觉token来构建负分支；3）高置信度步骤保留原始分布，避免不必要的负分支前向传播。

Result: 在POPE、AMBER、MME、MMHal-Bench和CHAIR五个基准上，CHASD在幻觉相关指标上优于强训练无关基线方法，同时保持有竞争力的推理效率。

Conclusion: CHASD通过按需校准策略在减少计算开销的同时有效缓解物体幻觉，验证了幻觉风险的瞬态和token特异性特点。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have shown strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, yet they remain susceptible to object hallucinations when language priors dominate insufficient or misaligned visual evidence. Training-free contrastive decoding methods mitigate this issue by comparing predictions from original and perturbed visual inputs, but existing approaches either apply global perturbations that may alter useful visual evidence or invoke an additional negative branch at every decoding step. In this paper, we observe that hallucination risks are transient and token-specific: visual attention shifts across generated tokens, while some functional tokens are produced with high confidence and do not require contrastive calibration. Based on this observation, we propose Contrastive Hallucination-Aware Step-wise Decoding (CHASD) for Large Vision-Language Models, an inference-time framework for "calibration on demand". CHASD uses an uncertainty-driven confidence gate to activate the contrastive branch only when the maximum probability of the next-token is less than the threshold, and constructs the negative branch through attention-guided localized perturbations of the currently salient visual tokens. This design reduces unnecessary negative-branch forward passes while preserving the original distribution for high-confidence steps. Experiments on POPE, AMBER, MME, MMHal-Bench, and CHAIR show that CHASD improves hallucination-related metrics over strong training-free baselines with competitive inference efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型展现出强大的多模态推理能力，但当语言先验主导了不充分或不对齐的视觉证据时，它们仍然容易产生物体幻觉。无训练的对比解码方法通过比较原始和扰动视觉输入的预测来缓解这一问题，但现有方法要么应用可能改变有用视觉证据的全局扰动，要么在每个解码步骤都调用额外的负分支。本文观察到，幻觉风险是瞬态且与特定token相关的：视觉注意力在生成的token之间发生转移，而一些功能token以高置信度生成，不需要对比校准。基于这一观察，我们提出了面向大视觉语言模型的对比幻觉感知逐步解码（CHASD），这是一种用于“按需校准”的推理时框架。CHASD使用不确定性驱动的置信度门控，仅在下个token的最大概率低于阈值时激活对比分支，并通过注意力引导的局部扰动来构建当前关注的视觉token的负分支。这种设计减少了不必要的负分支前向传播，同时保留高置信度步骤的原始分布。在POPE、AMBER、MME、MMHal-Bench和CHAIR上的实验表明，CHASD在幻觉相关指标上优于强训练无关基线方法，同时具有竞争力的推理效率。

</details>


### [33] [SCOPE: Simulating Cross-game Operations in Playable Environments for FPS World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23345)
*Zizhao Tong,Hongfeng Lai,Zeqing Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Kexu Cheng,Haoran Xu,Zhao Pu,Shangwen Zhu,Ruili Feng,Jian Zhao,Yan Zhang,Hao Tang,Yeying Jin,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对第一人称射击游戏中第一人称视角世界模型在密集控制信号下的动作响应与区域分离难题，提出SCOPE框架，通过在预训练视频扩散模型中插入每个Transformer块的条件调制模块，将特征重塑为逐像素时序序列，实现动作影响区域的精准局部化；同时构建首个多游戏FPS数据集CrossFPS（7款游戏69K片段，10自由度控制器信号），实现跨游戏零样本泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法全局注入动作且只在单一游戏上训练，无法处理FPS游戏中高频率重叠的控制信号，且无法在不影响未受区域的情况下对每个帧进行精准的局部动作响应。

Method: 提出SCOPE，在预训练视频扩散模型的每个Transformer模块中插入条件调制模块，将特征重塑为逐像素时序序列，使每个位置能够根据局部视觉内容计算其动作响应，从而无需语义分割标签即可分离受动作影响的局部区域（如武器周围）和未受影响区域。同时构建CrossFPS多游戏FPS数据集。

Result: 实验表明该方法具备强动作响应能力、精确的作用域分离效果以及有效的跨游戏泛化能力，能够在未见过的场景上实现零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过空间选择性动作条件机制解决了FPS游戏中密集控制信号的局部化建模难题，并借助多游戏数据集实现了跨游戏的通用视觉-动作映射学习。

Original Abstract: Interactive world models for first-person shooter (FPS) games must resolve high-frequency overlapping control signals at every frame without disrupting unaffected regions. Existing methods inject actions globally and train on single titles, failing under dense FPS inputs. We observe that FPS actions are spatially selective: discrete events such as firing or reloading affect only a localized region around the weapon (the scope), while continuous camera and movement signals govern stable surroundings. We propose SCOPE, which inserts a conditioning module into each transformer block of a pretrained video diffusion model. It reshapes features into per-pixel temporal sequences so that each position computes its action response from local visual content. This separates in-scope effects from out-of-scope generation without segmentation labels. We also introduce CrossFPS, the first multi-game FPS dataset with frame-aligned action telemetry. It comprises 69K clips from 7 titles with 10-DoF controller signals, curated to remove gameplay bias. The model learns general visual-to-action mappings rather than game-specific patterns, enabling zero-shot transfer to unseen scenes. Experiments confirm strong action responsiveness, precise scope separation, and effective cross-game generalization.

中文摘要翻译: 第一人称射击（FPS）游戏中的交互式世界模型需要在每一帧上解析高频率重叠的控制信号，同时不干扰未受影响的区域。现有方法全局注入动作且仅在单一游戏上训练，无法应对密集的FPS输入。我们观察到FPS动作具有空间选择性：离散事件（如开火或换弹）仅影响武器周围的局部区域（作用域），而连续的摄像头和移动信号则控制稳定的周边环境。我们提出SCOPE，该方法在预训练视频扩散模型的每个Transformer模块中插入一个条件调制模块。它将特征重塑为逐像素时序序列，使得每个位置能够根据局部视觉内容计算其动作响应。这无需分割标签即可将作用域内效果与作用域外生成分离开来。我们还引入了CrossFPS，这是首个带有帧对齐动作遥测的多游戏FPS数据集。它包含来自7款游戏的69K段剪辑，具有10自由度控制器信号，经整理以消除游戏偏差。该模型学习通用的视觉到动作映射而非特定于游戏的模式，从而实现对未见场景的零样本迁移。实验结果证实了其强动作响应能力、精确的作用域分离效果以及有效的跨游戏泛化能力。

</details>


### [34] [Decoupling Spatio-Temporal Adapter for Fine-Grained Badminton Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23355)
*Tianyu Wang,Junjie Wu,Jingquan Gao,Shishuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对专业羽毛球视频中的细粒度时序动作定位问题，提出了一个新的基准数据集Fine-Badminton和一个解耦时空适配器（DSTA），在参数高效框架下有效建模时空特征，在少量计算和参数增加的情况下达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时序动作定位研究主要面向通用视频理解，而专业体育场景（如羽毛球）因动作复杂微妙、时空动态精细而尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出解耦时空适配器（DSTA），将运动表征分解为三个并行分支：时间动态分支、垂直空间变化分支和水平空间变化分支，从而在参数高效框架下捕捉细粒度动作的细微差异。

Result: 在Fine-Badminton数据集和ShuttleSet基准上，所提方法达到了最先进的性能，同时仅引入极少的计算和参数开销。

Conclusion: DSTA方法在细粒度时序动作定位任务上既有效又高效，验证了其在实际应用中的可行性。

Original Abstract: Temporal Action Localization (TAL) has been extensively studied in generic video understanding, while fine-grained sports scenarios, such as professional badminton, remain underexplored due to their complex and subtle spatio-temporal dynamics. In this paper, we focus on fine-grained TAL in professional badminton videos and introduce a new benchmark dataset, Fine-Badminton, which consists of 31 matches with 29 fine-grained stroke categories, covering 2104 rallies and 27597 annotated actions. To effectively capture the intricate motion patterns in such scenarios, we propose a Decoupling Spatio-Temporal Adapter (DSTA), which enables efficient modeling of spatio-temporal features within a parameter-efficient framework. Specifically, DSTA decomposes motion representation into three parallel branches, capturing temporal dynamics as well as vertical and horizontal spatial variations. The design allows the model to better distinguish subtle differences among fine-grained actions. Extensive experiments on both the Fine-Badminton dataset and the ShuttleSet benchmark demonstrate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while introducing only a marginal increase in computational and parameter cost. These results validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach for fine-grained temporal action localization.

中文摘要翻译: 时序动作定位（TAL）在通用视频理解领域已被广泛研究，而在专业羽毛球等细粒度体育场景中，由于其复杂且微妙的时空动态，相关研究仍不充分。本文聚焦于专业羽毛球视频中的细粒度时序动作定位，提出了一个新的基准数据集Fine-Badminton，该数据集包含31场比赛、29个细粒度击球类别，涵盖2104个回合和27597个标注动作。为了有效捕捉此类场景中复杂的运动模式，我们提出了一种解耦时空适配器（DSTA），能够在参数高效框架内实现时空特征的高效建模。具体而言，DSTA将运动表征分解为三个并行分支，分别捕捉时间动态以及垂直和水平方向的空间变化。该设计使得模型能够更好地区分细粒度动作之间的细微差异。在Fine-Badminton数据集和ShuttleSet基准上的大量实验表明，所提方法在仅引入极小的计算和参数开销的情况下，达到了最先进的性能。这些结果验证了所提方法在细粒度时序动作定位任务中的有效性和高效性。

</details>


### [35] [VDE: Training-Free Accelerating Rectified Flow Model via Velocity Decomposition and Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23381)
*Junwen Tan,Jinglin Liang,Hongyuan Chen,Shuangping Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出速度分解与估计（VDE）方法，通过将模型速度分解为平行和正交分量并自适应估计，实现无需训练的推理加速，在图像和视频生成中取得3.22倍加速且质量损失极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 整流流模型虽在图像、视频和3D生成中表现优异，但推理速度慢。现有加速方法重用先前步骤的缓存特征，忽略了静态缓存与动态输入之间不断增长的失配问题，导致输出保真度下降。

Method: 将模型速度分解为平行于输入和正交于输入的分量，利用其时间可预测性和方向稳定性进行输入自适应的精确估计；通过定期执行完整前向传播来锚定模型状态，防止误差累积。

Result: 在图像和视频生成任务上实现显著加速，其中Flux加速3.22倍，Qwen-Image上LPIPS达到0.069，相比最佳基线降低52.2%。

Conclusion: VDE通过从缓存重用范式转向分解估计范式，有效解决了加速过程中的误差累积问题，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升推理速度。

Original Abstract: Though rectified flow models have achieved remarkable performance in image, video, and 3D generation, their practical deployments are challenged by slow inference speeds. Prior acceleration methods reuse cached features from previous steps, which neglects the growing mismatch between static caches and the evolving input, leading to reduced output fidelity. This work proposes Velocity Decomposition and Estimation (VDE), a training-free acceleration method that shifts the paradigm from caching-and-reusing to decomposing-and-estimating. Specifically, VDE decomposes the model's velocity into components parallel and orthogonal to the input, exploiting their temporal predictability and directional stability for precise, input-adaptive estimation. To prevent error accumulation, it periodically anchors the model's state via full forward passes. Extensive experiments on image and video generation tasks demonstrate that VDE achieves substantial acceleration with minimal loss in visual quality. Notably, VDE accelerates Flux by 3.22 times and achieves an LPIPS of 0.069 on Qwen-Image, outperforming the best baseline with a 52.2% reduction.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管整流流模型在图像、视频和3D生成领域取得了显著性能，但其实际部署因推理速度慢而面临挑战。先前的加速方法重复使用先前步骤的缓存特征，忽略了静态缓存与不断演化的输入之间日益增长的失配问题，导致输出保真度下降。本文提出速度分解与估计（VDE），一种无需训练的加速方法，将范式从缓存重用转向分解估计。具体而言，VDE将模型速度分解为平行于输入和正交于输入的分量，利用其时间可预测性和方向稳定性进行精确的输入自适应估计。为防止误差累积，它通过完整前向传播定期锚定模型状态。在图像和视频生成任务上的大量实验表明，VDE在视觉质量损失极小的情况下实现了显著加速。值得注意的是，VDE将Flux加速了3.22倍，并在Qwen-Image上取得了0.069的LPIPS，相比最佳基线降低了52.2%。

</details>


### [36] [Joint Target-Less Intrinsic and Extrinsic Camera-LiDAR Calibration using Deep Point Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23397)
*Simon Bultmann,Daniele Cattaneo,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个无需标定板的端到端相机-LiDAR联合标定方法，同时估计相机内参（含畸变）和外参，无需已知内参或图像矫正，在KITTI数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度点对应的无目标标定方法虽然效果优异，但都假设图像已矫正且内参已知。为突破这一限制，实现完全无目标的相机-LiDAR标定，本文提出可同时估计未知内参（含畸变）和外参的端到端方法。

Method: (i) 通过运动恢复结构自动初始化内参；(ii) 将相机-LiDAR匹配推广到含未知内参和畸变的原始图像；(iii) 将对应关系估计与内参、外参的联合非线性优化紧密耦合。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上对未见过的相机-LiDAR对进行评估，结果表明联合标定在提升外参精度的同时，还能恢复准确的内参。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了完全无目标、无需已知内参的相机-LiDAR联合标定，证明了深度像素-点对应关系可同时用于内参和外参的联合优化。

Original Abstract: Accurate camera-LiDAR calibration is a prerequisite for robust multi-modal perception in robotics. Recent target-less approaches based on deep point correspondences achieve remarkable performance for extrinsic calibration but assume rectified images with known intrinsics. In this work, we overcome this limitation and present the first fully target-less pipeline that jointly estimates camera intrinsics (pinhole model with radial-tangential distortion) and camera-LiDAR extrinsics with deep pixel-point correspondences. Our approach extends deep correspondence-based calibration by (i) automatic intrinsic initialization via structure-from-motion, (ii) generalizing camera-LiDAR matching to raw images with unknown intrinsics including distortion, and (iii) tightly coupling correspondence estimation with joint nonlinear optimization over both intrinsics and extrinsics. We evaluate our method on the KITTI dataset with unseen camera-LiDAR pairs and demonstrate that joint calibration achieves improved extrinsic accuracy while additionally recovering accurate intrinsics.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的相机-LiDAR标定是机器人稳健多模态感知的前提条件。近期基于深度点对应关系的无目标方法在外参标定方面取得了显著性能，但这些方法假设图像已矫正且内参已知。本文克服了这一限制，提出了首个完全无目标的标定流程，通过深度像素-点对应关系联合估计相机内参（针孔模型加径向-切向畸变）和相机-LiDAR外参。我们的方法在以下三个方面扩展了基于深度对应关系的标定技术：(i) 通过运动恢复结构实现内参自动初始化；(ii) 将相机-LiDAR匹配推广到含未知内参（包括畸变）的原始图像；(iii) 将对应关系估计与内参和外参的联合非线性优化紧密耦合。我们在KITTI数据集上对未见过的相机-LiDAR对进行了评估，结果表明联合标定在提升外参精度的同时还能恢复准确的内参。

</details>


### [37] [RS2AD-LiDAR: End-to-End Autonomous Driving LiDAR Data Generation from Roadside Sensor Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23406)
*Runyi Huang,Ni Ding,Ruidan Xing,Yuheng Shi,Lei He,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RS2AD-LiDAR框架，通过路边传感器点云重建生成车载LiDAR数据，解决车端数据采集成本高、场景稀缺和数据孤岛问题，构建R2V-LiDAR数据集验证，实验表明生成数据能提升BEV和3D检测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方案依赖单车数据采集，面临采集标注成本高、有价值场景稀缺、数据孤岛等挑战，亟需从路边传感器数据生成高质量车载LiDAR数据以弥补数据不足。

Method: 将路边LiDAR点云转换到车载LiDAR坐标系，通过虚拟LiDAR建模和点云重采样技术合成高保真的车载数据，并构建专用R2V-LiDAR数据集用于评估。

Result: 生成数据与真实数据具有语义相似性；将生成数据加入真实数据训练后，BEV和3D目标检测精度均得到提升，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 首次实现了从路边传感器输入重建车载LiDAR数据，为缓解车端数据稀缺问题提供了可行方案，具有良好的应用前景。

Original Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving solutions, which directly process multimodal sensory data and output fine-grained control commands, have gradually become a mainstream direction with the development of autonomous driving technology. However, current methods in this category rely on single-vehicle data collection for model training and optimization, which suffers from high acquisition and annotation costs, scarcity of valuable scenarios, and data silos. To address these challenges, we propose RS2AD-LiDAR, a novel framework for reconstructing and generating vehicle-mounted LiDAR data from roadside sensor observations. Since no public dataset currently provides highly overlapping perception coverage between roadside and vehicle-mounted LiDAR sensors, which is essential for studying roadside-to-vehicle data generation, we constructed a dedicated dataset named R2V-LiDAR which is used solely for evaluation in this work. Specifically, our method transforms roadside LiDAR point clouds into the vehicle-mounted LiDAR coordinate system, and synthesizes high-fidelity vehicle-mounted data via virtual LiDAR modeling and point cloud resampling techniques. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach to reconstruct vehicle-mounted LiDAR data from roadside sensor inputs. Extensive experimental comparisons demonstrate the semantic similarity between the generated data and real data. Furthermore, object detection experiments show that incorporating the generated data into real data for model training improves both Bird's Eye View (BEV) and 3D detection accuracy, thereby validating the effectiveness of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 端到端自动驾驶方案直接处理多模态传感数据并输出细粒度的控制指令，随着自动驾驶技术的发展，这类方法已逐渐成为主流方向。然而，当前该类方法依赖单车数据采集进行模型训练与优化，存在采集与标注成本高、有价值场景稀缺以及数据孤岛等问题。为应对这些挑战，我们提出RS2AD-LiDAR，一个从路边传感器观测数据中重建与生成车载LiDAR数据的新型框架。由于目前尚无公开数据集提供路边与车载LiDAR传感器之间具有高度重叠感知覆盖的数据——而这对于研究路边到车载数据生成至关重要——我们构建了一个专用数据集R2V-LiDAR，本工作仅将其用于评估。具体而言，我们的方法将路边LiDAR点云转换到车载LiDAR坐标系下，并通过虚拟LiDAR建模和点云重采样技术合成高保真的车载数据。据我们所知，这是首个从路边传感器输入重建车载LiDAR数据的方法。大量实验对比表明，生成数据与真实数据具有语义相似性。此外，目标检测实验结果显示，将生成数据加入真实数据中进行模型训练，能够同时提升鸟瞰图（BEV）和3D检测精度，从而验证了所提出方法的有效性。

</details>


### [38] [Online Hand Gesture Recognition Using 3D Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23409)
*Yinghao Qin,Tijana Timotijevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线手势识别系统，能够在实时视频流中定位和识别动态手势，采用滑动窗口方法提升鲁棒性，在Jester数据集上检测器精度达98%+，分类器精度达90%+，自制数据集上最佳分组在3秒内响应并达到37.5%的Levenshtein准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人机交互中动态手势的实时检测与分类面临两大挑战：系统必须在实时视频流中无延迟响应，以及不同用户执行手势的方式存在巨大差异导致识别困难。

Method: 提出一种在线手势识别系统，能够实时定位和识别视频流中的手势；采用滑动窗口方法对多个窗口的结果进行细化以提升系统鲁棒性。

Result: 在Jester数据集上，检测器准确率超过98%，分类器准确率超过90%；在自制数据集上，最佳分组可在3秒内响应，Levenshtein准确率达到37.5%。项目代码已公开。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线手势识别系统在实时性和准确性方面均取得了良好表现，验证了方法的有效性，且代码已开源。

Original Abstract: In human computer interaction, real-time detection and classification of dynamic hand gestures is challenging as: 1) the system must run in a real-time video stream and there is no noticeable lag in response after performing a gesture; 2) there is a large difference in how people perform gestures, making recognition more difficult. In this paper, an online hand gesture recognition system is proposed, which is able to localize gestures in real-time video stream and recognize what these gestures are. To improve the robustness of the system, the sliding window approach is used to refine results from multiple windows. All of the models in my project are trained on Jester database, achieving 98+% accuracy for detector and 90+% accuracy for classifier. For the overall performance of the system, the best group can respond within three seconds and reach 37.5% Levenshtein accuracy on the homemade dataset. The project codes used in this work are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 在人机交互中，动态手势的实时检测与分类具有挑战性，原因在于：1）系统必须在实时视频流中运行，且执行手势后不能有明显延迟；2）不同用户执行手势的方式存在巨大差异，使得识别更加困难。本文提出了一种在线手势识别系统，能够在实时视频流中定位手势并识别这些手势的含义。为了提升系统的鲁棒性，采用滑动窗口方法对多个窗口的结果进行细化。本项目中的所有模型均在Jester数据集上训练，检测器准确率达到98%以上，分类器准确率达到90%以上。在系统整体性能方面，最佳分组可在三秒内响应，并在自制数据集上达到37.5%的Levenshtein准确率。本工作中使用的项目代码已公开提供。

</details>


### [39] [DFSAttn: Dynamic Fine-grained Sparse Attention for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23445)
*Jie Hu,Zixiang Gao,Yutong He,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DFSAttn，一种无需训练的稀疏注意力框架，通过希尔伯特曲线令牌重排序、分层块评分和稀疏掩码缓存等技术，在高稀疏率下实现动态细粒度稀疏化，在视频生成扩散变压器中取得最高2.1倍加速且不损失生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散变压器（DiTs）依赖时空3D全注意力机制，其二次复杂度带来巨大计算成本。现有块稀疏注意力方法无法有效处理DiTs中注意力图固有的动态且细粒度的稀疏性，在高稀疏率下质量严重下降。

Method: DFSAttn包含三个核心设计：(1) 基于希尔伯特曲线的令牌重排序，实现细粒度稀疏性同时保持高效的GPU执行；(2) 分层块评分，准确估计块重要性；(3) 稀疏掩码缓存与自适应比率，平衡精度与效率。

Result: 在高稀疏率下，DFSAttn始终优于现有方法，实现高达2.1倍的端到端加速，同时保持高生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实践设计，DFSAttn证明了无需重新训练即可在扩散变压器中实现高效动态细粒度稀疏注意力，为视频生成模型的加速提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Diffusion transformers have achieved remarkable success in high-quality video generation, yet their reliance on spatiotemporal 3D full attention incurs prohibitive computational cost due to the quadratic complexity of attention. Block sparse attention is a common approach to mitigate this by focusing computation on important regions. However, attention maps in DiTs exhibit inherently dynamic and fine-grained sparsity, which causes existing block sparse attention methods to degrade significantly in quality, especially at high sparsity ratios. In this paper, we revisit block sparse attention and derive a theoretical lower bound on attention recall to characterize the key factors governing its effectiveness. Guided by these insights, we propose DFSAttn, a training-free sparse attention framework that enables dynamic, fine-grained sparsification efficiently. DFSAttn incorporates three core designs: Hilbert curve-based token reordering to achieve fine-grained sparsity while preserving efficient GPU execution, hierarchical block scoring for accurate block importance estimation, and sparse mask caching with adaptive ratios to balance accuracy and efficiency. Experimental results demonstrate that DFSAttn consistently outperforms prior methods under high sparsity, achieving up to 2.1$\times$ end-to-end speedup while maintaining high generation quality. Our code is open-sourced and available at https://github.com/jessica-hujie/DFSAttn.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散变换器在高品质视频生成中取得了显著成功，然而其对时空三维全注意力的依赖由于注意力的二次复杂度带来了高昂的计算成本。块稀疏注意力是通过将计算集中于重要区域来缓解这一问题的常见方法。然而，DiTs中的注意力图表现出固有的动态且细粒度的稀疏性，这导致现有的块稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏率时质量显著下降。本文重新审视了块稀疏注意力，推导了注意力召回率的理论下界，以刻画影响其有效性的关键因素。在这些洞见的指导下，我们提出了DFSAttn，一种无需训练的稀疏注意力框架，能够高效地实现动态、细粒度的稀疏化。DFSAttn包含三个核心设计：基于希尔伯特曲线的令牌重排序，以在保持高效GPU执行的同时实现细粒度稀疏性；分层块评分，以准确估计块的重要性；以及带有自适应比率的稀疏掩码缓存，以平衡精度与效率。实验结果表明，在高稀疏率下，DFSAttn始终优于先前的方法，实现了高达2.1倍的端到端加速，同时保持了高生成质量。我们的代码已开源，可在https://github.com/jessica-hujie/DFSAttn获取。

</details>


### [40] [Efficient One-Step Diffusion Restoration Model with Compact Token Compression and Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23451)
*Bingtian Qiao,Yue Shi,Yingjie Zhou,Yong Guo,Guangtao Zhai,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-SR提出了一种高效的图像超分辨率框架，通过32倍压缩的自编码器降低潜在token数量，结合线性注意力DiT和LoRA微调，实现线性复杂度的超分辨率重建，在保持高质量的同时显著降低计算量和延迟，适用于移动端部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成式Real-ISR方法继承高分辨率图像合成的密集潜在表示和二次复杂度全局建模范式，导致计算量、内存和推理延迟随分辨率增加而急剧增长，限制了实际部署。作者认为关键瓶颈不在于恢复先验不足，而在于高分辨率恢复过程中token冗余过多和token交互成本过高。

Method: 提出SANA-SR框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 32倍压缩比的深度压缩自编码器，大幅减少潜在token数量同时保留与恢复相关的结构和纹理；(2) 在紧凑潜在空间上引入线性注意力DiT（Linear-Attention Diffusion Transformer），结合LoRA微调，实现线性复杂度的token混合，支持高效高分辨率恢复。

Result: 在全部基准数据集上，SANA-SR取得了与现有方法高度竞争甚至更优的量化性能，并恢复了更清晰、更真实的纹理。剪枝后的模型在0.019秒内完成推理，MACs为407.95G，参数量为344M，展示了强大的移动端部署潜力。

Conclusion: 通过紧凑潜在表示和线性复杂度建模，SANA-SR有效解决了高分辨率图像超分辨率中的计算效率瓶颈，在保持甚至超越现有方法质量的同时实现了高效的移动端部署能力。

Original Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution aims to recover high-quality images from complex and unknown real-world degradations. However, existing generative Real-ISR methods largely inherit the dense latent representations and quadratic-cost global modeling paradigm developed for high-resolution image synthesis, causing computation, memory usage, and inference latency to scale unfavorably with resolution and thus limiting practical deployment. We argue that the key bottleneck lies not in insufficient restoration priors, but in excessive token redundancy and costly token interactions during high-resolution restoration. Motivated by this observation, we revisit Real-ISR from the perspectives of compact latent representation and linear-complexity modeling, and propose SANA-SR, an efficient one-step restoration framework. Specifically, SANA-SR employs a deep compression autoencoder with a 32x compression ratio to drastically reduce latent tokens while preserving restoration-relevant structures and textures. On top of this compact latent space, we introduce a linear-attention DiT with LoRA fine-tuning, enabling efficient high-resolution restoration with linear-complexity token mixing. Extensive experiments on all benchmark datasets demonstrate that SANA-SR achieves highly competitive and often superior quantitative performance against existing methods, while restoring clearer and more realistic textures. Moreover, after pruning, the deployed model runs in 0.019s with 407.95G MACs and 344M parameters, highlighting its strong potential for practical mobile deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界图像超分辨率旨在从复杂且未知的真实退化中恢复高质量图像。然而，现有的生成式Real-ISR方法很大程度上继承了为高分辨率图像合成而开发的密集潜在表示和二次复杂度全局建模范式，导致计算量、内存消耗和推理延迟随分辨率增大而急剧上升，从而限制了实际部署。我们认为，关键瓶颈不在于恢复先验的不足，而在于高分辨率恢复过程中过度的token冗余和高成本的token交互。受此观察启发，我们从紧凑潜在表示和线性复杂度建模的角度重新审视Real-ISR，提出了SANA-SR——一种高效的单步恢复框架。具体而言，SANA-SR采用具有32倍压缩比的深度压缩自编码器，在保留与恢复相关的结构和纹理的同时大幅减少潜在token数量。在此紧凑潜在空间之上，我们引入了带有LoRA微调的线性注意力DiT，实现了线性复杂度的token混合，从而支持高效的高分辨率恢复。在全部基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SANA-SR在量化性能上与现有方法具有高度竞争力且通常表现更优，同时恢复出更清晰、更逼真的纹理。此外，剪枝后的模型运行时间为0.019秒，MACs为407.95G，参数量为344M，凸显了其在实际移动端部署中的强大潜力。

</details>


### [41] [One-Forcing: Towards Stable One-Step Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23458)
*Jiaqi Feng,Justin Cui,Yuanhao Ban,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出One-Forcing方法，通过向DMD目标中添加辅助GAN损失，实现高质量、高效率的单步自回归视频生成，在VBench上取得83.76分的SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少步自回归视频生成方法（如4步采样）仍存在较大延迟，进一步减少采样步数（尤其是单步设置）会导致严重质量下降：轨迹式一致性蒸馏方法生成的视频动态性弱，而DMD类方法（如Self-Forcing）易产生模糊帧。需要一种既能保持高质量又能实现单步高效生成的方法。

Method: 提出One-Forcing方法，在DMD目标函数基础上增加辅助GAN损失，联合优化以实现高质量的单步视频生成。

Result: 在VBench上总评分达83.76，在单步因果视频生成方法中取得SOTA性能，且与强多步方法具有竞争力；仅需chunkwise模型1/3的训练成本即可稳定实现单步逐帧自回归生成，而此前方法无法成功实现。

Conclusion: One-Forcing通过简单的GAN损失增强DMD目标，能够高效稳定地实现高质量单步自回归视频生成，大幅降低训练成本和推理延迟，为实时交互式视频生成提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Recent advances have substantially improved real-time interactive video generation in the autoregressive regime. However, most existing few-step autoregressive video generation methods, often distilled from a corresponding many-step teacher, default to a 4-step sampling configuration, which still incurs considerable latency during deployment and suffers from severe quality degradation when the number of sampling steps is further reduced, particularly in the one-step setting. Trajectory-style consistency distillation methods often produce videos with weak dynamics, while DMD-based approaches, such as Self-Forcing, tend to yield blurry frames. To address this challenge, we propose One-Forcing, a simple yet effective approach which augments the DMD objective with an auxiliary GAN loss for high-quality and efficient one-step video generation. Experiments on VBench show that One-Forcing achieves a total score of 83.76, establishing state-of-the-art performance among one-step causal video generation methods and remaining competitive with strong many-step approaches. We further demonstrate that one-step framewise autoregressive generation can be achieved stably with merely one-third of the training cost of the chunkwise model, a setting that prior methods have failed to achieve successfully.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，自回归机制下的实时交互式视频生成取得了显著进展。然而，大多数现有的少步自回归视频生成方法通常从对应的多步教师模型中蒸馏而来，默认采用4步采样配置，这在实际部署中仍存在较大延迟，且当进一步减少采样步数（特别是单步设置）时，会出现严重的质量下降。轨迹式一致性蒸馏方法往往生成动态性较弱的视频，而基于DMD的方法（如Self-Forcing）则容易产生模糊帧。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了One-Forcing——一种简单而有效的方法，通过向DMD目标中引入辅助GAN损失，实现高质量、高效的单步视频生成。在VBench上的实验表明，One-Forcing取得了83.76的总分，在单步因果视频生成方法中达到了最优性能，并与强大的多步方法保持竞争力。我们进一步证明，仅需chunkwise模型三分之一训练成本，即可稳定实现单步逐帧自回归生成，而此前的方法未能成功实现这一设置。

</details>


### [42] [PhenoYieldNet: Learning Crop-Aware Phenological Responses for Multi-Crop Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23478)
*Yu Luo,Xiaogang Zhu,Shan Zeng,Wei Xiang,Thomas Francis Bishop,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhenoYieldNet，一种通过显式建模作物物候对时序驱动因素的响应来实现多作物产量预测的框架，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有作物产量预测方法主要针对单作物设计，难以泛化到多种作物类型，且未考虑作物物候响应随复杂天气模式动态变化的问题。

Method: 提出PhenoYieldNet框架，包含作物感知时序解码器（由作物物候库CPB和作物物候注意力CPA模块组成）；CPB集成可学习嵌入，通过查询引导CPA学习特定作物的物候模式；CPA显式捕获多尺度趋势和变化分量构建时序上下文；编码器通过预训练基础模型初始化，并采用时序对比自适应策略进行自监督微调。

Result: 在多作物数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，在不同区域和作物上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模作物物候响应及时序动态，PhenoYieldNet有效实现了多作物产量的准确预测和跨区域跨作物的强泛化。

Original Abstract: Accurate crop yield prediction is crucial for sustainable agriculture and global food security. While existing methods are predominantly developed for single-crop prediction, they often struggle to generalize across diverse crop types, without addressing the unique crop phenological responses that are dynamically modulated by complex weather patterns. In this paper, we propose PhenoYieldNet, a multi-crop yield prediction framework that learns crop-specific phenology by explicitly modeling their responses with temporal drivers. Specifically, we develop a crop-aware temporal decoder consisting of a Crop Phenology Bank (CPB) and a Crop Phenology Attention (CPA) module. The CPB integrates a set of learnable embeddings, which leverage a query to guide the CPA module to learn the most relevant phenology patterns for the specific crop. And the CPA module explicitly captures multi-scale trend and variation components to construct temporal contexts, enabling the model to dynamically adjust the attention across different phenological stages. To learn robust and generalizable features for multi-crop prediction, the encoder is initialized with a pre-trained foundation model, and further adapted via a self-supervised Temporal Contrastive Adaptation strategy to align with agricultural temporal dynamics. Extensive experiments conducted on multi-crop datasets indicate that our proposed method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, exhibiting strong generalization capabilities across different regions and crops.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的作物产量预测对于可持续农业和全球粮食安全至关重要。现有方法主要针对单作物预测开发，往往难以泛化到多种作物类型，且未能解决作物物候响应随复杂天气模式动态变化的问题。本文提出PhenoYieldNet，一种多作物产量预测框架，通过学习作物特异性物候，显式建模其对时序驱动因素的响应。具体而言，我们开发了一个作物感知时序解码器，由作物物候库（CPB）和作物物候注意力（CPA）模块组成。CPB集成了一组可学习嵌入，利用查询引导CPA模块学习特定作物最相关的物候模式。CPA模块显式捕获多尺度趋势和变化分量以构建时序上下文，使模型能够动态调整对不同物候阶段的注意力。为学习鲁棒且可泛化的多作物预测特征，编码器使用预训练基础模型初始化，并通过自监督时序对比自适应策略进一步微调，以对齐农业时序动态。在多作物数据集上进行的大量实验表明，所提出的方法显著优于现有最先进方法，在不同区域和作物上展现出强泛化能力。

</details>


### [43] [Multimodal Distribution Matching for Vision-Language Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23482)
*Jongoh Jeong,Hoyong Kwon,Minseok Kim,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MDM框架，通过数据级聚类初始化、模型级混合教师插值和损失级几何感知匹配，实现高效且跨架构泛化的多模态数据集蒸馏。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在多模态数据集蒸馏中计算开销大，且忽视了视觉-语言之间的相关性，难以在有限计算和内存预算下保持表示质量和跨模态对齐。

Method: MDM在三个层面融合互补组件：(1) 数据级：在联合嵌入空间中对聚类采样以初始化合成图像-文本对；(2) 模型级：根据模型与预训练锚点的角度偏差，在权重空间中对独立微调的模型进行插值形成混合教师；(3) 损失级：利用跨模态一致与差异方向的联合特征，结合对称对比学习，在单位超球面上进行几何感知的联合分布匹配。

Result: 在图像-文本检索基准上的跨架构评估中，MDM生成的紧凑合成集能够保留多模态语义，大幅降低蒸馏成本，并且在多种架构上保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MDM是一种几何感知的多模态蒸馏框架，通过数据、模型和损失三个层面的协同设计，实现了高效、可泛化且跨架构鲁棒的多模态数据集压缩。

Original Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses large training sets into compact synthetic datasets while preserving downstream performance. As modern systems increasingly operate on paired vision-language inputs, multimodal distillation must preserve representation quality and cross-modal alignment under tight compute and memory budgets, yet prior methods often require heavy computes and overlook their correlations. To address this, we present Multimodal Distribution Matching (MDM), a geometry-aware framework for efficient and generalizable multimodal distillation. Specifically, MDM integrates complementary components at the data, model, and loss levels. At the data level, it initializes synthetic image-text pairs by sampling from clusters in the joint embedding space. At the model level, it forms a mixed teacher by interpolating independently fine-tuned models in weight space according to their angular deviation from the pretrained anchor. At the loss level, it matches joint distributions on the unit hypersphere using a geometry-aware matching objective that exploits the joint features in the cross-modal agreement and discrepancy directions along with symmetric contrastive learning. Across image-text retrieval benchmarks with cross-architecture evaluation, MDM yields compact synthetic sets that preserve multimodal semantics, substantially reduce distillation cost, and remain robust across architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集蒸馏将大规模训练集压缩为紧凑的合成数据集，同时保持下游任务性能。随着现代系统越来越多地处理成对的视觉-语言输入，多模态蒸馏必须在有限的计算和内存预算下保持表示质量和跨模态对齐，然而现有方法通常需要大量计算并且忽视了它们之间的相关性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了多模态分布匹配（MDM），一个几何感知框架，用于高效且可泛化的多模态蒸馏。具体而言，MDM在数据、模型和损失三个层面集成了互补组件。在数据层面，它通过在联合嵌入空间中对聚类进行采样来初始化合成图像-文本对。在模型层面，它根据独立微调模型与预训练锚点之间的角度偏差，在权重空间中对这些模型进行插值，形成混合教师模型。在损失层面，它利用跨模态一致和差异方向上的联合特征，结合对称对比学习，在单位超球面上进行几何感知的联合分布匹配。在图像-文本检索基准上的跨架构评估中，MDM生成的紧凑合成集能够保留多模态语义，大幅降低蒸馏成本，并且在不同架构下保持鲁棒性。

</details>


### [44] [B-GRTO: Bootstrapped Group Relative Tool Optimization for Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23500)
*Mario Markov,Stefan Maria Ailuro,Mohammad Mahdi,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出群相对工具优化（GRTO）框架，将强化学习与可微工具目标联合优化，用于复杂指代分割任务。通过复用GRPO的rollout优化辅助工具目标，并推出B-GRTO预训练方法，在三个指代分割基准上显著超越标准GRPO，达到或超越领域最优方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有复杂指代分割方法通常将大视觉语言模型与分割解码器结合，但分割解码器等可微工具通常用独立可微目标单独优化，缺乏将其原则性地整合到强化学习框架中的方法。

Method: 提出GRTO框架，复用GRPO rollout来优化辅助工具目标，使解码器梯度与策略奖励互补；进一步提出B-GRTO预训练方法，廉价地引导工具训练，加速收敛并提升性能。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的指代分割设置中，B-GRTO相比标准GRPO有显著提升，匹配或超越了领域专用最优方法。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与可微辅助目标统一用于推理密集型分割任务具有重要价值，B-GRTO验证了该方向的有效性。

Original Abstract: Segmentation is a fundamental task in computer vision, underpinning pixel-level scene understanding and serving as a cornerstone for applications ranging from autonomous perception to medical image analysis. For complex referring segmentation, recent methods pair large vision-language models with segmentation decoders: the former analyzes the image and prompt, while the latter predicts the target mask. Although reinforcement learning improves reasoning-intensive vision-language systems, trainable tools such as segmentation decoders are typically optimized separately with differentiable objectives, and the principled integration of such objectives into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. Thus, we introduce group relative tool optimization (GRTO), a mathematically grounded framework for jointly optimizing a policy with differentiable tool use. GRTO reuses group relative policy optimization (GRPO) rollouts to optimize the auxiliary tool objective, letting decoder gradients complement policy rewards. Further, we derive Bootstrapped-GRTO (B-GRTO), a pre-training method that cheaply bootstraps the tool, leading to faster convergence and superior performance. Across three challenging referring segmentation settings, B-GRTO results in substantial improvements over plain GRPO, matching or surpassing domain-specific state-of-the-art methods. This demonstrates the value of unifying reinforcement learning with differentiable auxiliary objectives for reasoning-intensive segmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 分割是计算机视觉中的一项基础任务，支撑着像素级场景理解，并作为从自主感知到医学图像分析等应用的基石。对于复杂的指代分割，近期方法将大视觉语言模型与分割解码器配对：前者分析图像和提示，后者预测目标掩码。尽管强化学习改进了推理密集的视觉语言系统，但分割解码器等可训练工具通常使用可微目标单独优化，将这些目标原则性地整合到强化学习中仍未被充分探索。因此，我们引入群相对工具优化（GRTO），一个数学上坚实的框架，用于联合优化策略与可微工具的使用。GRTO复用群相对策略优化（GRPO）的rollout来优化辅助工具目标，使解码器梯度与策略奖励互补。进一步，我们推导出引导式GRTO（B-GRTO），一种低成本引导工具的预训练方法，导致更快的收敛和更优的性能。在三个具有挑战性的指代分割设置中，B-GRTO相比普通GRPO带来了实质性改进，匹配或超越了领域专用最优方法。这证明了将强化学习与可微辅助目标统一用于推理密集型分割任务的价值。

</details>


### [45] [VINS-120K: Ultra High-Resolution Image Editing with A Large-Scale Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23518)
*Zhizhou Chen,Shanyan Guan,Zhanxin Gao,En Ci,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Zhenyu Zhang,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向超高清（≥4K）图像指令编辑的大规模数据集VINS-120K，并基于此开发了高频感知后适应策略，使预训练的非高分辨率模型能够扩展到UHR领域，显著提升了细粒度细节合成与纹理真实感。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 超高清（UHR）图像的直接编辑具有重要价值但尚未被充分探索，主要面临两大挑战：缺乏高质量的UHR编辑数据集，以及难以建模高频纹理细节。

Method: (1) 构建VINS-120K数据集：包含12万个精心筛选的“指令-输入图像-编辑后图像”三元组，每张图像分辨率≥4K，经过多阶段严格筛选确保视觉质量与指令对齐；(2) 提出高频感知后适应策略：将预训练的非高分辨率模型扩展到UHR领域；(3) 建立VINS-4KEval基准：覆盖多种编辑类型，用于UHR场景下的标准化评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在超高清图像编辑中显著提升了细粒度细节合成效果和纹理真实感。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建大规模UHR编辑数据集和提出高频感知适应策略，有效解决了超高清图像编辑中的高频纹理建模难题，推动了该领域的发展。

Original Abstract: Directly editing ultra-high-resolution (UHR) images is valuable but underexplored, primarily due to the lack of high-quality data and the challenge in modeling high-frequency texture details. We introduce VINS-120K, the first large-scale dataset for instruction-based UHR image editing, comprising 120K carefully curated triplets of instruction, input image, and edited image. Each image exceeds 4K resolution ($\geq$4096 $\times$ 4096) and is filtered through a rigorous multi-stage pipeline to ensure visual quality, instruction alignment, and aesthetic fidelity. Built on VINS-120K, we further develop a high-frequency-aware post-adaptation strategy to extend pretrained non-high-resolution models to the UHR regime. We also present VINS-4KEval, a benchmark covering diverse editing types, to facilitate consistent evaluation in UHR settings. Experiments confirm that our work improves fine-grained detail synthesis and texture realism in UHR image editing.

中文摘要翻译: 直接编辑超高清（UHR）图像具有重要价值但尚未被充分探索，主要原因在于缺乏高质量数据以及高频纹理细节建模困难。本文提出了VINS-120K——首个面向指令式超高清图像编辑的大规模数据集，包含12万个经过精心筛选的“指令-输入图像-编辑后图像”三元组。每张图像分辨率超过4K（≥4096×4096），并通过严格的多阶段流水线进行筛选，以确保视觉质量、指令对齐度以及美学保真度。基于VINS-120K，我们进一步提出了一种高频感知后适应策略，将预训练的非高分辨率模型扩展到超高清领域。我们还提出了VINS-4KEval基准，涵盖多种编辑类型，以促进超高清场景下的统一评估。实验表明，我们的工作在超高清图像编辑中显著提升了细粒度细节合成效果与纹理真实感。

</details>


### [46] [ComPose: When to Trust Hands for Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23523)
*Jisu Shin,Junoh Lee,JunGyu Lee,Inhwan Bae,Dohyeon Lee,Hokyun Im,Youngwoon Lee,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ComPose，一种从RGB视频中利用手部运动作为互补线索进行6DoF物体姿态跟踪的框架，无需深度数据或3D模板，在手部严重遮挡下仍能保持鲁棒。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物体姿态追踪方法严重依赖深度数据或3D模板等外部先验信息，且在手部抓取造成的严重遮挡下仍然脆弱，未能有效利用手部运动信息作为辅助线索。

Method: 提出ComPose框架，结合基础模型中的物体和手部线索，在统一追踪流程中自适应选择信息丰富的手部关节点，融合物体和手部线索进行运动估计，并通过可见几何证据和学习到的修正项来优化物体运动，同时对旋转和平移施加时间一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明该方法在手部严重遮挡和几何模糊条件下准确、高效且鲁棒；生成的轨迹可有效迁移到下游机器人操作任务，使机器人能够从在线视频中重建人类动作。

Conclusion: 将手部运动视为互补线索而非纯粹的遮挡物，能够显著提升物体姿态追踪的性能和鲁棒性，且生成的轨迹对机器人模仿学习具有实用价值。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing the motion of objects from videos is a key component for embodied AI and robot manipulation. While diverse approaches to object pose tracking have been studied, they rely heavily on strong external priors, such as depth data or 3D templates, and remain highly vulnerable to severe occlusions by hand grasps despite the use of explicit masks. In this work, we present ComPose, a 6DoF object tracking framework designed for hand-aware object pose estimation from RGB video. Rather than treating the hand purely as an occluder, our method harmonizes hand motions as a \textit{complementary cue} for object tracking. In detail, we recover a variety of object motions over time by combining object and hand cues from foundation models within a unified tracking pipeline. Here, ComPose adaptively selects informative hand joints, combines object- and hand-derived cues for motion estimation, and refines the resulting object motion using visible geometric evidence and a learned correction. We further enforce the temporal consistency over both rotation and translation, yielding stable 3D object trajectories over time without any external smoothing. Extensive experiments show that our method is accurate, efficient, and robust under severe hand occlusion and geometric ambiguity. In addition, the resulting trajectories can also effectively transfer to downstream robot manipulation by enabling robots to reconstruct human actions from online videos.

中文摘要翻译: 从视频中重建物体的运动是具身AI和机器人操作的关键组成部分。尽管已有多种物体姿态追踪方法被研究，但它们严重依赖于强大的外部先验，如深度数据或3D模板，并且即使使用了显式掩码，在手部抓取造成的严重遮挡下仍然非常脆弱。在这项工作中，我们提出了ComPose，一种面向RGB视频中手部感知的物体6DoF姿态追踪框架。我们的方法不将手部仅仅视为遮挡物，而是将手部运动协调为物体追踪的互补线索。具体而言，我们通过在统一的追踪流程中结合来自基础模型的物体和手部线索，随时间恢复多种物体运动。在此框架下，ComPose自适应地选择信息丰富的手部关节点，融合来源于物体和手部的线索进行运动估计，并利用可见几何证据和学习到的修正项来优化得到的物体运动。我们进一步在旋转和平移上施加时间一致性约束，从而在无需任何外部平滑的情况下生成稳定的3D物体轨迹。大量实验表明，我们的方法在手部严重遮挡和几何模糊条件下准确、高效且鲁棒。此外，生成的轨迹还能够通过使机器人从在线视频中重建人类动作，有效地迁移到下游的机器人操作任务中。

</details>


### [47] [PixIE: Prompted Pixel-Space Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23531)
*Ruirui Lin,Guoxi Huang,David Bull,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PixIE，一种由视觉基础模型语义引导的前馈像素级低光照图像增强框架，通过跨尺度去噪、DINO特征引导的像素调制和高效像素注意力机制，在多个指标上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低光照图像存在严重噪声、对比度损失和语义模糊，增强需要同时解决去噪和细节恢复，现有方法难以兼顾两者。

Method: 1) 跨尺度去噪（Cross-scale Denoising）抑制噪声并保持结构；2) DINO-Prompted Pixel Blocks (DPPB) 利用DINOv3中间特征通过逐像素调制注入语义信息；3) Spatial-Channel Compaction (SCC) 将特征折叠为紧凑空间网格并在通道维度压缩，实现跨尺度的高效像素注意力计算；4) Multi-Receptive-Field Pixel Embedding (MRPE) 提供邻域感知的像素表示以增强对信号相关噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在LLIE基准上，PixIE相比最新SOTA方法PSNR提升1.9-15.0%，LPIPS降低8.5-44.4%，能恢复更清晰的细节和更稳定的纹理。

Conclusion: PixIE通过语义引导的像素级增强框架，在去噪与细节恢复上取得显著改进，实现了更高的重建保真度和感知质量。

Original Abstract: Low-light images exhibit severe noise, contrast loss, and semantic ambiguity, making enhancement a joint problem of denoising and detail recovery. We propose PixIE, a feed-forward pixel-space LLIE framework semantically-prompted by a vision foundation model. PixIE first performs a cross-scale denoising to suppress noise and preserve structure, then refines details with DINO-Prompted Pixel Blocks (DPPB) that inject intermediate DINOv3 features via patch-conditioned, spatially continuous per-pixel modulation. We introduce a Spatial-Channel Compaction (SCC), which folds features into a compact spatial grid and compresses in the channel dimension, so pixel-attention is computed efficiently with bounded cost across scales. We further propose Multi-Receptive-Field Pixel Embedding (MRPE) to provide neighborhood-aware pixel representations before semantic prompting, improving robustness to signal-dependent noise beyond point-wise embeddings. Experiments on LLIE benchmarks show that PixIE improves the average PSNR by 1.9-15.0% over recent state-of-the-art methods and reduces LPIPS by 8.5-44.4%. Qualitative comparisons further demonstrate that PixIE recovers sharper details and more stable textures, resulting in improved reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

中文摘要翻译: 低光照图像存在严重噪声、对比度损失和语义模糊，使得增强成为一个去噪与细节恢复的联合问题。本文提出PixIE，一种由视觉基础模型进行语义提示的前馈像素级低光照图像增强框架。PixIE首先执行跨尺度去噪以抑制噪声并保持结构，然后通过DINO提示像素块（DPPB）细化细节，该模块通过逐块条件化的、空间连续的逐像素调制注入DINOv3的中间特征。我们引入空间-通道压缩（SCC），将特征折叠为紧凑的空间网格并在通道维度进行压缩，从而以有界的计算成本实现跨尺度的像素注意力高效计算。我们进一步提出多感受野像素嵌入（MRPE），在语义提示之前提供邻域感知的像素表示，增强了模型对信号相关噪声的鲁棒性，优于逐点嵌入方法。在低光照图像增强基准上的实验表明，PixIE相比近期最先进方法，平均PSNR提升了1.9-15.0%，LPIPS降低了8.5-44.4%。定性比较进一步证明，PixIE能恢复更清晰的细节和更稳定的纹理，从而实现了更优的重建保真度和感知质量。

</details>


### [48] [Generator-Refiner-Examiner: A Tri-Module Data Augmentation Framework for 3D Human Avatar Learning from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23555)
*Gangjian Zhang,Jian Shu,Sicheng Yu,Wenhao Shen,Yu Feng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrioMan提出了一种三模块框架，通过数据增强（生成器）、质量优化（精炼器）和样本筛选（检查器）从单目视频中重建高保真且可动画化的3D人体化身，在数据稀缺场景下优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖每主体优化与通用人体先验，但在训练帧数有限时难以捕捉精细细节，旨在缓解数据稀缺问题。

Method: TrioMan包含三个协同模块：生成器通过姿态和相机的高斯扰动生成多样未见样本；精炼器利用纹理和几何线索引导的一步扩散提升生成数据质量；检查器使用双分支注意力相似度评估选择主体一致样本。

Result: 在X-Humans和NeuMan基准上，TrioMan的性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: TrioMan通过系统性的三模块数据增强框架有效解决了单目视频中3D人体化身重建的数据稀缺问题，显著提升了重建质量。

Original Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of reconstructing photorealistic and animatable 3D human avatars from monocular videos. While existing methods rely on combining per-subject optimization with generic human priors, they often fail to capture fine-grained details when training frames are limited. To mitigate this data scarcity, we propose TrioMan, a systematic tri-module framework for augmented 3D avatar learning. Our approach comprises three synergistic components. The Generator creates diverse unseen samples by imposing Gaussian perturbations on pose and camera. The Refiner improves the quality of generated data through one-step diffusion guided by texture and geometry cues. The Examiner selects subject-consistent samples using a dual-branch attention-based similarity evaluation. Experiments on the X-Humans and NeuMan benchmarks show that TrioMan outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对从单目视频中重建高保真且可动画化的3D人体化身这一挑战。现有方法依赖每主体优化与通用人体先验的结合，但在训练帧数有限时往往无法捕捉精细细节。为缓解数据稀缺问题，我们提出TrioMan，一个用于增强式3D化身学习的系统性三模块框架。我们的方法包含三个协同组件：生成器通过对姿态和相机施加高斯扰动来生成多样的未见样本；精炼器通过纹理和几何线索引导的一步扩散提升生成数据质量；检查器使用基于双分支注意力的相似度评估来选择与主体一致的样本。在X-Humans和NeuMan基准上的实验表明，TrioMan优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [49] [PathNavigate: A Training-Free Pathology Agent with Surprise-Guided Scan and Shared Slide Memory for Whole-Slide Image VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23559)
*Chunze Yang,Qidong Liu,Wenjie Zhao,Yue Tang,Jiusong Ge,Di Zhang,Jiashuai Liu,Lei Wu,Junbo Lu,Ni Zhang,Xian Wu,Zeyu Gao,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathNavigate提出了一种无需训练的病理学智能体，通过“扫描-搜索-读出”流程，在冻结核模型上利用共享在线记忆模块先扫描全切片生成异常区域池，再结合问题相关匹配进行高效搜索与答案生成，在WSI-VQA任务上提升了准确率和可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有WSI-VQA方法存在两难：监督式病理多模态大模型虽能联合定位与推理，但依赖任务特定监督和重训练，实用性受限；无需训练的病理智能体虽免去训练成本，但采用“问题优先”设计，仅基于查询相关性构建候选集，容易遗漏问题未明确提及的关键形态学证据，导致推理开销增加。

Method: 提出PathNavigate，一种无需训练的病理智能体，包含三个步骤：1) 扫描阶段：在低倍率下利用共享在线记忆模块对冻结核病理特征进行全切片扫描，生成“惊喜场”（surprise field），标记异常区域池；2) 搜索阶段：仅在异常区域池内应用问题条件的PLIP相关性匹配，选择高倍率搜索目标；3) 读出阶段：提取局部高倍率证据，使用冻结的感知器-裁决器堆栈（perceptor-adjudicator stack）回答问题，并将同一在线记忆作为切片级上下文。

Result: 在WSI-VQA和SlideBench-BCNB基准上的实验表明，所提出的扫描-搜索-读出设计能够提高答案准确率，生成更可解释的证据选择轨迹，并具有更高的效率。

Conclusion: PathNavigate通过将全局扫描与问题条件搜索解耦，在无需额外训练的情况下实现了更好的WSI-VQA性能，兼顾了准确率、可解释性和效率。

Original Abstract: Whole-slide image visual question answering (WSI-VQA) frames pathology as an extreme-context search problem: to answer a free-form clinical query, a system must first navigate a gigapixel slide under a strict inspection budget to locate sparse, high-resolution evidence. Existing approaches largely fall into two paradigms: i) supervised pathology multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and agents can absorb localization and reasoning into learned modules, but they often couple navigation to task-specific supervision and retraining, limiting their practicality; ii) training-free pathology agents avoid this cost by keeping core models frozen, but often follow a question-first design, constructing the initial candidate set mainly from query-conditioned relevance. This can miss decisive morphology that is not named in the question, and force heavier inference-time scaffolding. To address this challenge, we introduce PathNavigate, a training-free pathology agent built around a scan-search-readout routine. Before question matching, PathNavigate scans the current slide at low magnification with a shared online memory module over frozen pathology features, producing a slide-specific surprise field that marks an abnormal-region pool. It then applies question-conditioned PLIP relevance only within this pool to select high-magnification search targets. Finally, it extracts local high-magnification evidence and answers with a frozen perceptor-adjudicator stack, using the same online memory as slide-level context. Experiments on WSI-VQA and SlideBench-BCNB show that the proposed scan-search-readout design improves answer accuracy and yields more interpretable evidence-selection trajectories with higher efficiency.The code is available online.

中文摘要翻译: 全切片图像视觉问答（WSI-VQA）将病理学建模为一个极端上下文搜索问题：要回答一个自由形式的临床查询，系统必须首先在严格的检查预算下导航千兆像素级别的切片，以定位稀疏的高分辨率证据。现有方法主要分为两类：i) 监督式病理多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）和智能体可以将定位与推理吸收到学习模块中，但它们通常将导航与任务特定监督和重训练耦合，限制了其实用性；ii) 无需训练的病理智能体通过保持核心模型冻结来避免这一成本，但通常采用问题优先的设计，主要基于查询条件相关性构建初始候选集。这可能会遗漏问题中未明确命名的决定性形态学特征，并迫使进行更重的推理时脚手架构建。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了PathNavigate，这是一个无需训练的病理智能体，围绕扫描-搜索-读出流程构建。在问题匹配之前，PathNavigate在低倍率下利用共享在线记忆模块对冻结核病理特征进行当前切片扫描，生成一个切片特定的惊喜场（surprise field），标记异常区域池。然后，它仅在该池内应用问题条件的PLIP相关性匹配，以选择高倍率搜索目标。最后，它提取局部高倍率证据，并使用冻结的感知器-裁决器堆栈（perceptor-adjudicator stack）回答问题，将同一在线记忆用作切片级上下文。在WSI-VQA和SlideBench-BCNB上的实验表明，所提出的扫描-搜索-读出设计提高了答案准确率，并生成了更可解释且效率更高的证据选择轨迹。代码已在线公开。

</details>


### [50] [Calibration-Informative Region Selection for Online LiDAR--Camera Calibration in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23580)
*Rajitha de Silva,Grzegorz Cielniak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于支持图的非均匀多模态标定方法，通过聚合跨模态一致性来识别可靠观测，提升LiDAR-相机标定的平移精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态标定中，并非所有观测都能有效约束外参，部分观测会引入噪声或歧义，需要区分哪些观测真正提供可靠的标定证据。

Method: 提出支持图驱动的标定框架，分为四个模块：初始标定、跨模态残差提取、支持图估计、支持感知精炼。具体实例化为基于MDPCalib（运动与深度点对应）和CMRNext（稠密LiDAR-相机匹配）的在线标定方法，通过稠密标定支持图聚合对齐观测的跨模态一致性。

Result: 在BLT和KITTI数据集上，标定证据在空间和语义上呈现非均匀分布；在KITTI上，支持图引导的精炼提升了平移精度，但旋转精度提升有限。

Conclusion: 支持图驱动的精炼能够有效改善多模态标定的平移精度，不同语义区域对标定的贡献存在差异，未来可进一步探索旋转维度的优化。

Original Abstract: Reliable multi-modal calibration requires identifying which observations truly constrain the extrinsic parameters and which ones mainly add noise or ambiguity. In this paper, we propose a support-map-driven approach to multi-modal calibration that decouples four functional blocks: initial calibration, cross-modal residual extraction, support-map estimation, and support-aware refinement. We instantiate this formulation for online LiDAR--camera calibration using MDPCalib, a target-less LiDAR--camera calibration method based on motion and deep point correspondences, and CMRNext, a dense LiDAR--camera matching model that predicts optical-flow-like image-plane residuals. The key contribution is a dense calibration support map that aggregates cross-modal agreement over aligned observations and highlights where calibration evidence is consistently reliable. Across the Bacchus Long-Term (BLT) dataset and KITTI, we show that calibration evidence is spatially and semantically non-uniform, indicating that some semantic regions provide stronger cues for calibration than others. On KITTI, support-guided refinement improves the calibration performance with better translation accuracy while rotational gains remain limited.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的多模态标定需要识别哪些观测真正约束外参，哪些观测主要增加噪声或歧义。本文提出一种基于支持图的多模态标定方法，将标定过程解耦为四个功能模块：初始标定、跨模态残差提取、支持图估计和支持感知精炼。我们将该框架实例化为在线LiDAR-相机标定，使用基于运动和深度点对应的无靶标LiDAR-相机标定方法MDPCalib，以及预测光流式图像平面残差的稠密LiDAR-相机匹配模型CMRNext。关键贡献是一种稠密标定支持图，它聚合对齐观测上的跨模态一致性，突出标定证据持续可靠的区域。在Bacchus Long-Term（BLT）数据集和KITTI上的实验表明，标定证据在空间和语义上非均匀分布，即某些语义区域比其他区域提供更强的标定线索。在KITTI上，支持图引导的精炼改善了标定性能，平移精度更高，但旋转增益有限。

</details>


### [51] [GlowGS: Generative Semantic Feature Learning for 3D Gaussian Splatting in Nighttime Glow Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23602)
*Beibei Lin,Xiao Cao,Jingyuan Guo,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlowGS提出利用扩散模型和视觉基础模型为夜间场景的3DGS提供结构先验，通过语义特征生成和新视角语义学习，在缺乏纹理边缘的发光区域实现高质量新视角合成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在白天清晰场景表现良好，但在夜间场景的发光区域由于缺乏纹理和边缘等结构特征（对基于splatting的重建至关重要），导致渲染质量严重下降。需引入额外的结构线索来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出两个核心创新：1) 语义特征生成：利用扩散模型从训练视图合成未知相机位姿的新视角，再由VFM评估质量，对高质量新视角提取鲁棒语义特征构建特征库；2) 新视角语义学习：从渲染的新视角中提取语义特征，在特征库中检索最相似特征并最小化二者距离，从而施加隐式结构约束，无需真实标签即可优化渲染结果。

Result: 大量实验表明GlowGS在生成语义准确的3D视图方面效果显著，相比现有方法有大幅提升。

Conclusion: GlowGS通过引入扩散模型和视觉基础模型提供隐式结构线索，有效解决了夜间场景3DGS发光区域渲染质量差的问题，实现了语义一致、无伪影的新视角合成。

Original Abstract: Existing 3DGS methods effectively render high-quality novel views in clear-day scenes. However, they struggle with night scenes, particularly in glow regions, due to the lack of structural features such as textures and edges, which are key cues for splatting-based reconstruction. To address this problem, we leverage a diffusion model and a Vision Foundation Model (VFM) to compensate for missing structural cues. Our method consists of two key novel ideas: semantic feature generation and novel-view semantic learning. First, semantic feature generation produces high-quality semantic features as implicit structural cues for novel views. Specifically, a diffusion model synthesizes novel views with unknown camera poses from training views, while a VFM evaluates their quality. Once high-quality novel views are identified, the VFM extracts robust features to construct the semantic feature bank. Second, novel-view semantic learning enables 3DGS to optimize rendered novel views without requiring ground truth. It achieves this by extracting semantic features from a rendered novel view, searching the feature bank for the most similar features, and minimizing their distance. This process enforces implicit structural constraints, ensuring semantically coherent, artifact-free rendered views. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our GlowGS in generating semantically accurate 3D views, showing significant improvements over existing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的3DGS方法在晴朗场景下能够有效渲染高质量的新视角，但在夜间场景，尤其是发光区域，由于缺乏纹理和边缘等结构特征（这些特征是基于splatting重建的关键线索），性能显著下降。为解决这一问题，我们利用扩散模型和视觉基础模型来补偿缺失的结构线索。我们的方法包含两个关键创新：语义特征生成和新视角语义学习。首先，语义特征生成为新视角生成高质量的语义特征作为隐式结构线索。具体地，扩散模型从训练视图合成具有未知相机位姿的新视角，而视觉基础模型评估其质量。一旦识别出高质量的新视角，视觉基础模型提取鲁棒特征以构建语义特征库。其次，新视角语义学习使3DGS能够在无需真实标签的情况下优化渲染的新视角。它通过从渲染的新视角中提取语义特征，在特征库中检索最相似的特征，并最小化二者的距离来实现。这一过程施加了隐式结构约束，确保渲染视图语义一致且无伪影。大量实验证明了我们的GlowGS在生成语义准确的3D视图方面的有效性，相较于现有方法有显著提升。

</details>


### [52] [EM-Vid: Training-Free Entity-Centric Memory for Efficient and Consistent Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23610)
*Jente Vandersanden,Matheus Gadelha,Chun-Hao P. Huang,Hyeonho Jeong,Yulia Gryaditskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于实体中心的记忆模块（实体索引的潜在补丁库），结合稀疏令牌条件化、预算化记忆更新和噪声注入机制，实现多镜头视频生成中跨镜头的主体一致性和文本提示忠实性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归方法将完整生成帧作为记忆存储，导致持久实体信息与瞬态场景上下文纠缠，造成无关信息泄漏和计算成本高。

Method: 1) 实体中心记忆：以实体索引的潜在补丁库形式存储记忆；2) 稀疏令牌条件化：限制自注意力仅关注实体相关令牌，降低计算成本；3) 结构化多镜头脚本格式；4) 预算化记忆更新策略：维护紧凑且演化的记忆；5) 噪声注入机制：实现细粒度外观控制，防止无关信息泄漏。

Result: 方法在提升文本提示忠实性和计算效率的同时，保持了主体一致性。

Conclusion: 实体中心的记忆机制能有效解耦持久实体信息与瞬态场景上下文，在多镜头视频生成中兼顾主体一致性与提示忠实性。

Original Abstract: Multi-shot video generation requires maintaining a consistent appearance of recurring entities across shots while remaining faithful to shot-specific text prompts. Recent autoregressive methods reuse previously generated frames as memory. However, full-frame storage entangles persistent entity information with transient scene context, leading to irrelevant information leakage and high computational cost. We propose an entity-centric memory in the form of an entity-indexed bank of latent patches. We introduce sparse token conditioning compatible with pretrained models, restricting self-attention to entity-relevant tokens and reducing computational cost. To support this, we introduce a structured multi-shot script format. We additionally propose a budgeted memory update strategy to maintain a compact, evolving memory. Finally, we equip the entity representation with a noise-injection mechanism that enables fine-grained appearance control, preventing leakage of irrelevant information. Our method improves prompt adherence and efficiency while preserving subject consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 多镜头视频生成需要在不同镜头间保持重复出现实体的一致性外观，同时忠实于各镜头特定的文本提示。现有的自回归方法将先前生成的帧作为记忆进行复用。然而，全帧存储将持久实体信息与瞬态场景上下文纠缠在一起，导致无关信息泄漏和高计算成本。我们提出一种实体中心的记忆机制，其形式为实体索引的潜在补丁库。我们引入与预训练模型兼容的稀疏令牌条件化方法，将自注意力限制在实体相关令牌上并降低计算成本。为支持该方法，我们引入了一种结构化的多镜头脚本格式。此外，我们提出一种预算化记忆更新策略，以维护紧凑且不断演化的记忆。最后，我们为实体表示配备噪声注入机制，实现细粒度的外观控制，防止无关信息泄漏。我们的方法在保持主体一致性的同时，提升了提示忠实性和计算效率。

</details>


### [53] [DDX-TRACE: A Benchmark for Medical Diagnostic Trajectories in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23629)
*Jiazhen Pan,Weixiang Shen,Jun Li,Julian Canisius,Felix Bitzer,Paula Roßmüller,Jiancheng Yang,Virginie Kreutzinger,Daniel Rueckert,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DDX-TRACE基准，评估医学AI在多轮诊断推理中的证据获取、影像解读和不确定性更新能力，揭示仅评估最终答案会掩盖工作流缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学AI基准将相关上下文一次性给出并仅评估最终答案，无法检测无证据支撑的猜测、过早停止、低效工作流程以及不确定性更新不良等问题。

Method: 构建包含211个挑战性病例的神经放射学基准DDX-TRACE，采用隐藏证据的序贯诊断范式：模型从有限临床病史开始，自由形式请求影像检查，接收匹配图像，每轮更新概率鉴别诊断，最终给出定位诊断。

Result: 最终诊断分数无法准确反映工作流质量；模型可能在没有关键证据的情况下猜出合理诊断，请求有用检查但误读图像，或低效获取证据且不确定性更新不良。受控证据变体揭示了规划、视觉证据提取和下游鉴别推理中的瓶颈。

Conclusion: DDX-TRACE将医学AI评估从最终答案转向证据支持的诊断轨迹，更全面地衡量模型在真实临床工作流中的表现。

Original Abstract: Medical diagnosis is not a single prediction from a fully specified vignette. It is a sequential workup: clinicians decide what evidence to obtain, revise a differential diagnosis, and stop when the diagnosis is sufficiently supported. Most medical AI benchmarks instead reveal the relevant context upfront and score only the final answer, making unsupported correct guesses, premature closure, inefficient workups, and poor uncertainty updating invisible. We introduce DDX-TRACE, a physician-adjudicated benchmark for multimodal neuroradiology that evaluates diagnostic trajectories under hidden evidence over 211 challenging cases. Each case begins with limited clinical history; models request imaging studies in free form, receive matched image bundles when available, update a probabilistic differential diagnosis after each turn, and stop with a localized final diagnosis. Evaluating state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that final diagnosis scores can substantially misrepresent workup quality: models may guess plausible diagnoses without essential evidence, request useful studies but misinterpret raw images, or acquire evidence inefficiently while updating uncertainty poorly. Controlled evidence variants isolate bottlenecks in planning, visual evidence extraction, and downstream differential reasoning. DDX-TRACE shifts medical AI evaluation from final answers to evidence-supported diagnostic trajectories.

中文摘要翻译: 医学诊断并非基于完整描述的一次性预测，而是一个序贯的检查过程：临床医生决定获取哪些证据，更新鉴别诊断，并在诊断得到充分支持时停止。大多数医学AI基准将相关上下文一次性揭示出来，仅对最终答案进行评分，这使得无证据支撑的正确猜测、过早停止、低效工作流程以及不良的不确定性更新无法被察觉。我们提出DDX-TRACE，一个由医生评判的多模态神经放射学基准，在211个挑战性病例中评估隐藏证据下的诊断轨迹。每个病例从有限的临床病史开始；模型以自由形式请求影像检查，在可用时接收匹配的图像包，每轮后更新概率鉴别诊断，并以定位最终诊断结束。评估最先进的视觉语言模型，我们发现最终诊断分数可能严重误判工作流质量：模型可能在没有关键证据的情况下猜测出合理的诊断，请求有用的检查但误读原始图像，或低效地获取证据同时不良地更新不确定性。受控证据变体隔离了规划、视觉证据提取和下游鉴别推理中的瓶颈。DDX-TRACE将医学AI评估从最终答案转向证据支持的诊断轨迹。

</details>


### [54] [DualMem: Bypassing the Objectness Bottleneck for Calibrated Unknown-Stream Filtering in Open-World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23634)
*Yingjun Xiao,Xi Chen,Gang Fang,Siyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示开放世界目标检测（OWOD）中未知预测流被背景假正例严重污染的问题（背景假正例占46-71%），根源在于目标性头部的信息瓶颈。提出DualMem，一种基于冻结SigLIP特征的非参数后验过滤器，通过K近邻正记忆保护未来任务目标、负记忆抑制背景提案，并采用Neyman-Pearson校准设定决策阈值。在M-OWODB Task 1上，DualMem将每张图像的背景类假未知提案减少44.9%-66.3%（平均56.6%），且不降低已知类mAP。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OWOD检测器中未知预测流被严重污染：未来任务正类未知目标占比不足10%，而背景假正例高达46-71%。作者证明这并非信息缺失问题，而是目标性头部的信息瓶颈——解码器查询的256维特征AUROC达0.908，但压缩为1维目标性标量后降至0.642。冻结的SigLIP特征独立就能恢复多数提案级可分性（AUROC=0.871）。

Method: 提出DualMem，一种校准的后验过滤器。假设存在一个与图像不交叠的已标注校准集（包含持有未来任务目标），在冻结SigLIP特征空间中执行非参数似然比检验：构建K近邻正记忆保护未来任务目标，负记忆抑制背景类提案。采用Neyman-Pearson校准选择决策阈值，显式平衡假未知抑制与新类召回率。已知类检测结果绕过该过滤器，不影响已知类性能。

Result: 在M-OWODB Task 1上，针对PROB、OW-DETR和HypOW三种检测器，DualMem将每张图像的背景类假未知提案减少44.9%-66.3%（平均56.6%）。在PROB Task 1上，效果是自然K-means原型基线的两倍以上，且已知类mAP不变。

Conclusion: 开放世界目标检测中未知预测污染的主要根源在于目标性头部的信息瓶颈，而非信息缺失。基于冻结视觉特征的非参数后验过滤策略能够有效抑制背景假未知提案，显著提升未知预测质量，同时保持已知类检测性能不变，为OWOD提供了一种轻量且有效的校准方案。

Original Abstract: Open-world object detection (OWOD) requires detectors to localize known classes while identifying unknown objects for future incremental learning. We find that the unknown prediction streams of strong OWOD detectors are heavily polluted: on M-OWODB, across PROB, OW-DETR, and HypOW, future-task positive unknowns make up less than 10% of unknown predictions, whereas background false positives account for 46-71%. We show that this is not a missing-information problem, but an information bottleneck at the objectness head. On PROB Task 1, a linear probe on the 256-D decoder query achieves an AUROC of 0.908 for positive-versus-negative unknown discrimination, but the final one-dimensional objectness scalar drops to 0.642. A frozen SigLIP feature, without access to the detector, independently recovers much of this proposal-level separability at the filtering stage (AUROC = 0.871).
  Motivated by this finding, we propose DualMem, a calibrated post-hoc filter that assumes a small image-disjoint annotated calibration split of held-out future-task objects and performs a non-parametric likelihood ratio test in frozen SigLIP feature space. DualMem uses a k-nearest-neighbor positive memory to protect future-task objects and a negative memory to suppress background-like proposals. Its decision threshold is chosen by Neyman-Pearson calibration, giving users an explicit trade-off between false-unknown suppression and novel recall.
  Across PROB, OW-DETR, and HypOW on M-OWODB Task 1, DualMem reduces background-type false unknown proposals per image by 44.9%-66.3%, with a mean reduction of 56.6%. On PROB Task 1, it more than doubles the reduction achieved by a natural K-means prototype baseline, while leaving known-class mAP unchanged because known detections bypass the filter.

中文摘要翻译: 开放世界目标检测（OWOD）要求检测器定位已知类别，同时识别未知目标以供未来增量学习。我们发现，强OWOD检测器的未知预测流受到严重污染：在M-OWODB上，对于PROB、OW-DETR和HypOW，未来任务正类未知目标在未知预测中占比不足10%，而背景假正例占比达46-71%。我们证明这并非信息缺失问题，而是目标性头部的信息瓶颈。在PROB Task 1上，对256维解码器查询进行线性探测，正类与负类未知区分AUROC达0.908，但最终一维目标性标量下降至0.642。无需访问检测器，冻结的SigLIP特征独立就能在过滤阶段恢复大部分提案级可分性（AUROC=0.871）。受此发现启发，我们提出DualMem，一种校准的后验过滤器，它假设存在一个与图像不交叠的已标注校准集（包含持有未来任务目标），并在冻结SigLIP特征空间中执行非参数似然比检验。DualMem使用K近邻正记忆保护未来任务目标，使用负记忆抑制背景类提案。其决策阈值通过Neyman-Pearson校准选择，为用户提供假未知抑制与新类召回之间的显式权衡。在M-OWODB Task 1上，针对PROB、OW-DETR和HypOW，DualMem将每张图像的背景类假未知提案减少44.9%-66.3%，平均减少56.6%。在PROB Task 1上，其效果是自然K-means原型基线的两倍以上，同时由于已知检测结果绕过过滤器，已知类mAP保持不变。

</details>


### [55] [CVSearch: Empowering Multimodal LLMs with Cognitive Visual Search for High-Resolution Image Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23655)
*Liupeng Li,Haoqian Kang,Zhenyu Lu,Jinpeng Wang,Bin Chen,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVSearch提出了一种免训练的自适应框架，通过"评估-然后-搜索"工作流动态调度视觉专家辅助搜索和语义感知扫描策略，实现了高分辨率图像感知中覆盖范围与搜索效率的平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在高分辨率图像感知中面临覆盖范围与效率的权衡：视觉专家辅助搜索效率高但存在盲区，扫描式搜索保证覆盖但计算冗余且语义碎片化严重。

Method: 提出CVSearch框架，包含三个核心组件：1）语义引导自适应分割（Semantic Guided Adaptive Patching），将图像分解为语义一致的区域；2）动态自底向上搜索策略（Dynamic Bottom-Up Search），由视觉复杂度先验驱动；3）评估-然后-搜索（Assess-then-Search）工作流，全局信息不足时先调用专家搜索，失败时触发语义感知扫描机制。

Result: 在高分辨率基准测试上的大量实验表明，CVSearch在实现最先进精度的同时，显著提升了搜索效率。

Conclusion: CVSearch通过自适应调度搜索策略，有效解决了HR图像感知中覆盖范围与效率的冲突问题，是一种免训练、高精度、高效率的通用框架。

Original Abstract: High-resolution (HR) image perception presents a key bottleneck for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). While visual search offers a promising solution, existing methods struggle with the trade-off between coverage and efficiency. Visual expert-assisted search is efficient but prone to blind spots when proposals fail, whereas scan-based search guarantees coverage at the cost of computational redundancy and semantic fragmentation. To address this dilemma, we introduce CVSearch, a training-free adaptive framework that dynamically schedules search strategies via an Assess-then-Search workflow. Specifically, CVSearch first invokes expert-assisted search when global information is insufficient, and only triggers a novel semantic-aware scanning mechanism upon failure. Distinct from rigid grid partitioning, this efficient scanning paradigm incorporates Semantic Guided Adaptive Patching to decompose images into semantically consistent regions, effectively mitigating object fragmentation. Furthermore, we devise a Dynamic Bottom-Up Search strategy driven by a Visual Complexity prior to enable efficient and precise iterative exploration of local details. Extensive experiments on HR benchmarks demonstrate that CVSearch achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while substantially improving search efficiency. Code is released at https://github.com/liliupeng28/ICML26-CVSearch.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率（HR）图像感知是多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）面临的一个关键瓶颈。尽管视觉搜索提供了一种有前景的解决方案，但现有方法难以在覆盖范围与效率之间取得平衡。视觉专家辅助搜索效率高，但当提议失败时容易产生盲区；而基于扫描的搜索虽然保证了覆盖范围，却以计算冗余和语义碎片化为代价。为解决这一困境，我们提出了CVSearch，一种免训练的自适应框架，通过"评估-然后-搜索"工作流动态调度搜索策略。具体而言，CVSearch首先在全局信息不足时调用专家辅助搜索，仅在失败时触发一种新型的语义感知扫描机制。与僵化的网格划分不同，这种高效的扫描范式引入了语义引导自适应分割，将图像分解为语义一致的区域，有效缓解了目标碎片化问题。此外，我们设计了一种由视觉复杂度先验驱动的动态自底向上搜索策略，能够高效且精确地迭代探索局部细节。在高分辨率基准测试上的大量实验表明，CVSearch在实现最先进精度的同时，显著提升了搜索效率。代码已开源至 https://github.com/liliupeng28/ICML26-CVSearch。

</details>


### [56] [RiGS: Rigid-aware 4D Gaussian Splatting from a Single Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23672)
*Chenyu Wu,Wanhua Li,Zhu-Tian Chen,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RiGS提出了一种刚性感知的4D高斯泼溅方法，通过引入静态、刚性和瞬态三种高斯原语，分别建模静态背景、长期低频运动和短期高频动态，实现了从单目视频中高质量重建动态3D场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在建模真实世界运动时，难以同时保持时间一致性并捕捉高频动态，因为运动既包含长期平滑变换也包含短期复杂形变，而现有运动建模能力有限。

Method: RiGS引入三种高斯原语（静态、刚性、瞬态）分别建模不同时间尺度的运动；提出目标级动态掩码聚合长程时空运动信息以引导静态与动态区域分解；刚性高斯可根据时间持续度过渡为瞬态高斯，两者均在场景流引导下优化以获得密集3D运动监督。

Result: 在新视角合成基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过多尺度运动建模和显式的运动分解，RiGS能够有效同时捕捉长期和短期动态，在动态场景重建任务上超越了现有方法。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from monocular videos is a fundamental yet highly challenging task, as real-world motions often involve both long-term smooth transformations and short-term complex deformations. Existing methods either struggle to maintain temporal consistency or fail to capture high-frequency dynamics due to limited motion modeling capacity. In this work, we present Rigid-aware 4D Gaussian Splatting (RiGS), which simultaneously captures motions across multiple temporal scales. Specifically, RiGS introduces three types of Gaussian primitives: static, rigid, and transient, which represent static backgrounds, long-term low-frequency motions, and short-term high-frequency dynamics, respectively. An object-wise dynamic mask is proposed to aggregate long-range spatiotemporal motion information and guide the decomposition of static and dynamic regions. To jointly model motion across scales, rigid Gaussians are allowed to transition into transient Gaussians based on their temporal duration, and both are optimized under scene flow guidance, providing dense 3D motion supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RiGS achieves state-of-the-art performance on novel view synthesis benchmarks. Code is available at \hyperlink{https://github.com/ladvu/RiGS}{https://github.com/ladvu/RiGS}.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频中重建动态3D场景是一项基础但极具挑战性的任务，因为真实世界的运动通常同时涉及长期平滑变换和短期复杂形变。现有方法要么难以保持时间一致性，要么由于运动建模能力有限而无法捕捉高频动态。在这项工作中，我们提出了刚性感知的4D高斯泼溅（RiGS），它能够同时捕捉多个时间尺度上的运动。具体而言，RiGS引入了三种类型的高斯原语：静态、刚性和瞬态，分别表示静态背景、长期低频运动和短期高频动态。提出了一种目标级动态掩码，用于聚合长程时空运动信息并引导静态与动态区域的分解。为了联合建模跨尺度的运动，刚性高斯根据其时间持续度可过渡为瞬态高斯，两者均在场景流引导下进行优化，提供密集的3D运动监督。大量实验表明，RiGS在新视角合成基准上取得了最先进的性能。代码已开源：\hyperlink{https://github.com/ladvu/RiGS}{https://github.com/ladvu/RiGS}。

</details>


### [57] [CRONOS: Benchmarking Counterfactual Physical Consistency in Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23699)
*León Begiristain,Olaf Dünkel,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRONOS是一个基于干预的基准测试，用于评估视频预测模型的反事实物理一致性。实验表明，现有开源视频生成器在场景、视角、外观等变化下表现不稳定，物理预测质量受外观和视角影响显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频预测模型是否能学习到物理世界的因果结构，还是仅利用表面视觉相关性进行预测尚不清楚，缺乏系统评估模型反事实物理一致性的基准。

Method: 在Unreal Engine中构建CRONOS基准，通过系统干预视角、场景、物体类别和物体外观四个关键因素，保持底层物理事件类型（如碰撞、遮挡、坠落）不变，生成高保真视频来评估模型的反事实物理一致性。

Result: 现有开源视频生成器在反事实物理一致性方面存在显著缺陷：同一物理事件类型的预测质量受外观、环境特别是视角变化的影响。

Conclusion: CRONOS提供了一个可控且可重复的测试平台，用于诊断不同干预下生成视频质量的变化，为开发能在多种条件变化下保持稳定表现的模型建立了具体目标。

Original Abstract: Video prediction is increasingly viewed as a path toward generalizable world models, yet it remains unclear whether these systems learn underlying causal structure or merely exploit superficial visual correlations for future prediction. We introduce CRONOS, an intervention-based benchmark designed to evaluate counterfactual physical consistency: whether a model's predictions of physical events respond appropriately to controlled changes in the visual input, such as variations of scene context, viewpoint, object appearance, and object category. Built in a photorealistic Unreal Engine environment, CRONOS enables controlled, high-fidelity generation of videos across diverse scenes and dynamics. In contrast to previous benchmarks, CRONOS systematically intervenes on four key factors - viewpoint, scene, object category, and object appearance - while keeping the underlying physical event type, such as a collision, occlusion, or fall, fixed. Our evaluation of recent open-source video generators reveals substantial failures in counterfactual physical consistency: prediction quality for the same physical event type is affected by appearance, environment, and, particularly by viewpoint changes. CRONOS provides a controlled and reproducible testbed for diagnosing how the quality of generated videos changes for different interventions, establishing a concrete target for developing models that perform consistently across changes of multiple conditions. The dataset and code are available at our project page.

中文摘要翻译: 视频预测日益被视为通往可泛化世界模型的路径，但目前尚不清楚这些系统是学习了底层的因果结构，还是仅利用表面的视觉相关性进行未来预测。我们提出了CRONOS，一个基于干预的基准测试，旨在评估反事实物理一致性：即模型的物理事件预测是否对视觉输入中的受控变化（如场景背景、视角、物体外观和物体类别的变化）做出正确的响应。CRONOS基于高保真的Unreal Engine环境构建，能够在多种场景和动力学条件下生成可控、高保真的视频。与以往的基准不同，CRONOS系统性地干预了四个关键因素——视角、场景、物体类别和物体外观——同时保持底层物理事件类型（如碰撞、遮挡或坠落）不变。我们对近期开源视频生成器的评估揭示出其在反事实物理一致性方面的严重缺陷：同一物理事件类型的预测质量受外观、环境，尤其是视角变化的显著影响。CRONOS提供了一个可控且可重复的测试平台，用于诊断不同干预下生成视频质量的变化，为开发能在多种条件变化下保持稳定表现的模型建立了具体目标。数据集和代码已在项目页面公开。

</details>


### [58] [Weierstrass Positional Encoding for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23719)
*Zhihang Xin,Rui Wang,Xitong Hu,Xiaojun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Weierstrass椭圆函数的二维位置编码方法WePE，通过复平面映射保留图像空间结构，在多种ViT架构上带来稳定性能提升且无额外计算开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Vision Transformer中可学习的一维位置编码削弱了图像的二维空间结构，且缺乏几何约束，无法保持欧氏空间距离与序列索引距离之间的单调关系，限制了ViT利用空间邻近先验的能力。

Method: 将归一化的二维patch坐标映射到复平面，利用Weierstrass椭圆函数及其导数构造紧凑的四维位置特征。该方法具有双周期性，其格点结构天然匹配图像patch网格，非线性几何性质能更真实地建模空间距离关系，且代数加法公式使得任意patch对之间的相对位置信息可直接从绝对编码中推导。

Result: 大量实验表明WePE在多数设置下带来一致的性能提升；通过预计算查找表，这些改进不引入显著的计算或内存开销；消融研究进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: WePE是一种即插即用、分辨率无关的位置编码方法，可无缝集成到现有ViT中，有效提升视觉任务的性能。

Original Abstract: Vision Transformers have achieved remarkable success in computer vision, but their common use of learnable one-dimensional positional encodings weakens the inherent two-dimensional spatial structure of images after patch flattening. Existing positional encodings often lack geometric constraints and do not preserve a monotonic relationship between Euclidean spatial distances and sequential index distances, limiting ViTs' ability to exploit spatial proximity priors. Motivated by the usefulness of periodicity in positional encoding, we propose Weierstrass elliptic Positional Encoding (WePE), a mathematically grounded method for encoding two-dimensional coordinates in the complex domain. WePE maps normalized 2D patch coordinates onto the complex plane and constructs compact four-dimensional positional features using the Weierstrass elliptic function and its derivative. The double periodicity provides a principled representation of 2D positions, and its intrinsic lattice structure naturally matches the regular geometry of image patch grids. Its nonlinear geometric properties help model spatial distance relationships more faithfully, while the algebraic addition formula enables relative positional information between arbitrary patch pairs to be derived directly from their absolute encodings. WePE is plug-and-play and resolution-agnostic, allowing seamless integration into existing ViTs. Extensive experiments show that WePE brings consistent performance gains in most settings. With precomputed lookup tables, these improvements introduce no noticeable computational or memory overhead. Additional analyses and ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉Transformer在计算机视觉领域取得了显著成功，但其中常用的可学习一维位置编码在图像块展平后削弱了图像固有的二维空间结构。现有的位置编码常缺乏几何约束，无法保持欧氏空间距离与序列索引距离之间的单调关系，限制了ViT利用空间邻近先验的能力。受周期性在位置编码中作用的启发，本文提出Weierstrass椭圆位置编码（WePE），一种基于数学理论的复域二维坐标编码方法。WePE将归一化的二维patch坐标映射到复平面，利用Weierstrass椭圆函数及其导数构造紧凑的四维位置特征。双周期性提供了二维位置的原则性表示，其固有的格点结构天然匹配图像patch网格的规则几何。其非线性几何性质有助于更真实地建模空间距离关系，而代数加法公式使得任意patch对之间的相对位置信息可直接从其绝对编码中推导得出。WePE即插即用且分辨率无关，可无缝集成到现有ViT中。大量实验表明，WePE在大多数设置下带来一致的性能提升。通过预计算查找表，这些改进不引入显著的计算或内存开销。额外的分析和消融研究进一步验证了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [59] [Revitalizing Dense Material Segmentation: Stabilized Vision Transformers and the Generalization Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23747)
*Allan Kazakov,Duygu Cakir,Hilal Kurt İrfanoğlu,Yavuz İrfanoğlu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文复兴了Apple Dense Material Segmentation (DMS)数据集并建立了现代Vision Transformer基线，提出了稳定训练方案（高保真Logit投影、查询熵正则化、物理合规数据增强），使SegFormer-B5达到SOTA；同时揭示了“泛化悖论”：重新划分数据为80/10/10的分割虽能提升指标，但会导致分布同质化，严重降低真实世界分布外性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Apple DMS数据集基准因缺乏现代基线而逐渐被几何偏置的基础模型所掩盖，标准训练范式在无定形纹理场上因高方差梯度而失效，亟需建立鲁棒的Vision Transformer基线并恢复该基准的学术关注度。

Method: 1) 全面评估SegFormer和Mask2Former架构；2) 提出稳定训练策略：高保真Logit投影、查询熵正则化、领域特定且物理合规的数据增强流程；3) 恢复并释放原始数据集索引。

Result: SegFormer-B5在原始数据集分割上达到0.4572 mIoU（SOTA）；80/10/10重分割虽将指标提升至0.5276 mIoU，但专家定性分析表明这导致分布同质化，严重损害真实分布外性能。

Conclusion: 材料感知问题远未解决，呼吁学界采用严格的原始数据集分割以推动物理理解驱动的AI进步。

Original Abstract: Material segmentation, the pixel-wise classification of physical surface properties, remains a challenging problem in computer vision, requiring physicochemical understanding distinct from object-centric parsing. Despite the introduction of the rigorous Apple Dense Material Segmentation (DMS) dataset, the benchmark has suffered from attrition and stagnation, increasingly overshadowed by geometry-biased foundation models. In this paper, we revive the Apple-DMS benchmark to establish a modern Vision Transformer baseline. We conduct an exhaustive evaluation of SegFormer and Mask2Former architectures, revealing that standard training paradigms fail on amorphous texture fields due to high-variance gradients. To address this, we introduce a stabilized training recipe featuring High-Fidelity Logit Projection, Query Entropy Regularization, and a domain-specific, physics-compliant augmentation pipeline. Our optimized SegFormer-B5 achieves a new State-of-the-Art (SOTA) of 0.4572 mIoU on the original dataset split, significantly surpassing the prior convolutional baseline. Furthermore, we identify a critical "Generalization Paradox": while re-partitioning the dataset into a data-rich 80/10/10 split inflates the metric to 0.5276 mIoU, expert qualitative analysis reveals this induces distributional homogenization, severely degrading real-world, out-of-distribution performance. By releasing our recovered dataset index and robust training framework, we demonstrate that material perception is far from solved and urge the community to leverage the rigorous original split to drive genuine progress in physically grounded artificial intelligence.

中文摘要翻译: 材料分割——对物理表面属性进行像素级分类——仍然是计算机视觉中的一项挑战性任务，需要不同于以物体为中心解析的物理化学理解。尽管引入了严格的Apple密集材料分割（DMS）数据集，该基准仍面临关注度流失和停滞不前的困境，日益被几何偏置的基础模型所掩盖。本文复兴了Apple-DMS基准，建立了现代Vision Transformer基线。我们对SegFormer和Mask2Former架构进行了详尽评估，揭示了标准训练范式因高方差梯度而在无定形纹理场上失效的问题。为此，我们引入了一种稳定的训练方案，包括高保真Logit投影、查询熵正则化以及领域特定且物理合规的数据增强流程。优化后的SegFormer-B5在原始数据集分割上达到了新的最先进水平（SOTA），mIoU为0.4572，显著超越了之前的卷积基线。此外，我们识别出一个关键的"泛化悖论"：将数据集重新划分为数据密集型80/10/10分割虽将指标提升至0.5276 mIoU，但专家定性分析表明这会导致分布同质化，严重降低真实世界分布外性能。通过发布我们恢复的数据集索引和稳健的训练框架，我们证明了材料感知远未解决，并敦促学界利用严格的原始数据集分割，推动物理理解驱动的真正人工智能进步。

</details>


### [60] [PhotoFlow: Agentic 3D Virtual Photography Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23771)
*Jiarui Guo,Haojia Wei,Yiming Zhang,Yifei Liu,Yuning Gong,Hongjie Zhang,Xue Yang,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为PhotoFlow的Director-Reviewer-Reflector闭环相机搜索智能体，以及一个包含47个开源Blender场景和141个语言条件摄影任务的基准VPhotoBench，用于语言条件虚拟摄影任务。PhotoFlow在外部质量-对齐复合指标和成功率上均优于多种基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 虚拟摄影任务要求智能体在没有预设相机位姿或参考图像的情况下，基于场景信息和语言意图推断合适的镜头，这同时考验复杂的3D空间理解和抽象审美判断能力，而现有方法难以同时评估这两方面的能力。

Method: PhotoFlow采用Director-Reviewer-Reflector三模块闭环搜索架构：Director构建软摄影蓝图并生成多样化的候选相机；Reviewer结合规则检查、视觉批评和成对优选进行选择；Reflector将失败转化为区域记忆、死区抑制和高探索重定位。同时构建VPhotoBench基准，包含47个开源Blender场景和141个语言条件摄影任务，涵盖主体放置、关系构图和氛围/风格三类任务。

Result: 在六轮渲染预算的保留实验中，PhotoFlow在外部质量-对齐复合指标和成功率上均优于一次性预测、单链反思、锚点库选择和随机搜索等基线方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个将任意Blender场景中的语言条件虚拟摄影设定为可执行智能体任务的工作，结果表明以LLM为中心的空间智能体在同时挑战3D推理和审美选择的设置下已能生成优秀的照片。

Original Abstract: Virtual photography asks an agent to enter a prepared 3D scene with no preselected camera pose or reference image, infer a suitable shot from scene information and a language intent, choose executable camera parameters, and render the final photograph. Recent progress in vision-language models makes this kind of spatial agent increasingly plausible, but the task stresses two capabilities that remain hard to evaluate together: complex 3D spatial understanding and abstract aesthetic judgment. We introduce PhotoFlow, a Director-Reviewer-Reflector agent for closed-loop camera search. The Director builds a soft photographic blueprint and proposes diverse candidate cameras; the Reviewer combines rule checks, visual critique, and pairwise incumbent selection; and the Reflector converts failures into region memory, dead-zone suppression, and high-explore relocation. We also introduce VPhotoBench, a benchmark of 47 open-license Blender scenes and 141 language-conditioned photography missions spanning subject placement, relational composition, and atmosphere/style. On held-out experiments, PhotoFlow achieves the strongest external quality-alignment composite and success rate among one-shot prediction, single-chain reflection, anchor-bank selection, and random search under a six-round rendering budget.
