### [79] [PilotWiMAE: Pilot-Native Representation Learning for Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22856)
*Berkay Guler,Giovanni Geraci,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: PilotWiMAE是一种自监督框架，通过直接处理含噪导频观测并采用因子化注意力机制（时间与联合空频处理分离），在去除全信道状态信息（Full-CSI）假设的前提下，实现了跨频率波束选择与信道表征，且在更小的观测空间下性能超越监督基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有信道基础模型假设可获取完全观测的信道（Full-CSI），这一假设在实际部署中无法成立，导致理论与应用之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出PilotWiMAE框架：编码器直接输入含噪导频观测；注意力机制沿时间轴与联合空频轴进行因子化分解；采用补丁归一化重建（捕获小尺度衰落）与辅助尺度损失（恢复大尺度衰落）相结合；使用AWGN课程学习匹配预训练与部署时的导频噪声；并提出解码器中心预训练阶段以解耦解码器容量与表征质量。

Result: 仅基于3.5GHz数据预训练，在28GHz的分布内和分布外场景下评估，PilotWiMAE的跨频率波束选择和信道表征性能优于监督基线；导频输入将观测空间缩小了两个数量级，并实现了99%的预训练掩码比率。

Conclusion: PilotWiMAE通过物理启发的因子化注意力设计和导频级输入，有效解决了全信道假设不可行的问题，实现了高效、鲁棒的跨频率信道表征，且解码器中心预训练进一步提升了信道估计性能而不损失表征质量。

Original Abstract: Channel foundation models assume access to fully observed channels, an assumption that fails in deployment. We introduce PilotWiMAE, a self-supervised framework whose encoder ingests noisy pilot observations directly and whose attention factorizes along the axis separating temporal from joint space-frequency processing, an inductive bias inspired by the physics of the problem. Pilot input shrinks the observation space by up to two orders of magnitude and also removes the unrealistic assumption of full-CSI availability while incurring lower latency. The factorized design generates robust representations by exploiting the separable channel structure and allows a pretraining mask ratio of $99\%$. We pair patch-normalized reconstruction, which captures small-scale fading structure, with an auxiliary scale loss that recovers the large-scale fading features, and use an AWGN curriculum to match pilot noise at pretraining and deployment. Pretrained solely on $3.5$\,GHz and evaluated at $28$\,GHz across in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, PilotWiMAE's cross-frequency beam selection and channel characterization beat supervised baselines despite operating on a smaller observation space. To weaken the coupling between decoder capacity and representation quality, we further propose a decoder-centric pretraining stage following the encoder-decoder joint pretraining, which allows PilotWiMAE to demonstrate competitive channel estimation without sacrificing representation quality. To foster further work in this direction, we release the PilotWiMAE pretrained weights and training pipeline, together with CSIGen, our Sionna-based ray-tracing channel-generation tool, and the channel datasets used in this work.

中文摘要翻译: 信道基础模型假设可以获取完全观测的信道，这一假设在实际部署中无法成立。我们提出PilotWiMAE，一个自监督框架，其编码器直接输入含噪导频观测，其注意力机制沿时间轴与联合空频轴进行因子化分解——这一归纳偏置受问题物理原理启发。导频输入将观测空间缩小了多达两个数量级，同时消除了全信道状态信息（Full-CSI）可用的不现实假设，并降低了延迟。因子化设计通过利用可分离的信道结构生成鲁棒的表征，并允许预训练掩码比率达到99%。我们将补丁归一化重建（捕获小尺度衰落结构）与辅助尺度损失（恢复大尺度衰落特征）相结合，并采用AWGN课程学习来匹配预训练和部署时的导频噪声。仅基于3.5 GHz数据预训练，在28 GHz的分布内和分布外场景下评估，PilotWiMAE的跨频率波束选择和信道表征性能优于监督基线，尽管其操作的观测空间更小。为进一步削弱解码器容量与表征质量之间的耦合，我们在编码器-解码器联合预训练之后提出了一个以解码器为中心的预训练阶段，这使得PilotWiMAE能够在展示出有竞争力的信道估计性能的同时不牺牲表征质量。为促进该方向的进一步研究，我们发布了PilotWiMAE的预训练权重和训练流程，以及CSIGen（我们基于Sionna的射线追踪信道生成工具）和本工作中使用的信道数据集。

</details>


### [80] [JointHRRP-Net: A Statistically Constrained Decoupling Network for Joint Target and Jamming Recognition in Composite Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22857)
*Yunfei Zhao,Mei Liu,Shuowei Liu,Xunzhang Gao,Yujie Zhou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出JointHRRP-Net统一框架，通过统计约束解耦模块和多尺度时序编码模块，在复合干扰环境下实现雷达高分辨距离像的目标-干扰联合识别，有效提升目标识别与干扰分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 复合干扰环境下，有源干扰经脉冲压缩后与目标回波在HRRP域耦合，导致目标散射峰难以区分、特征可分性下降，现有方法性能严重退化。

Method: 1) 统计约束解耦模块：生成目标主导和干扰主导的潜变量分支，通过相关性引导的统计约束抑制跨分支冗余信息，缓解特征纠缠；2) 多尺度时序编码模块：建模局部散射结构和远程距离单元依赖；3) 双专家决策模块：分别进行单标签目标分类和多标签干扰分类。

Result: 在多种信干比和信噪比水平下，JointHRRP-Net在目标识别和复合干扰识别上均优于代表性基线方法；开集评估表明目标表示对未知目标拒绝具有判别性。

Conclusion: JointHRRP-Net在复合干扰场景下具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: High-resolution range profile (HRRP)-based radar automatic target recognition suffers from severe performance degradation in composite jamming environments. Active jamming introduces suppression- and deception-related components into the received range profile. After pulse compression, these components are coupled with target echoes in the HRRP domain, making target-related scattering peaks difficult to distinguish and weakening feature separability. To address this problem, this paper proposes JointHRRP-Net, a unified framework for joint target-jamming recognition. A statistically constrained decoupling module is first developed to generate target-dominant and jamming-dominant latent branches from the mixed HRRP representation. Correlation-guided statistical constraints are imposed to suppress redundant cross-branch information and alleviate target-jamming feature entanglement. A multi-scale temporal encoding module is then designed to model local scattering structures and long-range range-cell dependencies, followed by a dual-expert decision module for single-label target classification and multi-label jamming classification. Experiments under diverse signal-to-jamming ratio (SJR) and signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) levels demonstrate that JointHRRP-Net outperforms representative baseline methods in both target recognition and composite jamming recognition. Open-set evaluation further shows that the learned target representation remains discriminative for unknown-target rejection. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of JointHRRP-Net in composite jamming scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 基于高分辨距离像(HRRP)的雷达自动目标识别在复合干扰环境中存在严重的性能退化问题。有源干扰会在接收到的距离像中引入压制性和欺骗性分量。经脉冲压缩后，这些分量与目标回波在HRRP域耦合，使得目标相关的散射峰难以区分，削弱了特征可分性。针对这一问题，本文提出JointHRRP-Net，一个用于目标-干扰联合识别的统一框架。首先设计统计约束解耦模块，从混合HRRP表示中生成目标主导和干扰主导的潜变量分支。通过相关性引导的统计约束抑制跨分支冗余信息，缓解目标-干扰特征纠缠。随后设计多尺度时序编码模块，建模局部散射结构和远程距离单元依赖，并采用双专家决策模块分别进行单标签目标分类和多标签干扰分类。在不同信干比和信噪比水平下的实验表明，JointHRRP-Net在目标识别和复合干扰识别上均优于代表性基线方法。开集评估进一步表明，学习到的目标表示对未知目标拒绝保持判别性。这些结果证明了JointHRRP-Net在复合干扰场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [81] [Classification of IED-free EEG Responses for Assisted Epilepsy Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22858)
*Giacomo Zanardini,Ryan Moesman,Paul van der Kleij,Robert van den Berg,Justin Dauwels*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机器学习的流水线，利用时间、频谱、小波和连接性等多域特征以及堆叠集成方法，对间歇性光刺激和过度换气刺激下的EEG进行分类，从而在缺乏癫痫样放电的情况下辅助癫痫诊断。在TUH数据集上，IED-free静息态EEG分类AUC达97.8%，BAC达93.1%；在EMC数据集上，IPS刺激分类AUC达79.4%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 常规EEG缺乏发作间期癫痫样放电(IEDs)时，癫痫诊断非常困难。IPS和HV刺激虽能提高诊断率，但其解读具有主观性。因此，需要一种客观、可复现的流水线来分类刺激过程中的EEG记录。

Method: 提出可复现的流水线，提取时间域、频谱域、小波域和连接性域等多域机器学习特征，并使用堆叠集成(Stacked Ensemble)方法组合互补特征集。采用留一受试者交叉验证(LOSO)在TUH癫痫语料库和Erasmus MC临床队列上评估性能，包括针对无IED数据的分

Result: 在TUH数据集上，集成模型在无IED静息态EEG上达到97.8% AUC/93.1% BAC，在无IED IPS上达到94.1% AUC/86.8% BAC。在EMC数据集上，IPS提供最强区分能力(79.4% AUC/73.9% BAC)，HV性能通过按反应性分层受试者得到提升。

Conclusion: 刺激诱发的活动（尤其是IPS）包含对无IED癫痫分类有意义的判别信息，多域集成方法能提高分类的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Diagnosing epilepsy is challenging when routine EEGs lack interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs). Intermittent photic stimulation (IPS) and hyperventilation (HV) can increase diagnostic yield, but their interpretation is subjective. We propose a reproducible pipeline that classifies EEG recordings acquired during stimulation procedures, using machine-learning features spanning temporal, spectral, wavelet, and connectivity domains, and a stacked ensemble to combine complementary feature sets. Performance is evaluated with leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation on the TUH Epilepsy Corpus and a clinical Erasmus MC (EMC) cohort, including IED-free analyses on TUH. On TUH, ensembles achieve up to 97.8\% AUC / 93.1\% BAC on IED-free resting-state EEG and 94.1\% AUC / 86.8\% BAC on IED-free IPS. On EMC, IPS provides the strongest discrimination (79.4\% AUC / 73.9\% BAC), while HV performance benefits from stratifying subjects by responsiveness. These results indicate that stimulation-evoked activity, particularly IPS, contains meaningful discriminative information for IED-free epilepsy classification and that multi-domain ensembling improves robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 当常规脑电图缺乏发作间期癫痫样放电(IEDs)时，诊断癫痫具有挑战性。间歇性光刺激(IPS)和过度换气(HV)可提高诊断率，但其解读具有主观性。我们提出了一种可复现的流水线，该流水线使用跨时间域、频谱域、小波域和连接性域的机器学习特征对刺激过程中采集的EEG记录进行分类，并通过堆叠集成方法组合互补特征集。在TUH癫痫语料库和Erasmus MC(EMC)临床队列上使用留一受试者交叉验证(LOSO)评估性能，包括在TUH上进行无IED分析。在TUH上，集成模型在无IED静息态EEG上达到高达97.8% AUC/93.1% BAC，在无IED IPS上达到94.1% AUC/86.8% BAC。在EMC上，IPS提供了最强的区分能力(79.4% AUC/73.9% BAC)，而HV性能通过按反应性分层受试者得到提升。这些结果表明，刺激诱发的活动（尤其是IPS）包含对无IED癫痫分类有意义的判别信息，并且多域集成可提高鲁棒性。

</details>


### [82] [Statistical Characterization of Wind-Induced Beam Refraction in Water-to-Air Optical Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22861)
*Mohamed Nennouche,Ikenna Chinazaekpere Ijeh,Mohammad-Ali Khalighi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对动态海面下垂直水-空光通信链路，提出了一种基于Beta混合分布拟合风致指向误差的统计信道模型，推导了信道分布与中断概率的闭式表达式，发现了全内反射导致的中断底限，为鲁棒系统设计提供了理论依据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 直接水-空光通信在动态海面会受到强烈的光束折射影响，现有模型难以准确刻画风致指向误差及全内反射导致的链路中断特性，亟需一种可解析处理的统计信道模型来指导系统设计。

Method: 提出一种新型可处理的统计信道模型，利用期望最大化算法拟合Beta混合分布来建模不同风速下的风致指向误差，并考虑全内反射和接收机视场限制导致的链路中断，推导出信道分布与中断概率的闭式表达式。

Result: 分析揭示了全内反射引起的中断底限（outage floor）从根本上限制了链路可靠性，为鲁棒水-空光通信系统设计提供了关键洞见。

Conclusion: 全内反射引起的中断底限是限制垂直水-空光通信可靠性的根本因素，所提模型为系统参数优化和抗中断设计提供了理论工具。

Original Abstract: Direct water-to-air (W2A) optical communications experience strong beam refraction at the dynamic sea surface. This letter proposes a novel and tractable statistical channel model for a vertical W2A link between an underwater node and an unmanned aerial vehicle under varying wind speeds, modeling wind-induced pointing errors with a Beta mixture fitted via the Expectation-Maximization algorithm. By accounting for link interruptions due to total internal reflection (TIR) and receiver field-of-view limitations, we derive closed-form expressions for the channel distribution and link outage probability. Our analysis reveals a fundamental TIR-induced outage floor limiting link reliability and providing insight for robust W2A system design.

中文摘要翻译: 直接水-空（W2A）光通信在动态海面会经历强烈的光束折射。本文针对水下节点与无人机之间的垂直水-空链路，提出了一种新颖且可处理的统计信道模型，该模型适用于不同风速条件，利用期望最大化算法拟合Beta混合分布来建模风致指向误差。通过考虑全内反射和接收机视场限制导致的链路中断，我们推导出信道分布和链路中断概率的闭式表达式。分析揭示了全内反射引起的中断底限，该底限从根本上限制了链路可靠性，并为鲁棒水-空通信系统设计提供了重要洞见。

</details>


### [83] [L-FAME: Longitudinal Focused Attention Meditation EEG Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22893)
*Angqi Li,Ab Basit Rafi Syed,Hamzeh Alzweri,Taosheng Liu,Barry H. Cohen,Saiprasad Ravishankar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个纵向专注冥想脑电图数据集（L-FAME）及其配套基准测试，包含74名参与者、三种冥想技术和两个时间点的脑电图与心理评估数据，并设计了三个分类任务来评估冥想状态、技术区分及跨会话泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 目前缺乏纵向设计的、包含多种冥想技术对比的公开脑电图数据集，难以系统研究不同冥想练习的神经效应及其随时间演化的规律。

Method: 收集74名健康大学生在干预前后两个时间点的脑电图数据和心理评估数据，参与者随机分配到三种冥想组（两种咒语冥想和一种呼吸专注冥想）。基于该数据集设计三个分类任务：认知状态解码（静息vs冥想）、冥想技术细粒度分类、跨会话适应评估，并使用多种机器学习和深度学习方法建立基线。

Result: 提供了多种经典机器学习算法和深度学习架构在三个分类任务上的全面基线结果。

Conclusion: 该数据集和基准测试为计算冥想研究和脑电图机器学习提供了标准化框架和宝贵资源，有助于推动新分析方法的开发和比较。

Original Abstract: We introduce a novel Longitudinal Focused Attention Meditation Electroencephalography (L-FAME) dataset and an accompanying benchmark, designed to foster research into the neural effects of various meditation practices and the evolution of these effects over a six-week training period. The dataset contains EEG recordings and psychological assessments from 74 healthy college participants, collected at two distinct time points: pre-intervention and post-intervention. Participants were randomly assigned to one of three distinct meditation groups: two mantra-based techniques (SA-TA-NA-MA and Hare Krishna) and one Breath Focus practice. Leveraging this unique longitudinal and comparative dataset, we propose a benchmark suite comprising three distinct classification tasks: (1) cognitive state decoding to distinguish between resting and meditation states, (2) fine-grained classification of the specific meditation techniques, and (3) cross-session adaptation to evaluate model generalization across the longitudinal time gap. We provide comprehensive baseline results for these tasks utilizing a range of classical machine learning algorithms and deep learning architectures. The complete dataset, preprocessing pipelines, and benchmark evaluation code will be publicly released, offering a valuable resource and a standardized framework for the development and comparison of new analytical methods in computational meditation research and EEG-based machine learning. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/L-FAME-Dataset-Benchmark/L-FAME

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个新颖的纵向专注冥想脑电图数据集（L-FAME）及其配套基准测试，旨在促进对不同冥想实践的神经效应及其在六周训练期间演化规律的研究。该数据集包含74名健康大学生参与者的脑电图记录和心理评估数据，这些数据在两个不同的时间点采集：干预前和干预后。参与者被随机分配到三个不同的冥想组：两种基于咒语的技巧（SA-TA-NA-MA 和哈瑞·奎师那）和一种呼吸专注练习。利用这一独特的纵向对比数据集，我们提出了一个包含三个不同分类任务的基准测试套件：(1) 认知状态解码——区分静息状态与冥想状态；(2) 特定冥想技术的细粒度分类；(3) 跨会话适应——评估模型在纵向时间间隔上的泛化能力。我们利用一系列经典机器学习算法和深度学习架构，为这些任务提供了全面的基线结果。完整的数据集、预处理流程和基准评估代码将公开发布，为计算冥想研究和基于脑电图机器学习的分析方法的开发与比较提供了一个宝贵的资源和标准化框架。该数据集可在 https://huggingface.co/datasets/L-FAME-Dataset-Benchmark/L-FAME 获取。

</details>


### [84] [OctCGS: Octree-Contextual Gaussian Splatting with Explicit Multi-Order Propagation Modeling for Channel Knowledge Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22961)
*Jinghan Zhang,Xitao Gong,Qi Wang,Richard A. Stirling-Gallacher,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出OctCGS，一种基于八叉树上下文的高斯泼溅框架，显式建模无线多跳传播顺序，在信道增益预测上超越基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有隐式神经方法训练推理成本高，而基于高斯泼溅的CKM方法虽然效率更高，但仍将多径交互压缩为聚合散射表示，缺乏对多跳无线传播的显式建模。

Method: 将环境划分为多分辨率八叉树，每个叶子节点锚定一个高斯基元；通过树注意力机制在八叉树层级上建模散射体间的复杂电磁交互，显式刻画跳数顺序。

Result: 信道增益MAE为2.99 dB，NMAE为0.065，相比最强基线分别提升0.88 dB和0.021。

Conclusion: OctCGS通过八叉树上下文和高斯泼溅实现了对多跳传播的显式建模，在效率与精度上均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) learn the relation between transmitter (Tx) and receiver (Rx) positions and channel knowledge to support environment-aware wireless communications. Implicit neural methods can model continuous channel variation but often incur high training and inference cost, while existing Gaussian-splatting-based CKM methods improve efficiency yet still compress wireless multipath interactions into aggregated scattering representations. Consequently, explicit modeling of multi-bounce wireless propagation remains absent from CKM construction. We propose OctCGS, an octree-contextual Gaussian splatting framework that explicitly models the order of bounce jointly over Tx/Rx positions and carrier frequencies. OctCGS partitions the environment into a multi-resolution octree and anchors one Gaussian primitive to each leaf node. Rather than having each Gaussian independently encode all multi-path propagations, it models complex electromagnetic interactions among scatterers through tree attention over the octree hierarchy with controlled complexity. Experiments on simulated benchmarks show that OctCGS achieves a 2.99 dB channel-gain mean absolute error (MAE) and 0.065 channel gain normalized mean absolute error (NMAE), outperforming the strongest baseline by 0.88 dB MAE and 0.021 NMAE.

中文摘要翻译: 信道知识地图（CKM）学习发射机（Tx）和接收机（Rx）位置与信道知识之间的关系，以支持环境感知的无线通信。隐式神经方法可以建模连续的通道变化，但通常带来较高的训练和推理成本；而现有的基于高斯泼溅的CKM方法虽然提高了效率，但仍将无线多径交互压缩为聚合的散射表示。因此，CKM构建中仍然缺乏对多跳无线传播的显式建模。我们提出OctCGS，一个八叉树上下文的高斯泼溅框架，在Tx/Rx位置和载波频率上联合显式建模跳数顺序。OctCGS将环境划分为多分辨率八叉树，并为每个叶子节点锚定一个高斯基元。每个高斯并非独立编码所有多径传播，而是通过受控复杂度的树注意力机制，在八叉树层级上对散射体间的复杂电磁交互进行建模。在模拟基准上的实验表明，OctCKS实现了2.99 dB的信道增益平均绝对误差（MAE）和0.065的信道增益归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE），在MAE和NMAE上分别比最强基线提升了0.88 dB和0.021。

</details>


### [85] [Copula-Induced Correntropy for Robust Conjugate Gradient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23044)
*Farshad Rostami Ghadi,F. Javier Lopez-Martinez,David Morales-Jimenez,Kai-Kit Wong,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于copula诱导的信息论学习框架（CITL），通过将残差依赖的copula空间表示嵌入相似性度量来扩展传统的correntropy方法，从而在存在非高斯、统计依赖噪声的情况下实现鲁棒学习。在合成多变量信号处理回归任务中，该方法在依赖重尾噪声条件下优于MSE、Huber、Student-t以及经典correntropy方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的信息论学习准则（如correntropy）虽能抵抗脉冲性和重尾扰动，但通常按分量逐点应用，未能显式利用多变量、多传感器及时间信号中固有的依赖结构，因此在处理非高斯且统计依赖的噪声时性能受限。

Method: 提出copula诱导的信息论学习框架（CITL）：在copula变换后的残差空间中定义相似性度量，将边际鲁棒性与依赖加权分离；推导了copula诱导的correntropy（CIC）目标函数和混合边际-依赖目标；给出了信息论和贝叶斯解释；设计了适配该准则的鲁棒共轭梯度（CG）学习算法，并建立了固定估计子问题在标准线搜索条件下的充分下降和全局平稳性保证。

Result: 在合成多变量信号处理回归问题上的实验表明，所提方法在依赖重尾噪声环境下持续优于MSE、Huber、Student-t以及经典correntropy-based方法。

Conclusion: 通过将copula空间表示融入correntropy，CITL框架有效分离了边际鲁棒性与依赖结构建模，显著提升了在非高斯、统计依赖噪声下的学习鲁棒性，为多变量和时序信号处理提供了新的理论基础和实用算法。

Original Abstract: Robust learning in the presence of non-Gaussian and statistically dependent noise remains a fundamental challenge in signal processing and adaptive systems. Although information-theoretic learning criteria such as correntropy offer strong robustness against impulsive and heavy-tailed disturbances, existing formulations are commonly applied componentwise and therefore do not explicitly exploit the dependence structures inherent in multivariate, multi-sensor, and temporal signals. In this paper, we propose a learning framework, termed \textit{copula-induced information-theoretic learning} (CITL), which extends correntropy by embedding a copula space representation of residual dependence into the similarity measure. Unlike conventional correntropy-based approaches that operate pointwise on raw residuals, the proposed criterion is defined in a copula-transformed residual space, thus separating marginal robustness from dependence weighting. We derive a copula-induced correntropy (CIC) objective and a mixed marginal--dependence objective used in the implementation, provide information-theoretic and Bayesian interpretations, and develop a robust conjugate gradient (CG) learning algorithm tailored to this criterion. For fixed smooth marginal estimators, a fixed copula-space metric, and a regularized radial penalty, we establish sufficient descent and global stationarity guarantees for the corresponding fixed-estimator subproblem under standard line-search conditions. Experiments on synthetic multivariate signal processing regression problems demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms mean squared error (MSE), Huber, Student's-$t$, and classical correntropy-based approaches, particularly in the presence of dependent heavy-tailed noise.

中文摘要翻译: 在非高斯且统计相关的噪声存在下进行鲁棒学习，仍然是信号处理与自适应系统中的一个基本挑战。尽管诸如correntropy之类的信息论学习准则对脉冲性和重尾扰动具有强鲁棒性，但现有公式通常按分量逐点应用，因此未能显式利用多变量、多传感器和时间信号中固有的依赖结构。在本文中，我们提出了一种称为“copula诱导的信息论学习”（CITL）的学习框架，该框架通过将残差依赖的copula空间表示嵌入到相似性度量中，从而扩展了correntropy。与传统的基于correntropy的方法在原始残差上逐点操作不同，所提出的准则在copula变换后的残差空间中定义，从而将边际鲁棒性与依赖加权分离开来。我们推导了一个copula诱导的correntropy（CIC）目标函数以及在实现中使用的混合边际-依赖目标函数，提供了信息论和贝叶斯解释，并开发了一种针对该准则定制的鲁棒共轭梯度（CG）学习算法。对于固定的光滑边际估计量、固定的copula空间度量和正则化径向罚函数，我们建立了在标准线搜索条件下相应固定估计子问题的充分下降和全局平稳性保证。在合成多变量信号处理回归问题上的实验表明，所提出的方法在存在依赖重尾噪声的情况下，始终优于均方误差（MSE）、Huber、Student-t和经典的基于correntropy的方法。

</details>


### [86] [AFDM as a Software Upgrade of OFDM: One Firmware Patch, a New Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23062)
*Hyeon Seok Rou,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文指出仿射频分复用（AFDM）可通过正交频分复用（OFDM）的简单软件升级（固件补丁）以极低成本实现，并揭示AFDM在双弥散信道鲁棒性、集成感知与通信（ISAC）兼容性、物理层安全以及静态LTI信道全分集增益等方面的优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纠正学术界对AFDM实现成本高的普遍认知，证明AFDM可基于现有OFDM基础设施通过简单固件升级低成本部署，从而推动其在6G高移动性场景、802.11bf感知场景及下一代物联网安全场景中的应用。

Method: 理论分析AFDM与OFDM之间的数学关系，论证AFDM可通过固件补丁方式在现有OFDM系统上实现；同时基于相同的数学原理分析AFDM在静态LTI信道上获得全未编码分集增益的内在能力。

Result: 1. AFDM可通过OFDM的简单软件升级（固件补丁）以边际成本实现。2. 该升级可使系统获得AFDM的三大优势：双弥散信道鲁棒性（支持6G高移动性）、ISAC兼容性（支持802.11bf感知）、低复杂度物理层安全（支持下一代IoT）。3. AFDM具备在静态LTI信道上获得全未编码分集增益的能力。

Conclusion: AFDM可以极低成本通过OFDM固件升级实现，这一简单升级能释放多载波波形此前未被发现的优势，为无线通信系统提供显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: In this white paper, we summarize for the benefit of the wider research community on wireless communications, the two key results that we shared with the attendees of the 2026 IEEE Communication Theory Workshop in Azores, Portugal, about affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM).
  Firstly, we show that in contrast to the wide perception by most researchers, AFDM can be implemented at marginal costs by means of a simple software upgrade (firmware patch) of conventional orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM), indicating that its adoption can potentially be achieved across a wide range of OFDM-based wireless infrastructure and systems. The most crucial relevance of this finding is that such an upgrade would enable, under the specific conditions of the corresponding systems and their applications, exploiting various advantageous features of AFDM, including robustness to doubly dispersive channels (i.e., to support high-mobility use-cases in 6G), inherent integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) compatibility (i.e., to support sensing use-cases in 802.11bf), and the straightforward introduction of low-complexity physical-layer security at the waveform level (as needed in next-generation IoT systems).
  Secondly, we also show that the same mathematical principles underpinning the aforementioned finding, also imply an inherent capability of AFDM to reap the full uncoded diversity of static linear time-invariant (LTI) channels, demonstrating that this simple upgrade taps into previously undiscovered strengths of multicarrier waveforms.

中文摘要翻译: 在本白皮书中，我们总结了两项关键成果，惠及更广泛的无线通信研究社区。这些成果曾在2026年葡萄牙亚速尔群岛举行的IEEE通信理论研讨会上与参会者分享，内容涉及仿射频分复用（AFDM）。首先，我们证明：与大多数研究人员的普遍认知相反，AFDM可以通过对传统正交频分复用（OFDM）进行简单的软件升级（固件补丁）以边际成本实现。这意味着AFDM的采用有可能在广泛的基于OFDM的无线基础设施和系统中实现。该发现最关键的意义在于，在相应系统及其应用的特定条件下，这种升级能够利用AFDM的各种优势特性，包括对双弥散信道的鲁棒性（即支持6G中的高移动性用例）、固有的集成感知与通信（ISAC）兼容性（即支持802.11bf中的感知用例），以及在波形层面直接引入低复杂度物理层安全的能力（如下一代物联网系统所需）。其次，我们还证明：支撑上述发现的相同数学原理，也意味着AFDM具有在静态线性时不变（LTI）信道上获得完整未编码分集增益的内在能力，表明这一简单升级能够释放多载波波形此前未被发现的优势。

</details>


### [87] [Self-Calibration DOA Estimation for Movable Antenna Systems with Antenna Position Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23140)
*Chengzhi Ye,Ruoyu Zhang,Wen Wu,Byonghyo Shim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对可移动天线（MA）系统中存在未知天线位置误差（APE）的波达方向（DOA）估计问题，提出了一种基于交替优化（AO）的自校准估计算法，通过交替估计DOA和APE实现鲁棒无线感知。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MA系统中的DOA估计方法通常假设天线位置精确已知，但在实际场景中存在天线位置误差，导致估计性能严重下降。因此需要一种能够同时估计DOA和未知天线位置误差的鲁棒方法。

Method: 将DOA估计问题转化为导向向量与噪声子空间正交性的优化问题，采用两阶段交替优化：第一阶段固定APE，使用MUSIC算法求解经典DOA估计；第二阶段固定DOA，利用拉格朗日乘子法推导出APE估计的闭式表达式。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的自校准算法相比现有方法在MA系统中具有更优的DOA估计性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于AO的自校准算法能够有效处理MA系统中的天线位置误差问题，显著提升DOA估计的鲁棒性和准确性。

Original Abstract: In this letter, we investigate the direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation problem for wireless sensing with movable antenna (MA) systems in the presence of unknown antenna position errors (APE). To achieve robust wireless sensing, we transform the DOA estimation problem with APE into an optimization problem via the orthogonality between the steering vector and the noise subspace. Then we propose an alternating optimization (AO)-based self-calibration estimation, which consists of two stages and iteratively estimates the APE and DOA. Specifically, in the first stage, by fixing the APE, the problem reduces to the classical DOA estimation problem, which is solved using the multiple signal classification (MUSIC) algorithm. In the second stage, we fix the DOA to estimate the APE. By applying the Lagrange multiplier technique to the subproblem, we obtain a closed-form expression for the APE estimation. Simulation results demonstrate the superior DOA estimation performance of the proposed self-calibration algorithm for MA systems compared to the existing approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了存在未知天线位置误差（APE）的可移动天线（MA）系统中的波达方向（DOA）估计问题。为了实现鲁棒的无线感知，我们利用导向向量与噪声子空间之间的正交性，将带有APE的DOA估计问题转化为一个优化问题。然后提出了一种基于交替优化（AO）的自校准估计算法，该算法包含两个阶段，迭代估计APE和DOA。具体而言，在第一阶段，固定APE后问题简化为经典DOA估计问题，采用多信号分类（MUSIC）算法求解。在第二阶段，固定DOA以估计APE。通过将拉格朗日乘子法应用于子问题，得到了APE估计的闭式表达式。仿真结果表明，与现有方法相比，所提出的自校准算法在MA系统中具有更优的DOA估计性能。

</details>


### [88] [Fundamental Bounds and Efficient Estimation for Dead-Time-Constrained Event Detection, with Application to Single-Photon Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23210)
*Frederic J. N. Jorgensen,Steven G. Johnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对非独立同分布、存在非瘫痪死时间和门控的周期性二元事件检测过程（DED过程），建立了参数估计的渐近统计理论，证明了最大似然估计（MLE）和Le Cam一步估计器的渐近最优性，并通过单光子激光雷达仿真和实验验证了理论的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在单光子激光雷达、荧光寿命成像、X射线天文学及核物理等应用中，检测器在每次事件检测后需经历一段恢复间隔（死时间），导致数据非独立同分布且存在门控效应。传统直方图硬件会丢弃有用统计信息，而现有理论缺乏对此类过程参数估计基本下界的量化分析。

Method: (1) 识别了充分统计量，证明激活计数包含直方图丢弃的统计信息；(2) 证明局部渐近正态性，推导Fisher信息率，得到参数估计的基本下界；(3) 证明MLE可达到该下界；(4) 针对MLE的非凸优化难题，提出Le Cam一步估计器，仅需单次局部校正即可达到相同渐近界。

Result: 仿真和单光子激光雷达真实数据实验验证了渐近理论的有效性，展示了Le Cam一步估计器在实际应用中的实用性。

Conclusion: 建立了DED过程参数估计的渐近统计理论基础，明确了死时间和门控对估计下界的影响，MLE和Le Cam一步估计器均能渐近达到Fisher信息率给出的下界，为实际系统设计提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: We develop an asymptotic statistical theory for parameter estimation from a class of non-i.i.d. periodic binary event-detection processes subject to nonparalyzable dead time and gating, which we call "dead-time event detection" (DED) processes. Such processes arise in single-photon lidar, fluorescence lifetime imaging, X-ray astronomy, and particle or radiation flux measurements in nuclear physics, where each detection renders the radiation/particle detector inactive for a recovery interval. Our theory quantifies how dead time and gating affect the fundamental lower bounds of estimation and identifies practical estimators that attain these bounds. First, we identify a sufficient statistic, showing in particular that activation counts can carry statistically useful information discarded by conventional histogramming hardware. We then prove local asymptotic normality and derive the corresponding Fisher-information rate, thereby obtaining fundamental lower bounds for estimation from DED processes. We prove that the maximum likelihood estimator (MLE), widely used in DED applications, attains these lower bounds. Since computing the MLE typically requires solving a nonconvex optimization problem, we also propose Le Cam one-step estimators, which attain the same asymptotic bounds with only a single local correction rather than iterative optimization. We illustrate the validity of our asymptotic theory and the practical usefulness of one-step estimators through the example of single-photon lidar in both simulations and real-data experiments.

中文摘要翻译: 我们针对一类非独立同分布、受非瘫痪死时间和门控影响的周期性二元事件检测过程（称为“死时间事件检测”（DED）过程），建立了参数估计的渐近统计理论。此类过程出现在单光子激光雷达、荧光寿命成像、X射线天文学以及核物理中的粒子或辐射通量测量中，每次探测会使辐射/粒子探测器在一段恢复间隔内失效。我们的理论量化了死时间和门控如何影响估计的基本下界，并确定了能达到这些下界的实用估计器。首先，我们识别了一个充分统计量，特别表明激活计数可以携带被传统直方图硬件丢弃的统计有用信息。然后，我们证明了局部渐近正态性并推导了相应的Fisher信息率，从而得到了DED过程参数估计的基本下界。我们证明了在DED应用中广泛使用的最大似然估计（MLE）能够达到这些下界。由于计算MLE通常需要求解非凸优化问题，我们还提出了Le Cam一步估计器，该估计器仅需单次局部校正而非迭代优化即可达到相同的渐近界。我们通过单光子激光雷达的例子，在仿真和真实数据实验中验证了渐近理论的有效性以及一步估计器的实际实用性。

</details>


### [89] [Stochastic Geometry Analysis of Uplink CUMA-Enabled Cellular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23366)
*Chenguang Rao,Kai-Kit Wong,Xusheng Zhu,Hanjiang Hong,Chan-Byoung Chae,Ross Murch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于流體天線系統（FAS）的緊湊超大規模陣列（CUMA）在上行多小區蜂窩網絡中的性能。利用隨機幾何框架推導了SIR覆蓋概率、平均用戶速率和小區和速率的緊湊近似表達式，揭示了關鍵設計參數的影響及網絡密集化時的縮放規律。結果表明，在有限CSI條件下，CUMA性能優於傳統方案，是一種低複雜度、硬體高效的潛在解決方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 上行蜂窩網絡受干擾主導，但大規模獲取干擾信道狀態信息（CSI）極為困難。流體天線系統（FAS）通過多端口重構提供額外空間自由度，為替代依賴大量CSI的多天線處理提供了有前景的方案。

Method: 利用隨機幾何框架對多小區蜂窩網絡中的上行CUMA進行建模分析，推導了信乾比（SIR）覆蓋概率的緊湊近似表達式，並表徵了平均用戶速率和小區和速率。

Result: 推導的表達式準確量化了關鍵設計參數對性能的影響，揭示了網絡密集化時的縮放行為。仿真驗證了表達式的準確性，並表明在實際CSI約束下，上行CUMA的性能競爭力強，且通常優於傳統方案。

Conclusion: CUMA作為一種低複雜度、硬體高效的上行解決方案，在未來大規模蜂窩網絡中具有巨大潛力。

Original Abstract: Uplink cellular networks are interference-dominated but interference channel state information (CSI) is rarely available at scale. The emerging fluid antenna system (FAS) concept, which provides additional spatial degrees of freedom through multi-port reconfiguration, offers a promising alternative to CSI-intensive multi-antenna processing. Building on this concept, compact ultra-massive arrays (CUMA) exploit large-scale port selection with low implementation complexity. In each uplink transmission, CUMA activates a subset of ports based on only the desired-link CSI and combines the selected ports via simple superposition, yielding coherent enhancement of the desired user signal, while inter-cell interference aggregates largely non-coherently due to the random superposition effect. Consequently, CUMA is well suited to multi-cell uplink scenarios where CSI is limited. In this paper, we analyze uplink CUMA in multi-cell cellular networks using a stochastic geometry framework. We derive a tight approximate expression for the signal-to-interference ratio (SIR) coverage probability, and further characterize the average user rate and cell sum-rate. The analysis quantifies how key design parameters impact performance and reveals the scaling behavior with network densification. Simulation results validate the accuracy of the derived expressions and show that uplink CUMA achieves competitive, and often superior, performance relative to conventional schemes under practical CSI constraints, highlighting its potential as a low-complexity, hardware-efficient uplink solution for future large-scale cellular networks.

中文摘要翻译: 上行蜂窩網絡受干擾主導，但干擾信道狀態信息（CSI）在大規模場景中難以獲取。新興的流體天線系統（FAS）概念通過多端口重構提供額外的空間自由度，為依賴大量CSI的多天線處理提供了有前景的替代方案。基於此概念，緊湊超大規模陣列（CUMA）以低實現複雜度利用大規模端口選擇。在每次上行傳輸中，CUMA僅基於目標鏈路CSI激活一組端口，並通過簡單疊加合併所選端口，從而實現對目標用戶信號的相干增強，而小區間干擾由於隨機疊加效應基本非相干疊加。因此，CUMA非常適合CSI受限的多小區上行場景。本文利用隨機幾何框架分析了多小區蜂窩網絡中的上行CUMA。我們推導了信乾比（SIR）覆蓋概率的緊湊近似表達式，並進一步表徵了平均用戶速率和小區和速率。該分析量化了關鍵設計參數對性能的影響，揭示了網絡密集化時的縮放行為。仿真結果驗證了所推導表達式的準確性，並表明在實際CSI約束下，上行CUMA相較於傳統方案具有競爭力且通常更優的性能，凸顯了其作為未來大規模蜂窩網絡中低複雜度、硬體高效上行解決方案的潛力。

</details>


### [90] [Communication Security and Sensing Privacy in FMCW-Based ISAC Through Signal Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23429)
*Murat Temiz,Christos Masouros*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以雷达为中心的ISAC安全架构，利用索引调制和相位编码来增强通信安全性和感知隐私性，防止未经授权的被动感知硬件进行目标速度估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ISAC系统在实现通信感知一体化的同时，面临通信数据被窃听以及雷达信号被非法被动感知硬件监视的安全和隐私风险，亟需一种统一的安全信号设计框架。

Method: 在FMCW啁啾信号上联合采用索引调制（IM）提供外层数据安全，并专门设计相位编码（PC）扰动信号的模糊函数（AF），以恶化感知窃听者（S-Eve）的速度和距离估计性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在实现高数据吞吐量的同时，显著提升了通信安全性和感知隐私性，使非法被动感知硬件无法进行有效的目标速度估计，距离估计能力也严重受损。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于索引调制和相位编码的雷达中心式ISAC信号设计，能够有效兼顾通信安全与感知隐私，为一体化系统提供了一种可行的物理层安全解决方案。

Original Abstract: This study proposes a novel radar-centric signaling design and architecture for secure integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. The proposed framework is designed to provide robust physical layer security for data transmission while simultaneously enhancing sensing privacy. It employs index modulation and phase coding over frequency-modulated continuous-wave radar (FMCW) chirps, where index modulation (IM) provides an outer layer of data security, and we explicitly design the phase coding (PC) to perturb the resulting signal's ambiguity function (AF) to enhance sensing privacy. This design reduces the risk of unauthorized surveillance by rendering target velocity estimation practically infeasible for unauthorized passive sensing hardware (i.e., a sensing eavesdropper, S-Eve) and significantly impairing its range estimation capabilities. Furthermore, this study also presents the transmitter and receiver architectures required for effective modulation and demodulation of the proposed ISAC signaling and for performing sensing at the legitimate sensing hardware. Simulation results show that the proposed approach achieves high data throughput while enhancing communication security and sensing privacy.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究提出了一种新颖的以雷达为中心的信号设计与架构，用于安全的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统。所提出的框架旨在为数据传输提供鲁棒的物理层安全性，同时增强感知隐私。该方案在调频连续波雷达（FMCW）啁啾信号上采用索引调制和相位编码，其中索引调制（IM）提供外层数据安全，我们明确设计了相位编码（PC）以扰动所得信号的模糊函数（AF），从而增强感知隐私。该设计通过使未经授权的被动感知硬件（即感知窃听者，S-Eve）实际上无法进行目标速度估计，并显著削弱其距离估计能力，降低了未经授权监视的风险。此外，本研究还提出了用于所提ISAC信号有效调制解调以及在合法感知硬件上执行感知所需的发射机和接收机架构。仿真结果表明，所提方法在增强通信安全性和感知隐私的同时，实现了高数据吞吐量。

</details>


### [91] [Optimal Design Framework for Distributed Array Using Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23481)
*Seang Shim,Yuta Takahashi,Naoto Usami,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于电磁编队飞行（EMFF）分布式空间天线的系统级设计框架，通过耦合天线性能、卫星质量、功率生成、线圈几何和编队保持等约束，实现静态网格基分布式天线的可行孔径设计与评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 分布式空间天线通过电磁编队飞行（EMFF）实现大口径、长寿命空间通信，但其可行孔径受天线性能、卫星质量、功率生成、线圈几何和编队保持功率等多重耦合约束限制，缺乏系统级的设计框架来综合考虑这些因素。

Method: 提出系统级设计框架，将相控阵需求与卫星级尺寸约束联系起来，通过分布式控制仿真推导编队维持的控制指标，并将其集成到天线孔径最大化问题中，同时考虑尺寸、功率、线圈和旁瓣包络约束。

Result: 1) 增加系统质量仅在卫星级设计余量存在时才能改善足迹缩减或有效各向同性辐射功率；2) 在0.15m间距的直接对设备场景中，发电功率和线圈几何约束主导可行孔径；3) 在0.60m大间距场景中，所需线圈负担可能超过卫星级质量、尺寸和功率容量，导致设计不可行。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够在耦合天线、卫星和控制约束下设计和评估可行的静态网格基EMFF分布式天线，为系统级设计提供了有效参考。

Original Abstract: Distributed space antennas using electromagnetic formation flight (EMFF) are a promising architecture for large-aperture, long-life space communication systems. Their feasible aperture, however, is governed by coupled constraints on antenna performance, satellite mass, power generation, coil geometry, and formation-keeping power. This paper proposes a system-level design framework for EMFF-based distributed space antennas. It links phased-array requirements with satellite-level sizing constraints and provides a static grid-based reference for designing feasible apertures under a fixed system mass. Unlike our previous bucket-brigade disturbance-compensation model, the formation-maintenance requirement is incorporated through a control index derived from distributed-control simulations. This index is integrated into an antenna-aperture maximization problem with sizing, power, coil, and sidelobe-envelope constraints. Parametric case studies examine margin magnetic moment, prescribed transmit power, and large inter-satellite spacing. Results show that increasing system mass improves footprint reduction or effective isotropic radiated power only while satellite-level design headroom remains. In direct-to-device cases with 0.15 m spacing, generated-power and coil-geometry constraints dominate the feasible aperture. In the 0.60 m large-spacing case, the required coil burden can exceed satellite-level mass, size, and power capacities, making the design infeasible despite favorable communication performance. The proposed framework enables the design and evaluation of feasible static grid-based EMFF distributed antennas under coupled antenna, satellite, and control constraints.

中文摘要翻译: 采用电磁编队飞行（EMFF）的分布式空间天线是实现大口径、长寿命空间通信系统的一种有前景的架构。然而，其可行孔径受天线性能、卫星质量、功率生成、线圈几何形状和编队保持功率的耦合约束共同决定。本文提出了一种基于EMFF的分布式空间天线的系统级设计框架。该框架将相控阵需求与卫星级尺寸约束联系起来，并为在固定系统质量下设计可行孔径提供了静态网格基参考。与我们先前的水桶接力式扰动补偿模型不同，编队维持需求通过从分布式控制仿真中导出的控制指标来纳入。该指标被集成到一个考虑尺寸、功率、线圈和旁瓣包络约束的天线孔径最大化问题中。参数化案例研究考察了裕度磁矩、指定发射功率和大星间间距。结果表明，仅在卫星级设计余量尚存时，增加系统质量才能改善足迹缩减或有效各向同性辐射功率。在间距为0.15m的直接对设备场景中，发电功率和线圈几何约束主导了可行孔径。在0.60m的大间距场景中，所需的线圈负担可能超过卫星级的质量、尺寸和功率容量，使得设计不可行，尽管通信性能良好。所提出的框架能够在耦合天线、卫星和控制约束下设计和评估可行的静态网格基EMFF分布式天线。

</details>


### [92] [Broad learning system with robust adaptive kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23495)
*Haiquan Zhao,Jinhui Hu,Xin Lua*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对宽度学习系统(BLS)在非高斯噪声环境下性能退化的问题，本文提出一种基于自适应稳健核(AR-BLS)的BLS变体，通过交替优化模型权重与核参数实现鲁棒性的自适应调整，并证明了算法的迭代收敛性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于M估计的BLS变体在非高斯噪声环境下虽有良好鲁棒性，但最优损失函数的确定通常因缺乏样本数据先验知识而非常耗时，亟需一种无需人工干预的自适应鲁棒方法。

Method: 构建基于自适应稳健核的BLS变体(AR-BLS)。自适应稳健核函数是一种包含多种常见M估计范式的通用损失函数，通过交替优化模型权重和自适应稳健核参数，实现对不同离群噪声分布下模型鲁棒性的自适应调整，无需人工干预。同时基于Zangwill全局收敛定理证明了算法的迭代收敛性。

Result: 在多个公共数据集和实际应用场景上的仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: AR-BLS能够在不依赖先验知识的情况下自适应调整鲁棒性，有效提升非高斯噪声环境下模型的泛化性能，且算法具有收敛性保证。

Original Abstract: For the performance degradation problem of broad learning system (BLS) in non-Gaussian noise environment, the variant of BLS based on M-estimator shows good robust performance. However, in most cases, the determination of the optimal loss function is often very time-consuming due to the lack of prior knowledge of the sample data. Therefore, this paper constructs a variant of BLS based on adaptive robust kernel (AR-BLS) to improve the generalization performance of the model in non-Gaussian noise environment. Adaptive robust kernel function is a general loss function that includes many common M-estimator paradigms. By alternately optimizing model weights and adaptive robust kernel parameters, AR-BLS realizes the adaptive adjustment of model robustness under different outlier noise distributions without human intervention. In addition, the iterative convergence of AR-BLS algorithm is proved based on Zangwill's global convergence theorem. Simulation experiments on multiple public datasets and actual application scenarios verify the effectiveness of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 针对宽度学习系统(BLS)在非高斯噪声环境下存在的性能退化问题，基于M估计的BLS变体展现出良好的鲁棒性能。然而，在多数情况下，由于缺乏样本数据的先验知识，最优损失函数的确定往往非常耗时。因此，本文构建了一种基于自适应稳健核的BLS变体(AR-BLS)，以提升模型在非高斯噪声环境下的泛化性能。自适应稳健核函数是一种包含多种常见M估计范式的通用损失函数。通过交替优化模型权重与自适应稳健核参数，AR-BLS能够在无需人工干预的情况下，实现模型对不同离群噪声分布下鲁棒性的自适应调节。此外，基于Zangwill全局收敛定理证明了AR-BLS算法的迭代收敛性。在多个公共数据集及实际应用场景上的仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [93] [Decentralized Variational Bayesian UKF with Maximum Generalized Student's t-kernel Correntropy for Wide-Area Power System state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23496)
*Jinhui Hu,Haiquan Zhao,Yi Peng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分散式最大广义学生t-核相关熵变分贝叶斯无迹卡尔曼滤波器(D-MGST-VBUKF)，通过三级优化策略解决大规模电网中传统集中式状态估计器鲁棒性差和部署困难的问题，在非高斯和未知噪声环境下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统集中式状态估计器在大规模电网中鲁棒性有限且实际部署困难，亟需一种能适应非高斯测量噪声和未知噪声环境的分散式状态估计方法。

Method: (1) 采用最大广义学生t-核相关熵(MGST)构建代价函数，扩展自由度参数以增强对复杂非高斯噪声的适应性；(2) 构建变分贝叶斯(VB)推理框架，通过共轭先验分布在线建模未知噪声分布，实现噪声统计特性与状态估计的联合优化；(3) 基于电网拓扑关联特性建立区域状态融合机制，通过边界节点状态协调方程实现本地估计结果的全局一致性修正。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点和IEEE 39节点系统上的仿真实验表明，与传统算法相比，该方法在非高斯噪声环境和未知噪声环境下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的D-MGST-VBUKF方法能有效解决大规模电网中非高斯噪声和未知噪声环境下的分散式状态估计问题，鲁棒性优于传统算法。

Original Abstract: A Conventional centralized state estimators exhibit limited robustness in large-scale grids and face practical deployment hurdles. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes a decentralized maximum generalized Student's t-kernel correntropy Variational Bayesian unscented Kalman filter (D-MGST-VBUKF). The algorithm optimizes the estimation performance at three levels for the regionalized state estimation needs: first, to address non-Gaussian measurement noise in practical systems, we propose the cost function using MGST, retaining Student's t robustness while improving adaptability to complex noise by expanding the degree-of-freedom parameter; secondly, the VB inference framework is constructed to model the unknown noise distribution online, and the joint optimization of the noise statistical characteristics and state estimation is realized by constructing the conjugate prior distribution; finally, the regional state fusion mechanism is established based on the topological correlation characteristics of the power grid, and the global consistency correction of the local estimation results is realized by constructing the state coordination equation of the boundary nodes. Simulation experiments in IEEE 14-bus and IEEE 39-bus system show that the method has stronger robustness compared with the traditional algorithm under non-Gaussian noise environment and unknown noise environment.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的集中式状态估计器在大规模电网中鲁棒性有限，且面临实际部署的障碍。为克服这些挑战，本文提出了一种分散式最大广义学生t-核相关熵变分贝叶斯无迹卡尔曼滤波器（D-MGST-VBUKF）。该算法针对区域化状态估计需求，从三个层面优化估计性能：首先，为解决实际系统中的非高斯测量噪声问题，采用最大广义学生t分布（MGST）构建代价函数，在保留学生t分布鲁棒性的同时，通过扩展自由度参数增强对复杂噪声的适应性；其次，构建变分贝叶斯（VB）推理框架对未知噪声分布进行在线建模，通过构造共轭先验分布实现噪声统计特性与状态估计的联合优化；最后，基于电网拓扑关联特性建立区域状态融合机制，通过构建边界节点的状态协调方程实现本地估计结果的全局一致性修正。在IEEE 14节点和IEEE 39节点系统上的仿真实验表明，与传统算法相比，该方法在非高斯噪声环境和未知噪声环境下具有更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [94] [Outlier-Robust unscented Kalman filter based on generalized correntropy induced](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23499)
*Jinhui Hu,Haiquan Zhao,Yi Peng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于广义相关熵的鲁棒迭代平方根无迹卡尔曼滤波（SR-GCI-IUKF），通过广义核结构克服传统KF在非线性非高斯噪声下的局限性，具备核带宽不敏感性、非线性误差动态修正和数值稳定性，理论证明估计方差有界，实验验证其在非线性系统、车载导航和电力系统FASE中优于现有鲁棒算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波在处理非线性非高斯噪声污染的状态估计问题时性能严重退化，现有最大相关熵准则（MCC）方法虽能刻画高阶噪声统计量，但对核带宽敏感，难以适应复杂多样的噪声环境。

Method: 1) 采用广义相关熵（GCI）框架替代传统MCC，其广义核结构对核带宽不敏感，增强对不同噪声环境的鲁棒适应性；2) 构建非线性误差泛化模型，在状态更新阶段动态校正测量误差；3) 采用平方根分解实现，保证协方差矩阵正定性，提升数值鲁棒性；4) 通过严格的误差动力学分析建立理论稳定性保证。

Result: 在非线性系统、车载导航系统以及电力系统FASE中的实验表明，所提算法相比其他鲁棒算法具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的SR-GCI-IUKF算法在非线性非高斯噪声环境下具有更强的鲁棒性、更高的估计精度和数值稳定性，理论分析和实验验证均证明了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Conventional Kalman filtering (KF) approaches exhibit significant limitations in addressing nonlinear state estimation problems contaminated by non-Gaussian noise disturbances. To overcome these challenges, this work proposes a robust iterative square root unscented Kalman Filter based on the generalized correntropy induced (SR-GCI-IUKF). While sharing the maximum correntropy criterion's (MCC) ability to characterize higher-order noise statistics, the proposed GCI framework exhibits intrinsic kernel bandwidth insensitivit a critical advantage enabling robust adaptation to diverse complex noise environments through its generalized kernel structure. For nonlinear state estimation challenges, the algorithm constructs a nonlinear error generalization model that dynamically corrects measurement-induced errors during the state update phase, thereby significantly enhancing estimation accuracy in strongly nonlinear regimes. Furthermore, the square-root decomposition implementation ensures numerical robustness by preserving covariance matrix positive definiteness throughout recursive operations. Theoretical stability guarantees are established through rigorous error dynamics analysis, demonstrating bounded estimation variance under non-Gaussian disturbances. Finally, experiments are carried out in nonlinear systems, land vehicle navigation systems as well as power system FASE to compare other robust algorithms, and it is determined that the proposed algorithm has stronger robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 传统卡尔曼滤波方法在处理受非高斯噪声干扰的非线性状态估计问题时存在显著局限性。为克服这些挑战，本文提出一种基于广义相关熵的鲁棒迭代平方根无迹卡尔曼滤波（SR-GCI-IUKF）。该算法在保留最大相关熵准则（MCC）刻画高阶噪声统计量能力的同时，所提出的广义相关熵（GCI）框架展现出内在的核带宽不敏感性——这一关键优势使其通过广义核结构能够鲁棒地适应多样化的复杂噪声环境。针对非线性状态估计难题，该算法构建了一种非线性误差泛化模型，在状态更新阶段动态校正测量引入的误差，从而显著提升强非线性条件下的估计精度。此外，平方根分解实现通过在整个递归运算中保持协方差矩阵的正定性，确保了数值鲁棒性。通过严格的误差动力学分析建立了理论稳定性保证，证明在非高斯扰动下估计方差有界。最后，在非线性系统、车载导航系统以及电力系统FASE中开展实验，与其他鲁棒算法进行比较，结果表明所提算法具有更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [95] [Distributed Two-Phase Processing for Modular XL-MIMO with Wireless Fronthaul under Hardware Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23502)
*Özlem Tuğfe Demir*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对模块化超大规模MIMO系统，提出了一种考虑硬件损伤的联合优化框架，通过联合调整用户发射功率和前传放大电平来最大化上行频谱效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 模块化超大规模MIMO架构结合无线前传是一种可扩展的方案，但其性能对硬件损伤和资源分配策略敏感，现有研究缺乏对接入侧和前传侧畸变的联合建模与优化。

Method: 采用分布式两阶段处理框架，联合建模接入侧和前传侧畸变，构建基于加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）的优化问题，通过畸变感知的接收机设计与凸功率控制更新交替迭代求解。

Result: 与固定传输策略相比，所提联合优化显著提升了频谱效率，尤其在CPU天线数适中时效果明显；同时量化了接入侧与前传侧损伤的相对影响。

Conclusion: 联合优化发射功率和前传放大电平能有效缓解硬件损伤对模块化XL-MIMO系统性能的影响，提升上行频谱效率。

Original Abstract: Modular extremely large-scale MIMO (XL-MIMO) architectures combined with wireless fronthaul provide a scalable alternative to monolithic arrays, but their performance is sensitive to hardware impairments and resource allocation strategies. In this paper, we consider a distributed two-phase processing framework for modular XL-MIMO systems employing amplify-and-forward wireless fronthaul under practical hardware constraints. We jointly model access-side and fronthaul-side distortions and formulate a weighted minimum mean-square error (WMMSE)-based optimization problem that maximizes the uplink sum spectral efficiency (SE) by jointly adjusting UE transmit powers and fronthaul amplification levels. The resulting algorithm alternates between distortion-aware receiver design and convex power-control updates. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed joint optimization significantly improves spectral efficiency compared to fixed transmission strategies, particularly when the CPU has a moderate number of antennas, while also quantifying the relative impact of access and fronthaul impairments.

中文摘要翻译: 结合无线前传的模块化超大规模MIMO（XL-MIMO）架构为 monolithic 阵列提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，但其性能对硬件损伤和资源分配策略敏感。本文考虑一种分布式两阶段处理框架，用于在实用硬件约束下采用放大转发无线前传的模块化XL-MIMO系统。我们联合建模了接入侧和前传侧的畸变，并构建了一个基于加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）的优化问题，通过联合调整用户发射功率和前传放大电平来最大化上行总频谱效率（SE）。得到的算法在畸变感知的接收机设计与凸功率控制更新之间交替迭代。数值结果表明，与固定传输策略相比，所提出的联合优化显著提升了频谱效率，特别是在CPU天线数适中时，同时量化了接入侧与前传侧损伤的相对影响。

</details>


### [96] [Comprehensive Dataset and Signal Processing Framework for Phonocardiogram-Based Heart Rate and Blood Pressure Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23516)
*Abdul Ahad Mamun,Utsab Saha,Md Hasibul Hasan,Shahed Ahmed,MD Jahin Alam*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出并开发了一种低成本的心音追踪系统（PhonoTrack），仅使用心音信号（PCG）即可同时测量心率和血压，实验结果表明该系统在心率估计和血压预测方面均具有较高准确性，为低成本、便携式心血管连续监测提供了可行方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球重大健康挑战，需要定期可靠的监测以实现早期干预。现有研究要么仅从PCG信号估计心率，要么需要结合多种生理数据预测血压，缺乏仅使用PCG信号同时预测心率和血压的经济高效系统。

Method: 1) 开发了实验室规模的PhonoTrack低成本系统，仅使用PCG信号采集数据；2) 从15名参与者收集数据集；3) 采用Hilbert变换（HT）、Shannon熵（SE）和WES三种峰值检测方法确定心率；4) 基于多元回归技术开发了半经验模型来估计收缩压和舒张压。

Result: 心率估计：HT、SE和WES的Pearson相关系数分别为0.965、0.973和0.955，RMSE分别为2.467 bpm、1.688 bpm和1.992 bpm。血压预测：收缩压标准差2.10 mmHg，舒张压标准差3.20 mmHg；Pearson相关系数分别为0.89和0.70。

Conclusion: 这些发现为开发非侵入性、低成本、便携式的PhonoTrack设备铺平了道路，使其成为连续心血管监测场景中一种有前景的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases (CVDs) represent significant global health challenges today, necessitating regular and reliable monitoring to enable early intervention. Phonocardiogram (PCG) signals present a promising non-invasive method for assessing cardiovascular health. While recent studies have focused on estimating heart rate (HR) from PCG signals and blood pressure (BP) through multimodal combinations with other physiological data, reliable and cost-effective systems that can predict both HR and BP using only PCG signals remain largely unexplored. In this study, we proposed and developed a lab-scale cost-effective Phonocardiogram Tracking (PhonoTrack) system that can measure both HR and BP using only the PCG signal. We also introduced a corresponding dataset collected from 15 participants to evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed system. HR was determined using several peak detection methods, such as Hilbert Transform (HT), Shannon Entropy (SE), and WES, achieving notable Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.965, 0.973, and 0.955, respectively. The corresponding root mean square errors (RMSEs) were 2.467 bpm, 1.688 bpm, and 1.992 bpm for HT, SE, and WES, respectively. Additionally, we developed an advanced semi-empirical model based on multiple regression techniques to estimate systolic blood pressure (SBP) and diastolic blood pressure (DBP). This model demonstrated standard deviations of 2.10 mmHg for SBP and 3.20 mmHg for DBP across all subjects, with Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.89 and 0.70, respectively. These findings pave the way for developing a non-invasive, low-cost, and portable PhonoTrack device, positioning it as a promising solution for continuous cardiovascular monitoring settings.

中文摘要翻译: 心血管疾病（CVD）是当今全球面临的重大健康挑战，需要定期可靠的监测以实现早期干预。心音（PCG）信号为评估心血管健康提供了一种有前景的非侵入性方法。虽然近年来的研究集中在从PCG信号估计心率（HR），以及通过与其他生理数据的多模态组合来估计血压（BP），但仅使用PCG信号即可同时预测心率和血压的可靠且经济高效的系统仍鲜有探索。在本研究中，我们提出并开发了一套实验室规模的经济高效的心音追踪（PhonoTrack）系统，该系统仅使用PCG信号即可测量心率和血压。我们还引入了从15名参与者处收集的相应数据集，以评估所提系统的有效性。心率通过多种峰值检测方法确定，包括Hilbert变换（HT）、Shannon熵（SE）和WES，分别达到了0.965、0.973和0.955的显著Pearson相关系数。相应的均方根误差（RMSE）分别为2.467 bpm（HT）、1.688 bpm（SE）和1.992 bpm（WES）。此外，我们开发了一个基于多元回归技术的先进半经验模型来估计收缩压（SBP）和舒张压（DBP）。该模型在所有受试者中的收缩压标准差为2.10 mmHg，舒张压标准差为3.20 mmHg，Pearson相关系数分别为0.89和0.70。这些发现为开发一种非侵入性、低成本、便携式的PhonoTrack设备铺平了道路，使其成为连续心血管监测场景中一种有前景的解决方案。

</details>


### [97] [Utilizing Missed Detections in Directional Sensitivity-Based DOA Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23536)
*Gustav Zetterqvist,Fredrik Gustafsson,Gustaf Hendeby*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信号强度的到达方向（DOA）估计方法，通过将漏检（missed detections）信息显式纳入似然函数，显著提升了定向传感器在漏检率较高场景下的DOA估计精度，并通过BLE信号与定向天线的真实实验验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于相位的DOA估计方法无法处理低于检测阈值的发射源（即漏检）带来的负面信息，而现有基于信号强度的方法也未能充分利用漏检所携带的有价值信息。本文旨在通过建模漏检来提升DOA估计性能。

Method: 提出一种仅依赖接收信号强度（RSS）测量的DOA估计方法。通过将检测到的信号和漏检事件同时纳入似然函数，构建了一个概率估计框架，充分利用了底层测量模型和检测模型的信息。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在漏检率高的挑战性场景下显著优于基线方法；使用BLE信号和定向天线的真实世界实验进一步验证了方法的有效性，展示了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在传感器阵列处理中对漏检进行显式建模具有重要价值，为提升无线通信系统中的定位性能开辟了新途径。

Original Abstract: This paper introduces a signal strength-based direction of arrival (DOA) estimation approach for directional sensors that explicitly accounts for missed detections. In traditional phase-based DOA estimation frameworks, negative information from expected emitters that fall below the detection threshold fall outside the scope of standard measurement models. Unlike phase-based DOA estimation methods, the proposed approach relies only on received signal strength measurements. As a result, missed detections arise naturally from the sensing and detection process and convey valuable information via the known detection thresholds. By incorporating both detected signals and missed detections into the likelihood function, we develop a probabilistic estimation method that fully leverages the underlying measurement and detection models. Simulation results show that the proposed method significantly improves DOA estimation accuracy compared to baseline techniques, particularly in challenging scenarios with high missed-detection rates. Real-world experiments using Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) signals and directional antennas further validate the effectiveness of the approach, demonstrating substantial performance gains. These findings highlight the value of modeling missed detections in sensor array processing and open new avenues for enhancing localization performance in wireless communication systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于信号强度的到达方向（DOA）估计方法，用于定向传感器，并显式考虑了漏检（missed detections）的影响。在传统的基于相位的DOA估计框架中，预期发射源低于检测阈值所产生的负面信息超出了标准测量模型的范围。与基于相位的方法不同，所提方法仅依赖接收信号强度测量值。因此，漏检自然源自感知与检测过程，并通过已知的检测阈值传递出有价值的信息。通过将检测到的信号与漏检同时纳入似然函数，我们开发了一种概率估计方法，充分利用了底层的测量和检测模型。仿真结果表明，与基线技术相比，所提方法在漏检率较高的挑战性场景下显著提升了DOA估计精度。使用蓝牙低功耗（BLE）信号和定向天线的真实世界实验进一步验证了该方法的有效性，展示了实质性的性能增益。这些发现突显了在传感器阵列处理中对漏检进行建模的价值，并为增强无线通信系统中的定位性能开辟了新途径。

</details>


### [98] [Fast Fluid Antenna Multiple Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23642)
*Noor Waqar,Kai-Kit Wong,Chan-Byoung Chae,Ross Murch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Copula的快速流体天线多址接入（FAMA）框架，通过注意力-Copula时间序列模型学习各天线端口间接收信号、信道系数和干扰信号的联合依赖结构，使得用户终端仅观测少量端口即可推断未观测端口的信道与干扰信息，从而消除传统FAMA中需全端口理想干扰已知这一不现实假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统快速流体天线多址接入（FAMA）技术假设每个用户终端在每个符号时刻都能探测所有流体天线端口，并理想地知道所有端口接收信号中的信号-干扰分解信息。这一"Genie-aided"假设在现实中不成立，因为它要求极高的硬件能力、开关切换限制、导频开销，以及未知的信号-干扰分解能力。本文旨在解决这一核心矛盾：用户终端能否在仅观测少量端口的情况下，表现得如同拥有全端口干扰信息一样？

Method: 提出了一个Copula辅助的FAMA框架（Copula-aided FAMA），利用Copula理论学习各端口间复数三元组$(r_k, h_k, I_k)$（接收信号、信道系数、聚合干扰信号）的联合依赖结构。具体地，设计了注意力-Copula时间序列模型（attention-copula time-series model），该模型在随机部分观测掩码下进行训练，并能够在丰富散射和有限散射信道模型下进行推理，从而推断未观测端口的信道和干扰。

Result: 仿真结果表明，当观测端口数$M$超过空间自由度（DoF）时，信道$h$、接收信号$r$和干扰$I$的重构归一化均方误差（NMSE）可降至$10^{-4}$量级。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过Copula建模端口间统计依赖关系，用户终端可以在仅观测少量端口的情况下高精度地重构未观测端口的信道与干扰信息，从而避免了传统FAMA中不切实际的"全端口理想干扰已知"假设，为FAMA的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Fast fluid antenna multiple access (FAMA) is an idea that promises to overcome severe interference in massive access scenarios by reconfiguring the antenna's position at the receiver side on a symbol-by-symbol basis, without the need of precoding nor any other interference mitigation techniques. However, this idea is commonly studied under a \emph{genie-aided} premise: each user terminal (UT) can probe \emph{all} fluid-antenna ports in every symbol instance and ideally knows the instantaneous signal-interference split for the received signals at all the ports. Such assumption is unrealistic since it implies impractical hardware and switching limits, pilot overhead, as well as an unknown ability to determine the signal-interference split. This paper revisits the fast FAMA communication problem and asks a key question: can a UT act \emph{as if} it had full per-port interference knowledge while observing only a small fraction of ports? To this end, we propose a \emph{copula-aided FAMA} framework that learns the joint dependence structure of the complex triplets $(r_k,h_k,I_k)$ across ports, where $r_k$, $h_k$ and $I_k$ denote, respectively, the received signal, the channel coefficient and the aggregate interference signal at the $k$-th port, and uses this learned model to infer unobserved channels and interference. Concretely, we devise an attention-copula time-series model that is trained under random partial-observation masks and evaluated under both rich and finite-scattering channel models. Simulation results indicate that the reconstruction normalized mean-square-error (NMSE) for $h$, $r$, and $I$ drops to the order of $10^{-4}$ once the number of observed ports, $M$, exceeds the spatial degrees of freedom (DoF).

中文摘要翻译: 快速流体天线多址接入（FAMA）是一种有望通过接收端逐符号重配置天线位置来克服大规模接入场景中严重干扰的技术，无需预编码或任何其他干扰缓解技术。然而，这一思路通常是在"神助"假设下研究的：每个用户终端（UT）在每个符号时刻都能探测所有流体天线端口，并理想地知道所有端口接收信号中的瞬时信号-干扰分解信息。这种假设不现实，因为它意味着不可实际的硬件和切换限制、导频开销，以及未知的信号-干扰分解判断能力。本文重新审视了快速FAMA通信问题，并提出一个关键问题：用户终端能否在仅观测少量端口的情况下，表现得如同拥有全部端口的干扰信息一样？为此，我们提出了一个Copula辅助的FAMA框架，该框架学习各端口间复数三元组$(r_k, h_k, I_k)$的联合依赖结构，其中$r_k$、$h_k$和$I_k$分别表示第$k$个端口的接收信号、信道系数和聚合干扰信号，并利用学习到的模型推断未观测的信道和干扰。具体地，我们设计了一个注意力-Copula时间序列模型，该模型在随机部分观测掩码下进行训练，并在丰富散射和有限散射信道模型下进行评估。仿真结果表明，一旦观测端口数$M$超过空间自由度（DoF），信道$h$、接收信号$r$和干扰$I$的重构归一化均方误差（NMSE）可降至$10^{-4}$量级。

</details>


### [99] [A Measurement-Based Parameterization of Physics Reflection Models for Terahertz Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23795)
*Taihao Zhang,Chenzhou Lin,Cunhua Pan,Hong Ren,Ruyi Liu,Yongchao He,Tian Qiu,Bingchang Hua,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对300-400 GHz太赫兹通信频段，建立了信道测量平台，提出了一种SLI-EPLD反射系数模型，通过子带建模和参数化映射策略精确表征材料反射系数随频率的变化，并引入WF-TREND算法实现高精度参数拟合，在多种材料上优于现有模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 太赫兹通信中反射系数的精确建模对于开发可靠的通道模型至关重要，而现有模型在300-400 GHz高频段的精度不足。

Method: 1) 建立300-400 GHz信道测量平台进行材料反射系数测量；2) 提出SLI-EPLD（单层干扰扩展参数化Lorentz/Drude）模型，采用子带建模策略表征反射系数随频率的变化，并利用参数化映射方法保证模型参数稳定性；3) 引入WF-TREND（加权子带拟合趋势回归）算法实现精确的子带参数拟合。

Result: 验证结果表明，所提模型在多种材料上的性能优于现有模型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立的反射系数模型为300-400 GHz高频太赫兹通信的信道建模提供了关键基础。

Original Abstract: The accurate modeling of reflection coefficients is pivotal for developing reliable channel models in emerging terahertz (THz) communications. This study establishes a 300$\sim$400 GHz channel measurement platform to measure the reflection coefficients of various materials. Based on the analysis of measured data, we propose the single-layer interference with an extended-parameterized Lorentz/Drude (SLI-EPLD) reflection coefficient model. In this model, a sub-band modeling strategy is adopted to characterize the variation of reflection coefficients with frequency, while a parameterized mapping approach is employed to ensure the stability of model parameters. Furthermore, the weighted sub-band fitting for trend regression (WF-TREND) algorithm is introduced to achieve precise sub-band parameter fitting. Validation results demonstrate superior performance to existing models across multiple materials. The reflection coefficient model established in this work serves as a critical foundation for channel modeling in 300$\sim$400 GHz for high-THz communication.

中文摘要翻译: 反射系数的精确建模对于新兴太赫兹通信中可靠信道模型的开发至关重要。本研究搭建了300~400 GHz的信道测量平台，用于测量多种材料的反射系数。基于实测数据分析，我们提出了单层干扰扩展参数化Lorentz/Drude（SLI-EPLD）反射系数模型。该模型采用子带建模策略来表征反射系数随频率的变化，同时利用参数化映射方法确保模型参数的稳定性。此外，引入了加权子带拟合趋势回归（WF-TREND）算法以实现精确的子带参数拟合。验证结果表明，该模型在多种材料上的性能优于现有模型。本工作建立的反射系数模型为高频太赫兹通信在300~400 GHz频段的信道建模奠定了关键基础。

</details>


### [100] [Ray-Tracing vs. 3GPP TDL: Power Delay Profile Analysis in Outdoor-to-Indoor and Indoor Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23831)
*Julia Andrusenko,Chloe Makdad*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文对比了3GPP TR 38.901 TDL信道模型与确定性射线追踪模型（Remcom Wireless InSite）在密集城市室外到室内（O2I）场景下的功率延迟分布（PDP），发现3GPP TDL模型的统计平均方法无法捕捉站点特定的精细多径结构，确定性或混合方法更适用于站点特定的物理层设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3GPP TR 38.901信道模型被广泛用于物理层设计和系统级评估，但这些模型基于集合平均统计，忽略了站点特定的几何信息。本文旨在评估这种统计平均方法在密集城市O2I场景下与确定性射线追踪模型之间的差异，探讨其局限性。

Method: 使用Remcom Wireless InSite软件进行确定性射线追踪，在华盛顿特区密集城市O2I场景和代表性单层室内布局下生成PDP，并与3GPP TR 38.901 TDL信道模型在匹配链路距离和非视距（NLOS）条件下进行比较。所有Wireless InSite PDP均进行功率归一化，以比较相对多径延迟结构。评估指标包括均方根（RMS）延迟扩展、平均超额延迟、有效最大延迟和KL散度。

Result: 3GPP TDL模型通常表现出更长的延迟扩展，且往往无法捕捉到确定性、站点特定特征，如晚期到达的能量和不规则尖峰。TDL模型在某些情况下可以近似主要信道特征，但由于依赖集合平均统计而非几何信息，限制了其对精细多径结构的表征能力。

Conclusion: 3GPP TDL模型适用于大规模系统评估，但对于站点特定的物理层设计，确定性或混合方法更为合适。

Original Abstract: 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) Technical Report (TR) 38.901 channel models (Releases 15-19) are widely used for physical-layer design and system-level evaluation in dense urban outdoor-to-indoor (O2I) and indoor environments. These models capture ensemble-averaged channel statistics but do not account for site-specific geometry. In this paper, we compare Power Delay Profiles (PDPs) derived from a deterministic ray-tracing model (Remcom Wireless InSite software) with those from the 3GPP TR 38.901 Tapped Delay Line (TDL) channel models. This comparative analysis is performed using a dense urban O2I scenario and a representative single-story indoor layout modeled in Washington, D.C., under matched link-distance and Non-Line-of-Sight (NLOS) conditions. All Wireless InSite PDPs are power-normalized to enable comparison of relative multipath delay structure.
  We evaluate root-mean-square (RMS) delay spread, mean excess delay, effective maximum delay, and Kullback-Leibler (KL) distribution divergence. Results indicate that 3GPP TDL models generally exhibit longer delay spreads and often fail to capture deterministic, site-specific features such as late-arriving energy and irregular spikes. While TDL models can approximate primary channel features in some cases, their reliance on ensemble-averaged statistics rather than geometry limits their representation of fine multipath structures. We conclude that while 3GPP TDL models are suitable for large-scale system evaluation, deterministic or hybrid approaches are more appropriate for site-specific physical-layer design.

中文摘要翻译: 第三代合作伙伴计划（3GPP）技术报告（TR）38.901信道模型（第15-19版）广泛用于密集城市室外到室内（O2I）和室内环境的物理层设计及系统级评估。这些模型捕获了集合平均信道统计特性，但未考虑站点特定几何信息。本文比较了基于确定性射线追踪模型（Remcom Wireless InSite软件）导出的功率延迟分布（PDP）与3GPP TR 38.901抽头延迟线（TDL）信道模型中的PDP。该比较分析是在密集城市O2I场景和华盛顿特区建模的代表性单层室内布局下，在匹配链路距离和非视距（NLOS）条件下进行的。所有Wireless InSite PDP均经过功率归一化，以便比较相对多径延迟结构。我们评估了均方根（RMS）延迟扩展、平均超额延迟、有效最大延迟和Kullback-Leibler（KL）分布散度。结果表明，3GPP TDL模型通常表现出更长的延迟扩展，且往往无法捕获确定性、站点特定的特征，如晚期到达的能量和不规则尖峰。虽然TDL模型在某些情况下可以近似主要信道特征，但其依赖于集合平均统计而非几何信息，限制了其对精细多径结构的表征能力。我们得出结论，3GPP TDL模型适用于大规模系统评估，但对于站点特定的物理层设计，确定性或混合方法更为合适。

</details>

### [101] [STAMBRIDGE: Spectral-Temporal Amplitude-aware Mid-Feature Bridge for EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23137)
*Jiahe Meng,Weiming Zeng,Yueyang Li,Bo Chai,Hongjie Yan,Zhiguo Zhang,Wai Ting Siok,Nizhuan Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出STAMBRIDGE两阶段框架，通过谱-时幅度感知调制（STAM）提取稳健EEG特征，再通过中阶特征语义桥（MFSB）实现跨模态对齐，在THINGS-EEG基准上取得34.50% Top-1和65.95% Top-5零样本检索精度，并支持语义一致的图像重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低信噪比的EEG信号与高度结构化的视觉-语言空间之间存在模态差异，直接进行跨模态对齐不稳定。

Method: 提出两阶段框架STAMBRIDGE：1) 谱-时幅度感知调制（STAM），用幅度导出的软通道权重替代硬频率掩码，结合多尺度时间卷积，保留频率感知瞬态并减少时域振铃伪影；2) 中阶特征语义桥（MFSB），通过定向跨模态交互构建正则化的中间空间，实现分阶段蒸馏和更稳定的语义对齐。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG基准上，200路零样本检索Top-1准确率34.50%，Top-5准确率65.95%；学到的嵌入可结合扩散模型生成语义一致的图像重建。

Conclusion: STAMBRIDGE通过分阶段处理特征条件化和跨模态对齐，有效缩小了EEG与视觉-语言空间之间的模态鸿沟，实现了稳健的EEG到视觉语义对齐。

Original Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) visual decoding remains challenging due to the modality gap between low-SNR neural signals and highly structured vision--language spaces, making direct cross-modal alignment unstable. To address this, we propose STAMBRIDGE, a versatile two-stage framework that sequentially tackles feature conditioning and cross-modal alignment. First, we introduce a Spectral-Temporal Amplitude-aware Modulation (STAM) to extract well-conditioned EEG representations. By replacing hard frequency masking with amplitude-derived soft channel weighting and multi-scale temporal convolutions, STAM explicitly preserves frequency-aware transients while reducing the risk of time-domain ringing artifacts. Building upon these robust neural features, we further introduce a model-agnostic Mid-Feature Semantic Bridge (MFSB) that constructs a regularized intermediate space through directed cross-modal interactions, enabling staged distillation and more stable semantic alignment. Experiments on the THINGS-EEG benchmark show competitive 200-way zero-shot retrieval performance, with 34.50\% Top-1 and 65.95\% Top-5 accuracy. In addition, embeddings learned by STAMBRIDGE produce semantically coherent image reconstructions with a diffusion model, demonstrating robust EEG-to-vision semantic alignment. The code is available at: https://github.com/thabeatmjh/STAMBRIDGE.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图（EEG）视觉解码仍然具有挑战性，因为低信噪比的神经信号与高度结构化的视觉-语言空间之间存在模态差异，使得直接的跨模态对齐不稳定。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了STAMBRIDGE，一个通用的两阶段框架，该框架依次处理特征条件化和跨模态对齐。首先，我们引入了一种谱-时幅度感知调制（STAM）来提取条件良好的EEG表示。通过用幅度导出的软通道权重替换硬频率掩码，并结合多尺度时间卷积，STAM在降低时域振铃伪影风险的同时，明确保留了频率感知的瞬态特征。在这些稳健的神经特征基础上，我们进一步引入了一种模型无关的中阶特征语义桥（MFSB），该桥通过定向跨模态交互构建一个正则化的中间空间，从而实现分阶段蒸馏和更稳定的语义对齐。在THINGS-EEG基准上的实验展示了具有竞争力的200路零样本检索性能，Top-1准确率达到34.50%，Top-5准确率达到65.95%。此外，STAMBRIDGE学到的嵌入能够与扩散模型结合产生语义一致的图像重建，展示了稳健的EEG到视觉语义对齐能力。代码已在https://github.com/thabeatmjh/STAMBRIDGE开源。

</details>


### [102] [Discontinuous Galerkin Neural Operator for Pathology Defocus Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23282)
*Shaoqing Duan,Haofei Song,Xintian Mao,Qingli Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 针对病理显微镜图像去模糊中由位置依赖积分成像导致的空间变化和非局部连续模糊问题，本文提出不连续伽辽金神经算子（DGNO），通过元素局部体积算子和界面数值通量参数化积分核，在保持物理一致性的同时实现局部性、异质性和全局一致性的统一，在多项指标上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 病理显微镜中的散焦模糊呈现空间变化和局部不连续特性，而现有深度学习方法受限于平移不变性假设和可解释性不足，难以处理这种异质模糊模式。现有神经算子架构依赖全局参数化核，假设平滑性和平稳性，同样无法建模异质和局部不连续的模糊模式。

Method: 提出不连续伽辽金神经算子（DGNO），采用不连续伽辽金公式对积分核进行参数化，包含元素局部体积算子和界面数值通量两个核心组件，在保持光学图像形成物理原理的同时，实现了局部性、异质性建模和全局一致性的有机结合。

Result: 大量实验表明，DGNO在多个方面超越现有最先进方法，包括：更清晰的图像重建结果、对空间变化模糊的鲁棒处理以及可扩展的高分辨率性能。

Conclusion: DGNO通过不连续伽辽金公式有效解决了病理显微镜中异质散焦模糊的建模难题，为低层视觉任务中的神经算子设计提供了新的物理启发的范 paradigm。

Original Abstract: Defocus deblurring in pathological microscopy remains challenging due to the spatially varying and locally discontinuous nature of optical blur induced by a position-dependent integral imaging process.
  Existing deep learning methods, constrained by shift-invariance assumptions and limited interpretability, are not well suited to such heterogeneous blur patterns.
  Neural operators provide a principled alternative by modeling defocus formation directly as an integral operator, offering a new perspective on defocus deblurring.
  However, most existing neural operator architectures for low-level vision rely on globally parameterized kernels that assume smoothness and stationarity, limiting their ability to model heterogeneous and locally discontinuous blur patterns.
  To address this limitation, we propose the Discontinuous Galerkin Neural Operator (DGNO), which parameterizes the integral kernel using a discontinuous Galerkin formulation with element-local volume operators and interface numerical fluxes.
  DGNO provides a principled combination of locality,
  heterogeneity modeling, and global coherence while preserving the underlying physics
  of optical image formation.
  Extensive and insightful experiments demonstrate that DGNO surpasses state-of-the-arts, delivering sharper reconstructions, robust handling of spatially varying blur, and scalable high-resolution performance. The code will be released at https://github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Single-Image-Deblur.

中文摘要翻译: 病理显微镜中的散焦去模糊仍然具有挑战性，这是因为由位置依赖积分成像过程引起的光学模糊具有空间变化和局部不连续的特性。现有的深度学习方法受限于平移不变性假设和有限的可解释性，难以很好地适应这种异质模糊模式。神经算子通过将散焦形成直接建模为积分算子提供了一种原则性的替代方案，为散焦去模糊提供了新的视角。然而，现有面向低级视觉的大多数神经算子架构依赖于假设平滑性和平稳性的全局参数化核，限制了它们对异质和局部不连续模糊模式的建模能力。为了解决这一局限，我们提出不连续伽辽金神经算子（DGNO），该算子使用不连续伽辽金公式，通过元素局部体积算子和界面数值通量对积分核进行参数化。DGNO在保持光学图像形成的底层物理原理的同时，提供了局部性、异质性建模和全局一致性的有机结合。广泛而深入的实验表明，DGNO超越了现有最先进方法，实现了更清晰的重建、对空间变化模糊的鲁棒处理以及可扩展的高分辨率性能。代码将在 https://github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Single-Image-Deblur 发布。

</details>


### [103] [Efficient Learned Image Compression without Entropy Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23323)
*Hao Cao,Wenqi Guo,Zhijin Qin,Jungong Han*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出EF-LIC，一种无需熵编码的端到端学习图像压缩框架，通过无约束向量量化和上下文条件自回归变换消除统计冗余与相关冗余，实现了与带熵编码的LIC相当的压缩性能，同时编码速度提升3倍以上、解码速度提升5倍以上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统学习图像压缩中的熵编码是顺序操作，成为编码延迟的瓶颈。为了克服这一延迟问题，本文提出无需熵编码的学习图像压缩框架。

Method: 1) 引入无约束向量量化（Unconstrained VQ），证明其索引分布趋近最大熵界，从而最小化统计冗余；2) 提出上下文条件自回归变换（Context-conditioned Autoregressive Transform），通过直接重参数化潜变量来减少潜变量间的相互依赖性，从而消除相关冗余。

Result: 在Kodak数据集上，以LPIPS为感知度量，EF-LIC相比MS-ILLM实现了最高67.86%的比特率降低。消融实验表明，EF-LIC达到了与基于熵编码的变体相当的压缩性能，同时编码速度提升3倍以上，解码速度提升5倍以上。

Conclusion: EF-LIC证明了在不使用熵编码的情况下，通过设计合理的量化与变换方法，可以同时实现低延迟和高压缩性能，为学习图像压缩提供了新的方向。

Original Abstract: Entropy coding is widely used in typical learned image compression (LIC) that converts latents into a compact bitstream. However, entropy coding is typically sequential and becomes the coding latency bottleneck. To overcome it, we present Entropy-Coding Free Learned Image Compression (EF-LIC), a multi-rate framework that generates compact representation by removing statistical and correlation redundancy with low coding latency. First, we introduce unconstrained vector quantization and prove that its index distribution approaches the maximum-entropy bound, yielding minimal statistical redundancy. Second, we propose a context-conditioned autoregressive transform that directly reparameterizes the latents to reduce inter-dependency. Theoretical analysis shows that EF-LIC can remove correlation redundancy as effectively as typical LIC with entropy coding, leading to comparable compression performance. Experiments show EF-LIC achieves up to 67.86% bitrate reduction over MS-ILLM on Kodak with LPIPS. Ablation studies further show EF-LIC matches the compression performance of its entropy-coding based variant while achieving over $3\times$ faster encoding and $5\times$ faster decoding.

中文摘要翻译: 熵编码被广泛应用于典型的学习图像压缩（LIC）中，用于将潜变量转换为紧凑的比特流。然而，熵编码通常是顺序执行的，并成为编码延迟的瓶颈。为克服这一问题，我们提出了免熵编码学习图像压缩（EF-LIC），一种多码率框架，通过消除统计冗余和相关冗余来生成紧凑表示，同时实现低编码延迟。首先，我们引入无约束向量量化，并证明其索引分布趋近最大熵界，从而产生最小的统计冗余。其次，我们提出一种上下文条件自回归变换，通过直接重参数化潜变量来减少变量间的相互依赖性。理论分析表明，EF-LIC在去除相关冗余方面可以达到与使用熵编码的典型LIC相当的效果，从而获得可比的压缩性能。实验结果表明，在Kodak数据集上以LPIPS为度量，EF-LIC相比MS-ILLM实现了高达67.86%的比特率降低。消融研究进一步表明，EF-LIC在压缩性能上与基于熵编码的变体相当，同时实现了超过3倍的编码加速和超过5倍的解码加速。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Latent Cache Flow: Model-to-Model Communication Without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22863)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent Cache Flow (LCF)，一种通过联合翻译和压缩KV缓存实现LLM智能体间高效通信的方法，相比文本通信和C2C方法在效率和准确性上均有显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM智能体间文本通信存在延迟大、信息损失高的问题；C2C方法虽尝试直接交换KV缓存，但适配器过大、训练成本高，且要求目标上下文完全相同，不适用于智能体间上下文不同的实际场景。

Method: LCF方法通过联合翻译和压缩键值对，将适配器大小降至C2C的约4%；同时设计适配器仅传输目标模型缺失的新信息摘要，从而适配不同上下文场景。

Result: 在共享上下文场景下，13MB的LCF适配器比956MB的C2C适配器更准确；在不同上下文场景下，LCF相比文本通信准确率提升23%，速度提升8.5倍。

Conclusion: LCF通过压缩联合翻译和选择性信息传输，实现了轻量、高效且适用于不同上下文的LLM智能体间通信，显著优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: LLM agents today communicate via text, which incurs considerable latency and information loss due to the need to autoregressively decode the sharer model's state and encode at the receiver model. Recent work such as Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026) seeks to exchange KV caches by learning adapters that translate sharer KV matrices to the receiver model. However, the adapters are large and expensive to train, and translate individual tokens, which requires the target context to be identical. This is unsuitable for agent communication, where the LLMs have differing context.
  We introduce Latent Cache Flow (LCF). To address efficiency, we observe that keys and values can be jointly translated and compressed, reducing the adapter to about 4% of C2C's size. To address differing context, we design the adapter to transmit a summary of new information that the target model does not have. Our early experiments show that a 13 MB LCF adapter can be more accurate than a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for different contexts, LCF is 23% more accurate and 8.5x faster than text-based communication.

中文摘要翻译: 如今的LLM智能体通过文本进行通信，这需要自回归解码分享模型的状态并在接收模型端进行编码，从而带来显著的延迟和信息损失。近期如Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026)等工作尝试通过学习适配器将分享模型的KV矩阵转换为接收模型，从而交换KV缓存。然而，这些适配器体积庞大且训练成本高昂，且仅逐词元进行翻译，要求目标上下文完全一致。这对于LLM智能体间的通信并不适用，因为智能体的LLM所处的上下文各不相同。我们提出潜在缓存流（Latent Cache Flow, LCF）。为了解决效率问题，我们观察到键和值可以联合翻译和压缩，从而将适配器大小降至C2C的约4%。为了解决上下文不同的问题，我们将适配器设计为仅传输目标模型所缺失的新信息摘要。我们的初步实验表明，在共享上下文场景中，13MB的LCF适配器比956MB的C2C适配器更准确；在不同上下文场景中，LCF相比文本通信准确率提升23%，速度提升8.5倍。

</details>


### [105] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用MLP层更新轨迹的几何特征（11个尺度不变几何特征）构建稀疏线性探针，用于语言模型结构化输出时的不确定性量化，在选择性弃权任务上优于最大softmax概率（MSP），AURC最高提升21个点，且每个特征具有闭合几何意义，可解释错误在深度维度的形成过程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 最大softmax概率（MSP）虽简单但校准不佳；现有探针方法将原始隐藏状态输入黑盒分类器，将激活值视为静态快照，忽略了表示在层间形成的过程性轨迹。相似的端点可能源自不同的路径，而证据在深度维度上的累积、强化或反转过程可能揭示最终概率所掩盖的不确定性。

Method: 提取11个尺度不变的几何特征，追踪逐层MLP更新的累积路径，将这些特征输入到稀疏线性探针中进行不确定性量化。

Result: 稀疏线性探针在选择性弃权任务上优于MSP，性能提升幅度与基线校准误差成正比，AURC最高提升21个点。每个特征具有闭合几何意义，探针系数可追溯错误在深度维度的形成过程，包括哪些层过早承诺、哪些层与运行状态矛盾、轨迹在何处偏离端点。

Conclusion: 通过追踪层间表示轨迹的几何特征进行不确定性量化，不仅性能优于MSP，还能提供可解释的分析，揭示不确定性在模型深度维度的演化过程。

Original Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

中文摘要翻译: 在评估结构化输出的语言模型生成的不确定性量化时，最大softmax概率（MSP）是一种默认方法。虽然计算成本低，但MSP往往校准不佳。现有探针模型内部激活的方法将原始隐藏状态输入到不透明分类器中，将激活值视为静态快照，忽略了表示形成的逐层轨迹。然而，相似的端点可能源自截然不同的路径，证据在深度维度上如何累积、增强或反转，可能揭示最终概率所掩盖的不确定性。我们提取了11个尺度不变的几何特征，追踪逐层MLP更新的累积路径，并将其输入到稀疏线性探针中。在选择性弃权任务中，该探针优于MSP，性能提升幅度与基线校准误差成正比，AURC最高提升21个点。由于每个特征都具有闭合形式的几何意义，探针的系数可以追溯错误在深度维度上如何以及何处形成——哪些层过早地做出承诺，哪些层与运行状态相矛盾，轨迹在何处偏离其端点。

</details>


### [106] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionSense 是一种面向能量受限自主边缘系统的融合感知智能传感框架，通过轻量级近传感器分类器与边缘-服务器协同训练，在降低计算与通信开销的同时保持任务质量，相比单模态滤波器实现了最高33倍的能耗降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态传感器（如相机、LiDAR/深度传感器）在边缘系统中日益普及，但现有方法要么在强大服务器上进行融合，要么对每类模态单独使用近传感器滤波器，忽略了跨模态依赖关系，导致冗余传输或事件漏检。亟需一种融合感知的轻量级边缘滤波方法。

Method: 提出三步训练流程：(i) 服务端融合模型学习下游任务；(ii) 生成"安全过滤"(FoS) 标签，量化每个模态对融合决策的必要性；(iii) 通过将近传感器预测作为辅助信号注入，压缩边缘侧融合模型。运行时决策层联合减少计算与通信开销。

Result: 在SynDrone双模态（RGB+Depth/LiDAR）实验中，FusionSense在保持任务质量的同时实现了远高于单模态滤波器的数据缩减率：在1% FoI出现率下能耗降低33倍，10%下降低11倍；固定30%数据缩减时质量损失减少92.3%；能耗节省比最佳先验滤波基线高出约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense通过融合感知的智能传感框架，有效解决了边缘多模态系统中的能耗-质量权衡问题，证明了利用跨模态依赖关系进行近传感器滤波的可行性与高效性。

Original Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 自主系统和智能工业部署日益将计算任务分散到近传感器、边缘和云端资源中，而严格的能量、延迟和可靠性预算要求运行时自适应性。在实践中，决定在每个节点计算和传输什么至关重要；然而，随着多模态传感器套件（相机、LiDAR/深度传感器等）在边缘端激增，大多数现有方法要么（i）在强大服务器上融合模态，要么（ii）应用忽略跨模态依赖关系的单模态近传感器滤波器，导致冗余传输或事件漏检。本文提出FusionSense，一种面向能量受限自主边缘系统的融合感知智能传感框架。轻量级近传感器分类器通过三步流程训练：（i）服务端融合模型学习下游任务，（ii）生成"安全过滤"（FoS）标签以量化每个模态相对于融合决策的必要性，（iii）通过将近传感器预测作为辅助信号注入来压缩边缘侧融合模型。其结果是形成一个运行时决策层，能够联合减少计算与通信开销，且复杂度随传感器数量线性扩展。在基于SynDrone的双模态（RGB+Depth/LiDAR）实验中，FusionSense在保持任务质量的同时实现了远高于单模态滤波器的数据缩减率，并带来了显著的端到端收益：在1% FoI出现率下能耗降低33倍，10%下降低11倍，固定30%数据缩减时质量损失减少92.3%，能耗节省比最佳先验滤波基线高出约1.5倍。

</details>


### [107] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuRA提出了一种基于块张量训练分解的全秩微调框架，通过将核心矩阵固定为预训练的块状SVD基、仅优化紧凑核心和奇异值，实现了全秩谱预处理，在保持与LoRA相当参数效率的同时，多项任务上超越了全参数微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全参数微调和LoRA等参数高效微调方法在更新权重时未考虑预训练阶段建立的谱结构，导致有限微调数据中的噪声梯度会扰动鲁棒的预训练特征。作者识别出谱预处理是缺失的关键要素。

Method: 提出FuRA框架，采用块张量训练分解 W = LSR，其中大核心L固定为预训练的块状SVD基，仅优化紧凑核心R和块状奇异值S。该设计同时提供全秩谱预处理、保持全秩更新的表达能力，并实现与LoRA相当的参数、内存和步时效率。

Result: 在LLM微调（LLaMA-3-8B常识推理+1.37）、LLM强化学习数学推理、VLM视觉指令微调等多个设置中一致超越全参数微调。4-bit量化变体QFuRA也超越QLoRA。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用预训练的谱结构进行谱预处理，全秩微调可以在不增加额外参数开销的情况下超越全参数微调，为高效微调提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

中文摘要翻译: 全参数微调（Full FT）和LoRA等参数高效微调方法在引入权重更新时，均未考虑预训练阶段建立的谱结构。因此，来自有限微调数据的噪声梯度会扰动鲁棒的预训练特征。我们发现谱预处理是缺失的关键要素：通过每个权重矩阵的全秩奇异值分解（SVD）重新参数化，并冻结一个奇异基，将更新约束在预训练的列空间内，产生了一种预条件优化方案，在相同可训练参数数量下优于无约束的全参数微调。基于这一见解，我们提出了FuRA（全秩适应），一种基于块张量训练分解 W = LSR 的高效全秩适应框架，其中大核心L固定为预训练的块状SVD基，仅优化紧凑核心R和块状奇异值S。该设计同时提供全秩谱预处理、保持全秩更新的表达能力，并实现与LoRA相当的参数、内存和步时效率。FuRA在多个设置中一致超越全参数微调，包括LLM微调（LLaMA-3-8B常识推理+1.37）、LLM强化学习数学推理以及VLM视觉指令微调。此外，4-bit量化变体QFuRA也超越了QLoRA。代码见 https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [108] [MedExpMem: Adapting Experience Memory for Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22872)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Yannian Gu,Winnie Chiu Wing Chu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MedExpMem，一种让医学视觉语言模型通过记忆自身诊断失败经验来积累鉴别诊断能力的框架，在11个亚专科的放射学基准上最高提升7.0%准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型（VLM）的参数编码静态知识，无法像经验丰富的医生一样通过临床实践积累鉴别诊断经验，缺乏区分易混淆疾病的能力。

Method: 提出MedExpMem经验记忆框架，包含两阶段构建：初始实践暴露知识缺口，反思性重新诊断优化理解；将诊断失败经验编码为成对鉴别笔记（含关键鉴别特征、可操作决策规则和推理错误模式），在新病例中检索经验记忆指导鉴别推理。

Result: 在覆盖11个亚专科的放射学基准上，多个模型和规模下均取得一致的准确率提升，最高提升7.0%；分析实验验证了经验质量和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedExpMem是一种有竞争力的方法，能够解决参数化学习无法覆盖的医学适应需求。

Original Abstract: Experienced physicians develop diagnostic expertise through clinical practice, acquiring not only disease knowledge but also the ability to differentiate confusable conditions. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) lack this capability -- their parameters encode static knowledge that does not evolve across diagnostic encounters. We propose MedExpMem, an experience memory framework enabling VLM-based diagnostic agents to accumulate differential diagnosis expertise. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation, which retrieves encyclopedic disease descriptions, MedExpMem memorizes discriminative experience derived from the agent's own diagnostic failures and organizes them as pairwise differential notes encoding key discriminators, actionable decision rules and reasoning error patterns. The framework adopts a two-phase construction process mirroring physician learning: initial practice exposes knowledge gaps, and reflective re-diagnosis refines understanding. When encountering new cases, the agent retrieves experience memory to guide differential reasoning. We evaluate MedExpMem on a radiology benchmark spanning 11 subspecialties. Results demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements, maximum 7.0%, across diverse models and scales. Analytical experiments validate experience quality and robustness, demonstrating MedExpMem as a competitive method addresses medical adaptation needs beyond the reach of parameteric learning.

中文摘要翻译: 经验丰富的医生通过临床实践获得诊断专长，不仅掌握疾病知识，还能区分易混淆的病症。当前的医学视觉语言模型（VLM）缺乏这种能力——它们的参数编码了静态知识，无法在诊断过程中演化。我们提出MedExpMem，一种经验记忆框架，使基于VLM的诊断智能体能够积累鉴别诊断经验。与检索增强生成（检索百科全书式的疾病描述）不同，MedExpMem记忆从智能体自身诊断失败中获得的判别性经验，并将其组织为成对鉴别笔记，编码关键鉴别特征、可操作的决策规则和推理错误模式。该框架采用两阶段构建过程，模拟医生的学习方式：初始实践暴露知识缺口，反思性重新诊断优化理解。当遇到新病例时，智能体检索经验记忆以指导鉴别推理。我们在一个覆盖11个亚专科的放射学基准上评估了MedExpMem。结果表明，在多种模型和规模下，准确率均有持续提升，最高提升7.0%。分析实验验证了经验质量和鲁棒性，表明MedExpMem作为一种有竞争力的方法，能够解决参数化学习无法覆盖的医学适应需求。

</details>


### [109] [When Do LLMs Reason? A Dynamical Systems View via Entropy Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22873)
*Wei Xia,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG
