Method: 提出经验贝叶斯共形预测框架，使用r值将得分变异性转化为不确定性感知的非一致性得分。r值估计候选者潜在得分在考虑均值与不确定性后属于排名靠前组的概率。包含闭式正态-正态经验贝叶斯估计器和非参数后验采样估计器两种实现。

Result: 在图像分类、CLIP视觉语言模型基准和大型语言模型上，r值共形预测在保持目标覆盖的同时提高了排名稳定性，在变异性提供信息时减少了预测集大小，并在变异性消失时退化为标准CP行为。

Conclusion: r值共形预测能够有效利用得分变异性来改进共形预测的质量，在保持统计保证的前提下减少不稳定的候选纳入，是一种实用且理论上可证明的改进方法。

Original Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) gives distribution-free coverage for modern vision and language models, but it is often forced to make a ranking decision from a single unstable nonconformity score. Standard CP uses one realization, while average-then-calibrate variants smooth multiple realizations into a point estimate. Both options discard the inconsistency that can help identify whether a candidate is indeed stable. A weak answer can enter the conformal set even if the evidence is not strong, simply because one posterior sample or prompt phrasing made it look strong. But variability can help distinguish a stable signal from noise-driven fluctuations. We describe an empirical Bayes conformal prediction framework that uses $r$-values to convert score variability into an uncertainty informed nonconformity score. The resulting $r$-value estimates how likely a candidate's latent score belongs to the top-ranked group after accounting for both its mean score and its uncertainty. It admits both a closed-form Normal-Normal empirical Bayes estimator and a nonparametric posterior-sampling estimator. Using the $r$-value as the nonconformity score preserves the target conformal coverage while provably reducing the inclusion of high variance false candidates under mild regularity conditions. Across image classification, CLIP-based VLM benchmarks, and LLMs, we show that $r$-value conformal prediction preserves target coverage while improving ranking stability and reducing set size when variability is informative, and reverting to CP-like behavior when variability vanishes.

中文摘要翻译: 共形预测为现代视觉和语言模型提供了无分布假设的覆盖保证，但它通常被迫从单个不稳定的非一致性得分中做出排名决策。标准CP使用单次实现，而先平均后校准的变体将多次实现平滑为点估计。这两种方式都丢弃了有助于识别候选是否真正稳定的不一致性信息。一个弱答案可能仅仅因为某次后验样本或提示措辞使其看起来很强，就能够进入共形集。但变异性可以帮助区分稳定信号与噪声驱动的波动。我们描述了一种经验贝叶斯共形预测框架，使用r值将得分变异性转化为不确定性感知的非一致性得分。得到的r值估计了在考虑其均值得分和不确定性后，候选者潜在得分属于排名靠前组的可能性有多大。它既包含闭式正态-正态经验贝叶斯估计器，也包含非参数后验采样估计器。将r值作为非一致性得分，在温和的正则条件下，能在保持目标共形覆盖的同时，可证明地减少高方差假候选的纳入。在图像分类、基于CLIP的视觉语言模型基准和大型语言模型上，我们展示了r值共形预测在保持目标覆盖的同时，在变异性具有信息性时提高了排名稳定性并减少了预测集大小，并在变异性消失时退化为类似CP的行为。

</details>


### [140] [Label-Efficient Dataset Pruning via Semi-Supervised Pseudo-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23198)
*Yeseul Cho,Baekrok Shin,Changmin Kang,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SemiPrune，一种标签高效的数据集剪枝框架，仅需少量随机标注子集，利用半监督学习生成伪标签，使现有监督剪枝方法能应用于未标注数据，在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法大多需要全量标注数据，而现实场景中未标注数据丰富且标注成本高昂；无标签剪枝方法依赖预训练模型提取特征，当目标数据集与预训练分布差异较大时可靠性不足。

Method: SemiPrune仅使用少量随机标注子集，通过半监督学习为未标注数据生成伪标签，使需要标签信息的监督剪枝方法能无缝应用于伪标签训练集；然后从伪标签诱导的训练动态中估计样本难度并选择核心子集。

Result: 在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上，SemiPrune在无标签和标签高效的基线方法中达到最优性能，同时在标准基准上表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过直接从目标数据集学习，SemiPrune能更好地捕捉目标分布，为样本难度估计和核心子集选择提供更可靠的信号，在标签稀缺场景下有效实现了数据集剪枝。

Original Abstract: Dataset pruning reduces the storage and training costs of deep learning by selecting an informative subset from a large dataset. However, most existing pruning methods require fully labeled data, which limits their applicability in realistic settings where unlabeled data are abundant and annotation is costly. Recent label-free pruning methods address this issue, but they rely on features from pretrained models to estimate example difficulty. This dependence can be unreliable when the target dataset differs substantially from the pretraining distribution. We propose SemiPrune, a label-efficient dataset pruning framework, using only a small randomly labeled subset, that uses semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled data, allowing existing supervised pruning methods that require label information to be seamlessly applied to the resulting pseudo-labeled training pool. We then estimate example difficulty from pseudo-label-induced training dynamics and select a coreset. By learning directly from the target dataset, our method better captures the target distribution and provides more reliable signals for difficulty estimation and coreset selection. We validate our approach on domain-specific, image-corrupted, and long-tailed datasets, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance among label-free and label-efficient baselines, while also demonstrating competitive performance on standard benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集剪枝通过从大型数据集中选择信息丰富的子集来减少深度学习的存储和训练成本。然而，大多数现有的剪枝方法需要完全标注的数据，这在未标注数据丰富且标注成本高昂的现实场景中限制了它们的适用性。最近的无标签剪枝方法解决了这一问题，但它们依赖预训练模型提取的特征来估计样本难度。当目标数据集与预训练分布存在显著差异时，这种依赖关系可能不可靠。我们提出SemiPrune，一种标签高效的数据集剪枝框架，仅使用少量随机标注的子集，利用半监督学习为未标注数据生成伪标签，使得需要标签信息的现有监督剪枝方法能够无缝应用于生成的伪标签训练池。然后，我们从伪标签诱导的训练动态中估计样本难度并选择核心子集。通过直接从目标数据集学习，我们的方法能更好地捕捉目标分布，为样本难度估计和核心子集选择提供更可靠的信号。我们在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上验证了该方法，其在无标签和标签高效的基线方法中达到了最优性能，同时在标准基准上也展现出具有竞争力的表现。

</details>


### [141] [FastKernels: Benchmarking GPU Kernel Generation in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23215)
*Gabriele Oliaro,Yichao Fu,May Jiang,Owen Lu,Junli Wang,Zhihao Jia,Hao Zhang,Samyam Rajbhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有GPU kernel基准测试与生产环境严重脱节，导致基于LLM的agent优化方向错误。本文提出FastKernels——一个包含46个代表性架构的基准测试，同时可作为生产级推理框架，实验表明最强agent仅达到0.94×加速比，证实基准-生产不匹配是核心瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GPU kernel基准测试存在三大问题：仅用合成输入在单GPU上评估、忽略编译栈、奖励复现已知优化而非发现新优化。这导致agent在沙盒中高分但在实际系统中引入接口不兼容、编译栈冲突和静默正确性退化，亟需与生产环境对齐的基准测试。

Method: 构建一个包含8大类46个代表性架构的最小kernel基准测试，其kernel覆盖96.2%（409/425）的HuggingFace Transformers架构。同时将FastKernels设计为生产级推理框架，每个任务的接口对齐该架构族最先进库的对应模块，支持优化kernel直接部署到生产代码库。

Result: 在主流LLM服务上与vLLM、SGLang等成熟系统性能持平，在欠服务架构上显著超越上游参考。评估最强kernel agent仅达到0.94×加速比，较弱agent为0.78×和0.53×，确认基准-生产不匹配是关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: FastKernels作为通往生产可用kernel agent的垫脚石，确保基准测试上的增益直接转化为生产吞吐量提升。代码已开源。

Original Abstract: LLM-based agents for GPU kernel generation are advancing rapidly, yet their progress is fundamentally constrained by the benchmarks they optimize against. Existing benchmarks are poorly aligned with production inference frameworks: they evaluate kernels on a single GPU with synthetic inputs, ignore the surrounding compilation stack, and reward replicating known optimizations rather than discovering new ones. The resulting reward signals are misleading: agents learn to generate kernels that score well in sandboxes but introduce interface incompatibilities, compilation-stack conflicts, and silent correctness degradation when integrated into real systems. We introduce FastKernels, a kernel benchmark built around a minimal set of 46 representative architectures spanning 8 categories, whose kernels collectively subsume those of 96.2% (409/425) of HuggingFace Transformers architectures. FastKernels doubles as a minimalistic, production-grade inference framework that runs at parity with hardened systems such as vLLM and SGLang on mainstream LLM serving and substantially exceeds upstream references on under-served architectures; each task's interface mirrors the corresponding module in the state-of-the-art library for its architecture family, enabling direct deployment of optimized kernels into production codebases. Evaluating state-of-the-art kernel agents on FastKernels, we find that even the strongest agent achieves only 0.94$\times$ aggregate speedup over production baselines, with weaker agents at $0.78\times$ and $0.53\times$ -- confirming that benchmark-production misalignment is a critical bottleneck for the field. We release FastKernels as a stepping stone toward kernel agents whose benchmark gains translate directly into production throughput improvements. Code is available at https://github.com/Snowflake-AI-Research/fastkernels

中文摘要翻译: 基于大语言模型的GPU内核生成智能体正在快速发展，但其进展从根本上受限于它们所优化的基准测试。现有基准测试与生产推理框架严重脱节：它们在单GPU上使用合成输入评估内核，忽略周围的编译栈，并奖励复制已知优化而非发现新优化。由此产生的奖励信号具有误导性：智能体学会了生成在沙盒中得分高、但在集成到真实系统时会引入接口不兼容、编译栈冲突和静默正确性退化的内核。我们提出FastKernels，这是一个基于8大类46个代表性架构的最小化内核基准测试，这些内核共同涵盖了96.2%（409/425）的HuggingFace Transformers架构。FastKernels同时充当最小化的生产级推理框架，在主流LLM服务上与vLLM和SGLang等成熟系统性能持平，且在欠服务架构上显著超越上游参考；每个任务的接口对齐其架构族最先进库中的对应模块，支持将优化后的内核直接部署到生产代码库中。在FastKernels上评估最先进的内核智能体，我们发现即使最强的智能体也只达到生产基准0.94倍的总体加速比，较弱智能体为0.78倍和0.53倍——证实了基准-生产不匹配是该领域的关键瓶颈。我们开源FastKernels，作为通往基准增益能直接转化为生产吞吐量提升的内核智能体的垫脚石。代码已开源在https://github.com/Snowflake-AI-Research/fastkernels

</details>


### [142] [PaP-NF: Probabilistic Long-Term Time Series Forecasting via Prefix-as-Prompt Reprogramming and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23219)
*Minju Kim,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PaP-NF提出了一种基于前缀提示机制对齐连续时间序列表示与冻结的大语言模型，并利用归一化流解码器进行概率预测，在长期预测基准上稳健捕获多模态不确定性并保持竞争性的点预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法大多依赖确定性模型，无法有效量化现实环境中固有的不确定性和复杂未来行为，单一值预测不足以满足实际需求，亟需能够表示和量化不确定性的概率预测方法。

Method: 提出PaP-NF概率预测框架，采用Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与冻结的大语言模型对齐，并利用大语言模型提取的全局上下文条件化归一化流解码器，生成预测分布。

Result: 在多个长期预测基准上，PaP-NF稳健地捕获了多模态不确定性，同时保持了具有竞争力的点预测精度，使用连续排名概率评分（CRPS）作为评估指标。

Conclusion: PaP-NF能有效结合大语言模型的全局上下文理解能力与归一化流的灵活分布建模能力，实现高质量的概率时间序列预测，在不确定量化和点预测准确性之间取得了良好平衡。

Original Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a central role in many real-world applications and has been extensively studied. Most existing approaches rely on deterministic models. However, real-world environments exhibit inherently uncertain and complex future behaviors, making single-point predictions insufficient. This highlights the need for probabilistic forecasting methods that can quantify and represent uncertainty. In this work, we propose PaP-NF, a probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns continuous time series representations with a frozen large language model (LLM) using a Prefix-as-Prompt mechanism, and conditions a normalizing flow decoder on the global context extracted by the LLM. The quality of the resulting predictive distributions is evaluated using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS), a standard metric in probabilistic forecasting. Across a variety of long-term forecasting benchmarks, PaP-NF robustly captures multi-modal uncertainty while maintaining competitive point forecasting accuracy. The official implementation is available at: https://github.com/democracy04/PaP-NF

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测在众多现实应用中扮演着核心角色，并已得到广泛研究。大多数现有方法依赖于确定性模型。然而，现实环境表现出固有的不确定性和复杂的未来行为，使得单点预测不足以满足需求。这凸显了需要能够量化和表示不确定性的概率预测方法。在本文中，我们提出了PaP-NF，一个概率预测框架，它使用前缀作为提示（Prefix-as-Prompt）机制将连续的时序表示与冻结的大语言模型（LLM）对齐，并将归一化流解码器以大语言模型提取的全局上下文为条件。所得预测分布的质量使用连续排名概率评分（CRPS）进行评估，这是概率预测中的标准指标。在各种长期预测基准上，PaP-NF在保持竞争性点预测准确性的同时，稳健地捕获了多模态不确定性。官方实现代码可在 https://github.com/democracy04/PaP-NF 获取。

</details>


### [143] [WMAttack: Automated Attack Search for Adversarial Evaluation of World-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23220)
*Zhixiang Guo,Siyuan Liang,Shi Fu,Cheng Guo,Andras Balogh,Mark Jelasity,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMAttack，一个面向世界模型智能体的自动化攻击搜索框架，用于评估其对抗鲁棒性。通过自校正攻击搜索（SCAS）和表征引导攻击检索（RGAR），在有限预算下高效发现更强攻击，实验表明在Atari和DMC任务上显著优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 世界模型作为决策智能体被广泛使用，但其对抗鲁棒性因缺乏专用自动化评估方法而研究不足。现有方法要么依赖手动调参导致鲁棒性高估，要么穷举搜索因需闭环展开而计算成本过高。

Method: WMAttack将鲁棒性评估建模为有限预算下的攻击配置搜索问题，包含攻击族、扰动预算、优化步数、重启次数和分配规则等配置维度。核心包括：(1) SCAS，利用奖励下降、动作不稳定、运行时成本和展开变异性等反馈信号迭代细化攻击提议分布；(2) RGAR，从表征相似任务中检索历史有效配置，为未见环境提供热启动。

Result: 在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack一致发现比基线更强的攻击：DreamerV3 Atari上归一化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034，DMC上从0.319提升至0.682。消融实验表明RGAR提升初始候选质量，SCAS在固定评估预算下提升最终攻击效用。

Conclusion: WMAttack是一种高效且准确的自动化世界模型对抗鲁棒性评估框架，通过SCAS和RGAR在有限搜索预算下发现更强的攻击，理论分析也表明提议细化能在有限预算搜索中提升攻击效用。

Original Abstract: Despite the growing use of world models as decision-making agents, their adversarial robustness remains underexplored due to the lack of dedicated automated evaluation methods. A key obstacle is that attack evaluation must be both accurate and efficient: weak manually tuned attacks can overestimate robustness, while exhaustive hyperparameter search is prohibitively expensive because each candidate requires closed-loop rollouts through learned latent dynamics. We introduce WMAttack, an automated attack-search framework for adversarial evaluation of world-model agents. WMAttack formulates robustness evaluation as a finite-budget search over attack configurations, including attack families, perturbation budgets, optimization steps, restarts, and allocation rules. To improve search accuracy, Self-Correcting Attack Search (SCAS) refines the attack proposal distribution using feedback from reward degradation, action instability, runtime cost, and rollout variability. To improve search efficiency, Representation-Guided Attack Retrieval (RGAR) retrieves effective historical configurations from representation-similar tasks, providing a warm start for unseen environments. We provide a theoretical explanation showing that proposal refinement improves finite-budget search when it shifts probability mass toward high-utility attacks. Across Atari and DeepMind Control tasks, WMAttack consistently discovers stronger attacks than the evaluated baselines, improving normalized reward drop from 0.497 to 1.034 on DreamerV3 Atari and from 0.319 to 0.682 on DMC. Ablations further show that RGAR improves initial candidate quality and SCAS improves final attack utility under fixed evaluation budgets.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管世界模型作为决策智能体的应用日益增多，但由于缺乏专用的自动化评估方法，其对抗鲁棒性仍未被充分探索。一个关键障碍在于攻击评估必须同时兼顾准确性和效率：弱手工调参的攻击可能高估鲁棒性，而穷举超参数搜索因每个候选都需通过所学潜动力学进行闭环展开而计算成本过高。本文提出WMAttack，一个面向世界模型智能体对抗评估的自动化攻击搜索框架。WMAttack将鲁棒性评估建模为有限预算下的攻击配置搜索问题，搜索空间涵盖攻击族、扰动预算、优化步数、重启次数和分配规则。为提升搜索精度，自校正攻击搜索（SCAS）利用奖励下降、动作不稳定、运行时成本和展开变异性等反馈信号细化攻击提议分布。为提升搜索效率，表征引导攻击检索（RGAR）从表征相似任务中检索历史有效配置，为未见环境提供热启动。本文的理论分析表明，当提议细化将概率质量向高效用攻击转移时，能改善有限预算搜索的效果。在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack一致发现比基线更强的攻击，在DreamerV3 Atari上将归一化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034，在DMC上从0.319提升至0.682。消融实验进一步表明，RGAR提升了初始候选质量，SCAS在固定评估预算下提升了最终攻击效用。

</details>


### [144] [Assessing Predictive Models for Fairness Based on Movement Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23234)
*Francesco Lettich,Mario A. Nascimento,Chiara Pugliese,Chiara Renso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将空间公平性的概念从单一居住地扩展到个体的移动模式，并设计了一种结合多尺度空间划分和扫描统计的方法来检测预测模型基于移动模式的不公平性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空间公平性研究假设每个人只属于单一地理位置（如居住地），忽略了个体在不同区域间的移动模式也会导致不公平，因此需要将空间公平性概念泛化到包含移动模式。

Method: 首先将个体的移动轨迹关联到多个不同分辨率和对齐方式的空间分区中，然后采用合适的空间扫描统计量来评估预测模型是否基于移动模式存在不公平性。

Result: 在数千个合成不公平数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效检测这种新型不公平性，并能检索出被不公平对待的对象集合；定位性能表现出一致的多分辨率权衡。

Conclusion: 有必要将空间公平性推广到包含个体移动模式，所提方法能有效检测基于移动模式的不公平性，但定位效果存在多分辨率权衡。

Original Abstract: Assessing the spatial fairness of predictive models involves establishing whether they are statistically penalizing (favoring) individuals associated with certain geographical locations. Literature on this topic makes the fundamental assumption that each individual is assigned to a single geographical location (e.g., place of residence). However, fairness with respect to the set of locations where one has been, i.e., their movement patterns over different regions, also matters when fairness is considered. Consequently, we argue that it is necessary to generalize the notion of spatial fairness to also include movement patterns, leading to the novel problem of assessing predictive models for fairness relative to the movements of individuals. To deal with this problem, we propose an approach that first associates the movements of individuals to certain geographic regions, considering multiple spatial partitions with different resolutions and alignments, and then employs a suitable spatial scan statistic to assess whether a predictive model is fair based on movement patterns. In the experimental evaluation, we study the performance of our approach over thousands of synthetic unfair datasets, showing that it is effective at detecting this new type of unfairness and at retrieving the set of objects treated unfairly, while localization performance exhibits a consistent multi-resolution trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 评估预测模型的空间公平性涉及确定模型是否在统计上惩罚（或偏好）与某些地理位置相关的个体。关于该主题的文献都基于一个基本假设：每个个体被分配到一个单一的地理位置（例如居住地）。然而，当考虑公平性时，个体所到过的位置集合——即他们在不同区域间的移动模式——同样重要。因此，我们认为有必要将空间公平性的概念泛化到包含移动模式，这引出了一个新问题：评估预测模型相对于个体移动模式的公平性。为了解决该问题，我们提出了一种方法：首先将个体的移动轨迹关联到特定地理区域，考虑多个具有不同分辨率和对齐方式的空间划分，然后采用合适的空间扫描统计量来评估预测模型是否基于移动模式而公平。在实验评估中，我们在数千个合成不公平数据集上研究了该方法的性能，结果表明它能有效检测这种新型不公平性，并能检索出被不公平对待的对象集合，而定位性能则表现出一致的多分辨率权衡。

</details>


### [145] [Convex Low-resource Accent-Robust Language Detection in Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23235)
*Miria Feng,William Tan,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Convex Language Detection (CLD)框架，利用凸优化技术解决低资源场景下口语对话系统对不标准方言和口音的识别失败问题，通过多GPU ADMM算法实现快速训练，在低资源场景下达到97-98%的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全球化与多元文化导致语音多样性增加，但现有口语对话系统在低资源方言和口音上频繁失败，常误判输入语言，引发下游对话任务的级联错误。标准微调方法计算开销大且易在高维语音数据上过拟合。

Method: 提出Convex Language Detection (CLD)框架，将理论上可证明的凸优化技术集成到口语对话系统流程中。通过JAX实现多GPU交替方向乘子法（ADMM）进行高效训练，提供全局最优性保证和多项式时间内的快速训练。

Result: 理论上证明了凸目标函数可诱导经认证的边距稳定性并提供特征扰动下的保证。实验上展示了样本效率和对输入方言变体的鲁棒性，在挑战性的低资源场景下达到97-98%的准确率。

Conclusion: CLD框架有效解决了低资源方言和口音的语种检测难题，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势，开源包已发布。

Original Abstract: Globalization and multiculturalism continue to produce increasingly diverse speech varieties. Yet current spoken dialogue systems frequently fail on under-represented dialects and accents, often misidentifying the input language and causing cascading failures in downstream dialogue tasks. Addressing this dialectal variance under low-resource constraints remains an open challenge, as standard fine-tuning is computationally expensive and prone to overfitting on high-dimensional speech data. We propose Convex Language Detection (CLD), a novel framework that integrates theoretically grounded convex optimization techniques into the spoken dialogue systems pipeline. Our method is efficiently implemented via multi-GPU Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) in JAX, thus providing global optimality guarantees and fast training in polynomial time. Theoretically, we prove that our convex objective induces certified margin stability and provide guarantees against feature perturbations. Empirically, we demonstrate sample efficiency and robustness to input dialectical variation, achieving 97-98% accuracy in challenging low-resource regimes. Our open-source package is available at https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

中文摘要翻译: 全球化和多元文化不断催生日益多样的语音变体。然而，当前的口语对话系统在处理代表性不足的方言和口音时频繁失败，常常误判输入语言，从而在下游对话任务中引发级联错误。在低资源约束下解决这种方言差异仍然是一个开放挑战，因为标准微调方法计算成本高昂，且容易在高维语音数据上过拟合。本文提出凸性语种检测（CLD），一种新颖的框架，将具有理论基础的凸优化技术集成到口语对话系统流程中。我们的方法通过JAX中基于多GPU的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）高效实现，从而提供全局最优性保证和多项式时间内的快速训练。理论上，我们证明了凸目标函数可以诱导经认证的边距稳定性，并提供了针对特征扰动的保证。实验上，我们展示了样本效率和对输入方言变体的鲁棒性，在挑战性的低资源场景下达到了97-98%的准确率。我们的开源包可在 https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/ 获取。

</details>


### [146] [RelPrism: A Multi-Faceted Pre-training Framework with Self-Generated Tasks for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23241)
*Jinyu Yang,Cheng Yang,Junze Chen,Zedi Liu,Muhan Zhang,Hanyang Peng,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RelPrism，一种面向关系数据库的多方面自监督学习框架，通过构造内在、关系和混合属性并进行多粒度聚类生成伪任务池进行预训练，在分类和回归任务上均取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的关系数据库自监督学习方法通常仅从单一层面（如节点级内在属性或子图级关系结构）获取监督信号，无法覆盖下游任务对多方面信息（如交互模式、用户属性等）的异构需求，导致预训练表征的泛化能力有限。

Method: RelPrism首先从不同视角构造内在属性、关系属性和混合属性，然后对每种属性进行多粒度聚类，形成对应的伪任务池；通过在这些伪任务池上进行预训练，使表征暴露于更广泛的视角和粒度层级，从而为下游任务提供更强的表征基础。

Result: 在5个真实数据集上的14个任务中，RelPrism在分类任务上提升ROC-AUC 4.15%，在回归任务上降低MAE 10.75%，均超越当前最先进基线。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，通过多角度、多粒度的自监督预训练能够有效提升关系数据库表征学习的质量，满足下游任务对多方面信息的需求。

Original Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the cornerstone of modern data systems and support diverse predictive tasks. Recent relational deep learning (RDL) methods enable end-to-end prediction by converting RDBs into graphs, where rows are represented as nodes and inter-table interactions are represented as edges, and then applying graph-based models for representation learning. Despite the strong capability of RDL, effective self-supervised pre-training for RDBs remains non-trivial. RDB tasks often require multi-faceted information across different perspectives and granularities. For example, user churn classification may rely more on interaction patterns, whereas consumption value prediction requires both user-item behaviors and intrinsic user attributes for fine-grained regression. Such heterogeneous needs challenge RDB representation learning, as pre-training objectives should cover comprehensive information for downstream adaptation. However, existing SSL methods typically derive supervision from a single facet, such as node-level intrinsic attributes or subgraph-level relational structures, providing limited adaptability. To this end, we propose RelPrism, a multi-faceted self-supervised learning framework for RDBs. RelPrism constructs intrinsic, relational, and hybrid attributes from distinct perspectives, and applies multi-granularity clustering to each perspective to form corresponding pseudo-task pools. Pre-training over these pools exposes representations to broader perspectives and granularity levels, yielding a stronger basis for downstream adaptation. Experiments on 14 tasks across 5 real-world datasets show that RelPrism improves ROC-AUC by 4.15% for classification and reduces MAE by 10.75% for regression over state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism.

中文摘要翻译: 关系数据库（RDB）仍然是现代数据系统的基石，支撑着多样化的预测任务。最近的 relational deep learning（RDL）方法通过将关系数据库转换为图（其中行表示为节点，表间交互表示为边），然后应用基于图的模型进行表示学习，从而实现端到端预测。尽管RDL能力强大，但如何对关系数据库进行有效的自监督预训练仍然具有挑战性。关系数据库任务通常需要跨不同视角和粒度的多方面信息。例如，用户流失分类可能更依赖于交互模式，而消费价值预测则需要用户-物品行为与用户内在属性相结合以进行细粒度的回归。这种异构需求对关系数据库表示学习构成了挑战，因为预训练目标应覆盖全面的信息以便下游任务适应。然而，现有的自监督学习方法通常从单一层面获取监督信号（例如节点级的内在属性或子图级的关系结构），适应性有限。为此，我们提出 RelPrism，一种面向关系数据库的多方面自监督学习框架。RelPrism 从不同视角构造内在属性、关系属性和混合属性，并对每种属性进行多粒度聚类，形成对应的伪任务池。在这些伪任务池上进行预训练，使表征暴露于更广泛的视角和粒度层级，为下游任务适应提供更强的基础。在5个真实数据集上的14个任务实验中，RelPrism 在分类任务上较最先进基线提升了4.15%的ROC-AUC，在回归任务上降低了10.75%的MAE。我们的代码可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism 获取。

</details>


### [147] [Learning-Augmented Online Scheduling with Parsimonious Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23255)
*Mugen Blue,Sungjin Im,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了学习增强调度中算法性能与抢占复杂度之间的权衡，在仅需每任务常数次抢占的条件下实现了O(1) competitive算法，并将理论框架扩展到非关联和可塑机器等更现实的场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有学习增强调度工作主要优化任务延迟，但依赖频繁的"盲目"抢占，忽略了算法性能与抢占复杂度之间的根本权衡。本文旨在填补这一空白，在限制抢占次数的同时优化延迟。

Method: 提出了具有O(1) competitive比的调度算法，在单机和并行机场景下每任务仅需O(1)次抢占，抢占开销随预测误差对数增长。同时将有限抢占保证扩展到非关联机器和可塑机器。

Result: 在准确预测下实现了O(1) competitive算法与O(1)每任务抢占次数；抢占开销随预测误差对数缩放；为非关联和可塑机器提供了首个有限抢占保证；实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 延迟下界与抢占开销之间的差距可以通过坚实的分析基础来弥合，学习增强框架可以扩展到更受限和更现实的调度场景中，同时维持有限抢占保证。

Original Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have emerged as a powerful paradigm to surpass traditional worst-case lower bounds by integrating potentially noisy predictions. While this framework has seen success in online scheduling, existing work primarily optimizes job latency while relying on frequent, ``blind'' preemptions. This ignores the fundamental trade-off between algorithmic performance and preemption complexity. We provide the first systematic study of learning-augmented scheduling that curbs preemption while optimizing latency. We establish that the gap between theoretical latency bounds and preemption overhead can be bridged with solid analytical foundations. Our results include $O(1)$-competitive algorithms for single and unrelated parallel machines with only $O(1)$ preemptions per job under accurate predictions, with overhead scaling logarithmically with the prediction error. By providing the first bounded-preemption guarantees for unrelated and malleable machines, we extend the theoretical reach of the learning-augmented framework to more constrained and realistic settings. Finally, our algorithms are validated through experiments.

中文摘要翻译: 学习增强算法已成为一种强大的范式，通过整合潜在有噪声的预测来超越传统的悲观最坏情况下界。虽然这一框架在在线调度中取得了成功，但现有工作主要优化任务延迟，同时依赖频繁的"盲目"抢占。这忽略了算法性能与抢占复杂度之间的根本权衡。我们首次系统研究了在限制抢占的同时优化延迟的学习增强调度问题。我们证明，理论延迟界与抢占开销之间的差距可以通过坚实的分析基础来弥合。我们的成果包括：在准确预测下，针对单机和无关并行机器实现O(1) competitive算法，每任务仅需O(1)次抢占，且抢占开销随预测误差对数增长。通过为无关机器和可塑机器提供首个有限抢占保证，我们将学习增强框架的理论范围扩展到更受限和更现实的场景。最后，我们通过实验验证了算法的有效性。

</details>


### [148] [A Simple Plug-in for Improving Eviction-Based KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23258)
*Yuping Lin,Jiayuan Ding,Yue Xing,Pengfei He,Jiliang Tang,Subhabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VECTOR 是一种即插即用的增强方法，通过引入保留、近似和驱逐的三路令牌路由机制，利用可重建性信号恢复被二值驱逐策略丢弃的有用值信息，从而改善大语言模型长上下文推理中 KV 缓存的质量-内存权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于二值驱逐或表示近似的方法未能充分利用那些虽非精确保留所必需但仍可重建的令牌，导致有价值信息被不可逆地丢失。

Method: VECTOR 在驱逐型流水线中引入三路令牌路由：保留（retention）、近似（approximation）和驱逐（eviction）。它结合了基础评分器的重要性信号与离线校准的基于回归的值估计所提供的可重建性信号，根据两者对令牌进行分流处理。

Result: 实验结果表明，VECTOR 在中高压缩率下改善了质量-内存权衡，在更严格的预算约束下收益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 通过利用可重建性信号，VECTOR 能够恢复被二值驱逐策略不可逆丢失的有用值信息，同时保留关键向量以保证注意力路由的稳定性。

Original Abstract: KV cache growth is a major bottleneck for long-context inference in large language models. Existing methods are often dominated by binary eviction or representation approximation, which may underutilize tokens that are not critical for exact retention but are still reconstructable. We present VECTOR, a plug-and-play augmentation for eviction-based pipelines that introduces three-way token routing: retention, approximation, and eviction. VECTOR combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation. By leveraging reconstructability, VECTOR recovers useful value information that would otherwise be irreversibly lost under binary eviction, while preserving key vectors for attention routing stability. Experimental results show that VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression, with especially clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

中文摘要翻译: KV 缓存的增长是大规模语言模型长上下文推理的主要瓶颈。现有方法通常以二值驱逐或表示近似为主，这可能导致那些并非精确保留所必需但仍可重建的令牌未能得到充分利用。我们提出 VECTOR，一种针对基于驱逐的流水线的即插即用增强方法，引入了三路令牌路由：保留、近似和驱逐。VECTOR 将来自基础评分器的重要性信号与来自离线校准的基于回归的值估计所提供的可重建性信号相结合。通过利用可重建性，VECTOR 恢复了在二值驱逐下将被不可逆丢失的有用值信息，同时保留关键向量以保证注意力路由的稳定性。实验结果表明，VECTOR 在中高压缩率下改善了质量-内存权衡，在更严格的预算约束下收益尤为明显。

</details>


### [149] [When Good Equations Get Bad Scores: Improving Symbolic Regression Through Better Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23272)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Huang,Runxiang Wang,Ziwen Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 符号回归中“好结构、差分数”现象是优化瓶颈，SAGE-Fit通过利用符号表达的结构和语义先验设计专用模块，作为即插即用模块显著提升了评估保真度和多种SR系统的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有符号回归方法采用双层优化框架，内环因非线性算子导致高度非凸，依赖BFGS等快速局部求解器常陷入不良局部最小值，使得正确结构得到低估的分数（“好结构、差分数”现象），严重降低了搜索效率和误导搜索方向。

Method: 提出SAGE-Fit，一个符号回归原生拟合框架，利用符号表达式独有的结构和语义先验，针对每种属性设计专属模块，从而缓解优化瓶颈。该方法可作为即插即用模块集成到现有SR系统中。

Result: 大量实验表明，SAGE-Fit作为即插即用模块显著提升了评估保真度，并在多种符号回归系统上实现了普遍性的性能改进。

Conclusion: SAGE-Fit通过挖掘符号回归特有的结构和语义先验，有效解决了参数拟合质量导致的评估偏差问题，是一种通用且高效的SR优化增强方案。

Original Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) plays a central role in scientific knowledge discovery by distilling mathematical equations from observational data. Most existing SR methods function within a bi-level optimization framework: an outer loop that searches for the discrete equation structure, and an inner loop that optimizes the continuous parameters of that structure. Crucially, parameter-fitting quality directly determines a structure's score and thus the outer-loop search. However, nonlinear operators make the inner loop highly non-convex, and budget-driven reliance on fast local solvers (e.g., BFGS) often yields poor local minima and underestimated scores for correct structures. This ``Good Structure, Bad Score'' phenomenon becomes a key bottleneck, degrading efficiency and misguiding the search away from the true equation. To resolve this, we propose SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator for Symbolic Regression), an SR-native fitting framework that exploits the dual native priors of symbolic expressions. By capitalizing on the structural and semantic priors unique to SR, we design tailored modules for each property, thereby effectively mitigating this optimization bottleneck. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach, as a plug-and-play module, significantly enhances evaluation fidelity and universally improves the performance of various SR systems.

中文摘要翻译: 符号回归（SR）通过从观测数据中提炼数学方程，在科学知识发现中发挥着核心作用。现有大多数SR方法采用双层优化框架：外层循环搜索离散方程结构，内层循环优化该结构的连续参数。关键的是，参数拟合质量直接决定结构的评分，进而影响外层搜索。然而，非线性算子使得内层循环高度非凸，且受预算驱动依赖快速局部求解器（如BFGS）常常导致不良局部最小值，从而低估正确结构的分数。这种"好结构，差分数"现象成为关键瓶颈，降低搜索效率并误导搜索偏离真实方程。为解决此问题，我们提出SAGE-Fit（结构感知与语义引导的符号回归评估器），一个利用符号表达式双生原生先验的SR原生拟合框架。通过利用SR特有的结构和语义先验，我们针对每种属性设计专属模块，从而有效缓解这一优化瓶颈。大量实验表明，我们的方法作为即插即用模块，显著提升了评估保真度，并普遍提高了多种SR系统的性能。

</details>


### [150] [Prudent-Banker: No Extra Fees for Baseline Safety in Adversarial Bandits With and Without Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23351)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prudent-Banker算法，在对抗性多臂老虎机中（含/不含延迟反馈）同时实现极小极大最优最坏情况遗憾和相对于安全基线的常数遗憾，首次达到最优安全-鲁棒权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在即时反馈和平滑比较器下可以平衡安全与探索，但任意延迟会扰乱从保守到探索的切换时机，危及安全保障。亟需一种能处理任意延迟反馈同时保持安全保证的算法。

Method: 提出Prudent-Banker算法，结合延迟适应的在线镜像下降变体与改进的分阶段攻击机制。核心创新是延迟校准的重启阈值，严格考虑未观测反馈导致的最坏情况失真，并可靠检测比较器次优性。

Result: 理论：伪遗憾$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$且对安全比较器的遗憾为$\widetilde{O}(1)$，同时建立了安全约束下延迟对抗老虎机的新下界，证明该界限不可改进（对数因子内）。实验：在多种延迟分布下，Prudent-Banker比标准延迟鲁棒基线更好地平衡了安全与学习。

Conclusion: Prudent-Banker是首个在有无延迟反馈下均能实现最优安全-鲁棒权衡的算法，填补了延迟反馈场景下安全性保障的理论空白。

Original Abstract: We study adversarial multi-armed bandits with and without delayed feedback under a safety-aware goal: achieving minimax-optimal worst-case regret while keeping nearly constant regret relative to a designated "safe" baseline policy. Existing approaches can balance this trade-off with immediate feedback for smooth comparators, but arbitrary delays can mistime transitions between conservatism and exploration, endangering the safety guarantee. To bridge this gap, we propose Prudent-Banker, a novel algorithm that combines a delay-adapted variant of Online Mirror Descent with a modified phased-aggression mechanism. Its key technical contribution is a delay-calibrated restart threshold that rigorously accounts for the worst-case distortion induced by unobserved feedback and reliably detects comparator suboptimality. We also establish new lower bounds for safety-constrained adversarial delayed bandits, showing that the regret guarantees of Prudent-Banker are unimprovable, up to logarithmic factors, under the baseline-safety requirement. To the best of our knowledge, Prudent-Banker is the first algorithm to achieve the optimal safety--robustness trade-off: pseudo-regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$ together with $\widetilde{O}(1)$ regret against the safe comparator, both with and without delays. Experiments across diverse delay distributions show that, unlike standard delay-robust baselines, Prudent-Banker effectively balances safety and learning.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究在有无延迟反馈下的对抗性多臂老虎机问题，目标兼顾安全性：在实现极小极大最优最坏情况遗憾的同时，相对于指定的"安全"基线策略保持近乎恒定的遗憾。现有方法在即时反馈和平滑比较器下可平衡这一权衡，但任意延迟可能扰乱从保守到探索的切换时机，危及安全保证。为填补这一空白，我们提出Prudent-Banker，一种新颖的算法，它将延迟适应的在线镜像下降变体与改进的分阶段攻击机制相结合。其关键技术贡献是延迟校准的重启阈值，该阈值严格考虑了未观测反馈引起的最坏情况失真，并能可靠检测比较器的次优性。我们还为安全约束下的对抗性延迟老虎机建立了新的下界，表明Prudent-Banker的遗憾保证在基线安全要求下是不可改进的（至多对数因子）。据我们所知，Prudent-Banker是首个在有无延迟情况下均能实现最优安全-鲁棒权衡的算法：伪遗憾$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$，同时对安全比较器的遗憾为$\widetilde{O}(1)$。跨多种延迟分布的实验表明，与标准的延迟鲁棒基线相比，Prudent-Banker能有效平衡安全性与学习。

</details>


### [151] [Instance-Optimal Estimation with Multiple LLM Judges on a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23362)
*Junghyun Lee,Sanghwa Kim,Yassir Jedra,Alexandre Proutière,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定预算下，如何将评估查询分配给不同成本和可靠性的裁判（LLM-as-a-judge）以最小化评分估计误差，提出EST-IVWE自适应算法并证明其达到局部极小极大最优下界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估协议在不同裁判（judge）间存在价格和可靠性差异，且每个提示-响应对的难度不同。在固定预算下，如何最优分配评估查询以获取最准确的评分估计是一个基础但尚未被形式化研究的问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为预算异方差多裁判估计（Budgeted Heteroskedastic Multi-Judge Estimation）。分析逆方差加权估计量（IVWE）并推导最优分配的oracle策略；针对方差未知的实际情况，提出EST-IVWE自适应算法，利用乐观有偏方差估计来稳定经验分配。理论方面采用Assouad型期望论证（而非Fano型高概率论证）推导局部极小极大下界。

Result: 理论上证明EST-IVWE在预算上达到oracle IVWE的速率（仅差低阶项），并建立了匹配的局部极小极大下界，证明算法具有实例最优性。数值实验在合成数据和HelpSteer2数据集上验证了该方法优于朴素均匀分配。

Conclusion: 本文提出的EST-IVWE算法在预算受限的多裁判评估场景下能够实现接近理论最优的评分估计精度，且通过局部极小极大下界证明了其最优性，为LLM评估中的资源分配提供了理论保障和实用方案。

Original Abstract: Evaluating large language models increasingly relies on LLM-as-a-judge protocols, but such evaluations remain costly: different judges have different prices and reliabilities, and the difficulty of each prompt-response pair can vary substantially. This raises a basic allocation question: under a fixed budget, how should one distribute evaluation queries across heterogeneous judges and instances to obtain the most accurate score estimates? We formalize this question as *budgeted heteroskedastic multi-judge estimation*. Given $K$ prompt-response pairs, $J$ judges with known costs, and unknown query-judge variances, the goal is to estimate a bounded score vector while minimizing an $\ell_p$-error. Our first contribution is to analyze the inverse-variance weighted estimator (IVWE) and to derive the oracle allocation that minimizes its error rate. Since this allocation depends on the unknown variances, we then address the practical unknown-variance setting by proposing EST-IVWE, an adaptive algorithm that constructs and leverages *optimistically biased* variance estimates to stabilize the empirical allocation. We prove that EST-IVWE matches the oracle IVWE rate up to lower-order terms in the budget. Our second and central theoretical contribution is a matching *local* minimax lower bound, which establishes the instance-optimality of the proposed algorithms. A key technical insight is that Fano-type high-probability arguments are too coarse for this problem: their packing construction loses the local variance structure that governs the optimal allocation. We instead use an Assouad-type in-expectation argument, based on local perturbations, which preserves this structure and yields the sharp allocation-dependent lower bound. Finally, we numerically validate the superiority of our approach over naïve uniform allocation on synthetic and HelpSteer2 datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 评估大语言模型越来越依赖于LLM-as-a-judge协议，但这种评估代价高昂：不同裁判具有不同的价格和可靠性，且每个提示-响应对的难度差异显著。这引出了一个基本的分配问题：在固定预算下，如何将评估查询分配给异质的裁判和实例，以获得最准确的评分估计？我们将该问题形式化为*预算异方差多裁判估计*。给定$K$个提示-响应对、$J$个成本已知但查询-裁判方差未知的裁判，目标是估计有界评分向量并最小化$\ell_p$误差。我们的第一个贡献是分析逆方差加权估计量（IVWE），并推导使误差率最小化的oracle分配方案。由于该分配依赖于未知方差，我们随后针对实际中方差未知的情况，提出EST-IVWE自适应算法，该算法构建并利用*乐观有偏*方差估计来稳定经验分配。我们证明EST-IVWE在预算上匹配oracle IVWE的速率，仅差低阶项。我们的第二个也是核心的理论贡献是一个匹配的*局部*极小极大下界，确立了所提算法的实例最优性。一个关键的技术洞见是：Fano型高概率论证对该问题过于粗糙——其打包构造失去了支配最优分配的局部方差结构。我们转而使用基于局部扰动的Assouad型期望论证，该论证保留了该结构并导出了锐利的分配相关下界。最后，我们在合成数据集和HelpSteer2数据集上通过数值实验验证了我们的方法相对于朴素均匀分配的优越性。

</details>


### [152] [Every Component is a Lookup: Token Attribution and Composition from a Single Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23393)
*Po-Kai Chen,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unpack方法，通过反向递归分解Transformer中注意力层和MLP层的贡献，实现无需干预、梯度或辅助训练即可获得组件间端到端路径及词元级归因，并在间接宾语识别任务中验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Transformer机制可解释性方法难以刻画组件之间的组合计算路径，需要一种无需干预或辅助训练即可分解注意力与MLP层贡献、并揭示组件间交互强度的技术。

Method: 利用注意力和MLP共享的键-值模板φ(S)U结构，设计Unpack反向递归算法，通过单次前向传播分解两个子层中的贡献，生成带有K/Q/V组合标签的端到端路径以及每个词元的归因分数。

Result: 在GPT-2 small上成功恢复了Wang et al. (2023)描述的全部三种组合连接及其模式特异性路由（K、Q或V）；在词元级归因中，同一名称的首次出现保留强归因而重复位置被抑制，该模式在Pythia系列模型（160M-6.9B参数）中一致复现。

Conclusion: Unpack方法能够在不依赖真实电路标签的情况下追踪Transformer的机制结构，为大规模语言模型的机械可解释性提供了一种高效、无需干预的分析工具。

Original Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of transformers requires identifying not just which components matter but how they compose into the computational route that produced a prediction. Both attention and MLP follow a shared key-value template $φ(S)U$. We exploit this structure to develop Unpack, a backward recursion that decomposes credit through both sublayers, producing interaction strengths between any two components, named end-to-end paths with K/Q/V composition labels, and per-token attribution from a single forward pass, without intervention, gradients, or auxiliary training. We evaluate on the indirect object identification task. On GPT-2 small, the method recovers all three composition connections described by Wang et al. (2023), including the mode-specific routing of each connection (K, Q, or V). To test token-level attribution beyond trivial copying, we compare two occurrences of the same name in the same decomposition: the first mention retains strong credit while the duplicate-detection position is suppressed, a pattern absent in matched control prompts. Across the Pythia family from 160M to 6.9B parameters, this suppression pattern is consistently recovered at every scale, demonstrating that the method tracks mechanistic structure without ground-truth circuit labels. Code is available at https://github.com/Fun-Cry/unpacklm.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer的机械可解释性不仅需要识别哪些组件重要，还需理解它们如何组合成产生预测的计算路径。注意力和MLP层均遵循共享的键-值模板φ(S)U。我们利用这一结构开发了Unpack——一种反向递归方法，通过两个子层反向分解贡献，生成任意两个组件之间的交互强度（称为带有K/Q/V组合标签的端到端路径），以及单次前向传播中每个词元的归因，无需干预、梯度或辅助训练。我们在间接宾语识别任务上进行了评估。在GPT-2 small上，该方法恢复了Wang等人（2023）描述的全部三种组合连接，包括每种连接的模式特异性路由（K、Q或V）。为测试超越简单复制的词元级归因，我们比较了同一分解中同一名称的两次出现：首次提及保留强归因，而重复检测位置被抑制，这一模式在匹配的控制提示中不存在。在Pythia系列模型（160M至6.9B参数）中，该抑制模式在每个尺度上均被一致恢复，表明该方法能够在无真实电路标签的情况下追踪机制结构。代码见https://github.com/Fun-Cry/unpacklm。

</details>


### [153] [Parametric Prior Mapping Framework for Non-stationary Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23402)
*Jinglin Li,Jun Tan,QI Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数先验映射(PPM)框架，通过将参数化结构先验注入生成建模过程，在概率多元时间序列非平稳预测中兼顾了参数化方法的效率与生成模型的表达能力，实现了更优的准确率与计算效率权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有参数化方法具有强归纳偏置但缺乏灵活性，而深度生成模型虽表达能力强却需要大量数据和计算资源才能捕捉复杂的时间依赖关系。现有方法难以在表达能力与鲁棒性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: PPM框架利用参数化估计器推导动态自适应先验，通过可学习映射引导复杂预测分布的学习，将参数化结构先验注入生成建模过程，并采用混合目标函数进行训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，PPM在处理非平稳数据方面优于现有基线方法，在预测精度与计算效率之间实现了更优的权衡，并能提供校准良好的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: PPM成功融合了参数化方法与生成模型的优势，为非平稳概率多元时间序列预测提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Effectively modeling non-stationary dynamics in probabilistic multivariate time series(MTS) forecasting requires balancing expressiveness with robustness. Existing parametric approaches benefit from strong inductive biases but lack flexibility, whereas deep generative models struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies without extensive data and computation. We introduce Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM), a framework that injects parametric structural priors into a generative modeling process. Specifically, PPM utilizes a parametric estimator to derive a dynamic, adaptive prior that guides the learning of a complex predictive distribution via a learnable mapping. This design allows the model to retain the efficiency of parametric methods while exploiting the expressive power of generative models. Trained with a hybrid objective, PPM yields precise forecasts with well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Empirical results show that PPM outperforms existing baselines in handling non-stationary data, offering a superior trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM.

中文摘要翻译: 在概率多元时间序列（MTS）预测中，有效建模非平稳动态需要在表达力与鲁棒性之间取得平衡。现有参数化方法得益于强归纳偏置但缺乏灵活性，而深度生成模型则需要大量数据和计算资源才能捕捉复杂的时间依赖关系。本文提出参数先验映射（Parametric Prior Mapping, PPM）框架，将参数化结构先验注入生成建模过程。具体而言，PPM利用参数化估计器推导动态自适应的先验，通过可学习映射引导复杂预测分布的学习。该设计使模型既能保留参数化方法的效率，又能利用生成模型的表达能力。通过混合目标函数训练，PPM能够生成精确的预测结果并给出校准良好的不确定性估计。实验结果表明，PPM在处理非平稳数据方面优于现有基线方法，在预测精度与计算效率之间实现了更优的权衡。代码已开源：https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM。

</details>


### [154] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Corrective Diffusion Modeling for Meteorological Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23403)
*Rui Wang,Edoardo Pasetto,Amer Delilbasic,Morris Riedel,Kristel Michielsen,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典修正扩散模型，用于天气场的概率统计降尺度。在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈处插入变分量子电路层，作为潜在通道混合的非线性特征映射。实验表明，混合模型在多项指标上优于纯经典模型，但存在泛化差距，且量子电路规模和硬件部署仍是主要限制因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 统计降尺度需要从粗分辨率输入重建高分辨率输出，计算成本高昂。探索量子计算能否作为紧凑的非线性特征映射，在扩散UNet的瓶颈层实现潜在通道混合，从而提升降尺度性能。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典修正扩散模型，在扩散UNet最压缩的瓶颈处插入变分量子电路（VQC）层，保留回归分支完全经典。设计了通道内和跨通道两种量子电路结构，在10米风场分量上评估性能。

Result: 在2020验证集上，混合模型保持稳定，保留大尺度空间组织，在多个配置下改善了MAE和CRPS指标。结构诊断显示混合变体能保持动能谱和风速分布，但在尾部行为、极端风速定位和联合风场分量结构上产生可控变化。后端研究表明模拟设备噪声影响可忽略，但真实硬件受限于量子比特可用性和执行保真度。2021年分布外测试显示分布内增益不能均匀迁移，存在泛化差距。

Conclusion: 瓶颈层量子混合可以对天气统计降尺度做出有意义贡献，但电路规模和硬件部署仍是关键限制因素；未来需通过稳定化和正则化来缓解泛化差距。

Original Abstract: Statistical downscaling is a crucial component of the weather modeling field, where high-resolution outputs must be reconstructed from coarse-resolution inputs with the full cost of dynamical refinement. In this work, we investigate a hybrid quantum-classical corrective diffusion model for probabilistic statistical downscaling of weather fields. The proposed model inserts variational quantum circuit layers into the most compressed bottleneck of the diffusion UNet while leaving the regression branch fully classical. This placement tests whether quantum circuits can act as compact nonlinear feature maps for latent-channel mixing. We evaluate intra-channel and cross-channel ansätze on 10m wind components. On the 2020 validation set, the hybrid models remain stable, preserve the large-scale spatial organization of the generated wind fields, and improve both MAE and CRPS relative to a classical corrective diffusion model in several configurations. Structural diagnostics further show that the hybrid variants preserve kinetic-energy spectra and windspeed distributions similar to its classical counterpart while producing controlled changes in tail behavior, extreme-windspeed localization, and joint wind field components structure. Backend studies on the 2020 validation set show negligible impact from simulated device noise at the tested circuit scale, whereas real-hardware deployment remains limited by qubit availability and execution fidelity. The 2021 out-of-distribution test shows that these in-distribution gains do not transfer uniformly under temporal shift, revealing a generalization gap that motivates future mitigation through stabilization and regularization. These results show that bottleneck-level quantum hybridization can make a nontrivial contribution to weather statistical downscaling, while also highlighting that circuit scale and hardware deployment remain key limiting factors.

中文摘要翻译: 统计降尺度是天气建模领域的关键组成部分，其需要在动态精细化完全成本下，从粗分辨率输入重建高分辨率输出。在本工作中，我们研究了一种混合量子-经典修正扩散模型，用于天气场的概率统计降尺度。所提出的模型在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈处插入变分量子电路层，同时保留回归分支完全经典。这种放置方式测试了量子电路是否能够作为潜在通道混合的紧凑非线性特征映射。我们在10米风场分量上评估了通道内和跨通道两种电路结构。在2020验证集上，混合模型保持稳定，保留了生成风场的大尺度空间组织，并在多个配置下相比经典修正扩散模型改善了平均绝对误差（MAE）和连续排名概率分数（CRPS）。结构诊断进一步表明，混合变体能保持与经典对应模型相似的动能谱和风速分布，同时在尾部行为、极端风速定位和联合风场分量结构上产生可控变化。针对2020验证集的后端研究显示，在所测试的电路规模下，模拟设备噪声的影响可忽略，而真实硬件部署仍受限于量子比特可用性和执行保真度。2021年分布外测试表明，这些分布内增益在时间偏移下不能均匀迁移，揭示了一个泛化差距，这促使未来通过稳定化和正则化进行缓解。这些结果表明，瓶颈层量子混合可以对天气统计降尺度做出有意义的贡献，同时也凸显出电路规模和硬件部署仍是关键限制因素。

</details>


### [155] [Sample-wise Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Test-time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23411)
*Phuc Duc Nguyen,Quang Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对测试时自适应（TTA）的样本级定向后门攻击方法，通过元学习与优先级感知的梯度对齐策略，在保持全局标签分布不变的前提下，仅对携带触发器的样本实现高成功率定向误分类，更难被检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有针对TTA的类级定向攻击会因批次操作导致目标标签频率异常升高，容易被检测。本文旨在构造更现实、更隐蔽的威胁：攻击者仅对携带特定触发器的样本进行定向误分类，同时保持正常样本的标签分布不变，从而在无标签的TTA部署场景中逃避检测。

Method: 提出一种基于元学习的攻击方法，核心是优先级感知的梯度对齐策略。该策略将梯度更新形式化为椭圆信任域问题，缓解攻击成功率与分布隐蔽性之间的梯度不对齐矛盾，并提供理论保证来优化攻击目标。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-C上，针对多种TTA协议的实验表明：该方法实现了高定向攻击成功率，同时保持与无攻击基线一致的标签分布，难以被检测；且对现有防御方法具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的样本级定向攻击比现有的类级攻击更隐蔽、更现实，为TTA的安全研究提供了新的威胁模型与攻击范式，同时也揭示了当前TTA协议在安全方面的脆弱性。

Original Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) effectively counters distribution shifts but exposes models to adversarial manipulation via the unlabeled test stream. Existing class-wise targeted attacks remain impractical for stealthy exploitation in this setting: since TTA operates on batches, forcing a subset of samples toward a target label unintentionally pulls similar benign samples along, resulting in a conspicuously high frequency of the target label that is easy to detect. To capture a more realistic threat, we introduce a sample-wise targeted attack. Unlike prior approaches, the attacker aims to misclassify only inputs carrying an attacker-chosen trigger, while preserving the global label distribution of benign queries to evade detection. To achieve this, we propose a meta-learning-based attack with a novel priority-aware gradient alignment strategy that explicitly prioritizes attack success. The strategy formulates the gradient update as an ellipsoidal trust-region problem, mitigating the misalignment between attack success and distributional stealth, while providing theoretical guarantees for effective optimization of the attack objective in the presence of gradient misalignment. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C across TTA protocols demonstrate that our method achieves high targeted success rates while maintaining a label distribution that is consistent with the no-attack baseline, making it difficult to detect in unlabeled TTA deployment scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our attack shows strong robustness against existing defenses.

中文摘要翻译: 测试时自适应（TTA）能有效应对分布偏移，但通过无标签的测试数据流，模型会暴露在对抗性操纵的风险中。现有的类级定向攻击在该场景下难以实现隐蔽利用：由于TTA基于批量操作，强制部分样本朝目标标签移动会无意中将相似的正常样本也拉向该标签，导致目标标签出现频率异常高，容易被检测到。为捕捉更现实的威胁，本文提出一种样本级定向攻击。与以往方法不同，攻击者旨在仅对携带攻击者选定触发器的输入进行误分类，同时保持正常样本的全局标签分布不变以逃避检测。为此，我们提出一种基于元学习的攻击方法，并引入一种新颖的优先级感知梯度对齐策略，该策略显式优先保证攻击成功率。该策略将梯度更新形式化为椭圆信任域问题，缓解了攻击成功与分布隐蔽性之间的梯度不对齐矛盾，同时在存在梯度不对齐的情况下为攻击目标的有效优化提供了理论保证。在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-C上，针对多种TTA协议的大量实验表明，我们的方法实现了高定向攻击成功率，同时保持了与无攻击基线一致的标签分布，使其在无标签的TTA部署场景中难以被检测。此外，我们证明了该攻击对现有防御方法具有强鲁棒性。

</details>


### [156] [Reflex: Reinforcement Learning with Reflection Symmetry Exploitation in State-Based Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23415)
*Shuai Zhen,Yifan Zhang,Yuling Wang,Yanhua Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Reflex是一个利用反射对称性提升基于状态连续控制任务样本效率的强化学习框架，可与同策略和异策略RL算法无缝集成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习长期面临样本效率低下的问题，现有利用群不变MDP的方法主要关注基于图像的RL和旋转对称性（如SO(2)），而基于状态的RL和反射对称性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 形式化了两种反射对称性——轴向反射和双边反射，并刻画了它们对应的变换；基于对称性保持的最优值函数和策略的理论分析，通过原则性的对称性正则化机制将反射对称性融入策略学习。

Result: 将Reflex与PPO和SAC集成，在一系列OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准上评估，证明相比标准基线具有更优的性能和更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: 反射对称性可以有效提升基于状态的连续控制任务的样本效率和性能，Reflex框架为利用这种对称性提供了一种通用且原则性的方法。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning has long struggled with poor sample efficiency. One promising approach to mitigate this problem is leveraging group-invariant Markov Decision Processes ($G$-invariant MDPs). Existing works in this direction have primarily focused on image-based RL and rotational symmetry such as $\mathrm{SO(2)}$, leaving state-based RL and reflection symmetry largely underexplored. In this work, we focus on state-based continuous control tasks and exploit reflection symmetry by introducing Reflex, a paradigm that seamlessly integrates with both on-policy and off-policy RL algorithms. We formalize two types of reflection-axial reflection and bilateral reflection, and characterize their corresponding transformations. Building on a theoretical analysis of symmetry-preserving optimal value functions and policies, Reflex integrates reflection symmetry into policy learning through principled symmetry regularization mechanisms. We integrate Reflex with PPO and SAC, and evaluate it on a suite of OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance over standard baselines while improving sample efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TonyStark042/Reflex.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习长期以来一直面临样本效率低下的问题。缓解这一问题的一种有前景的方法是利用群不变马尔可夫决策过程（$G$-不变MDP）。现有相关工作主要关注基于图像的强化学习和旋转对称性（如$\mathrm{SO(2)}$），而基于状态的强化学习和反射对称性在很大程度上尚未被充分探索。本文聚焦于基于状态的连续控制任务，通过引入Reflex范式来利用反射对称性，该范式可以无缝集成到同策略和异策略强化学习算法中。我们形式化了两种类型的反射——轴向反射和双边反射，并刻画了它们对应的变换。基于对称性保持的最优值函数和策略的理论分析，Reflex通过原则性的对称性正则化机制将反射对称性融入策略学习。我们将Reflex与PPO和SAC集成，并在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准套件上进行评估，结果表明Reflex相比标准基线具有更优的性能，同时提升了样本效率。我们的代码已开源在https://github.com/TonyStark042/Reflex。

</details>


### [157] [An Open-Source Training Dataset for Foundation Models for Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23417)
*Aaron Klein,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Luca Thale-Bombien,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBO-Pile，首个包含50万+优化轨迹的大规模开源数据集，并基于该数据集训练了从2M到80M参数的基础模型，验证了大规模预训练对黑盒优化任务的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有黑盒优化方法依赖大量超参数调优且泛化能力有限；基础模型方法虽有前景，但此前工作局限于非公开数据集或纯合成数据，缺乏大规模、真实世界、公开可用的预训练数据，限制了可重复性和泛化能力。

Method: 构建了BBO-Pile数据集，包含500K+优化轨迹，覆盖3095个不同的黑盒问题和多种优化器；基于该数据集训练了多个规模的基础模型（2M~80M参数，200M~2B训练token），并研究了模型的计算扩展行为。

Result: 实验表明，大规模预训练是模仿黑盒优化方法的可行且有效的途径，在多个尺度上验证了模型性能随计算量增长而提升的扩展规律。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练能够有效学习黑盒优化原理，为未来该方向的研究奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Most black-box optimization methods require extensive hyperparameter tuning, often limiting their ability to generalize across different optimization domains. Foundation models for black-box optimization that learn optimization principles from a large collection of optimization trajectories offer a promising alternative, with the potential to outperform manually designed methods across diverse problem classes. However, prior work has either relied on non-public datasets or on purely synthetic data, limiting reproducibility and generalization to real-world problems. As a result, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of large-scale, real-world, publicly available pre-training data. We introduce BBO-Pile, the first open-source dataset comprising over 500K optimization trajectories evaluated across 3095 different black-boxes for different optimizers, which represents by far the largest public dataset for this task. Using this dataset, we train a family of foundation models at multiple scales, ranging from 2M to 80M parameters and from 200M to 2B training tokens, and study their scaling behavior with respect to compute. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pre-training is a viable and effective approach to imitate black-box optimization methods, paving the way for future research in this direction.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数黑盒优化方法需要大量的超参数调优，这往往限制了它们在不同优化领域的泛化能力。用于黑盒优化的基础模型从大量优化轨迹中学习优化原理，提供了一种有前景的替代方案，且有望在多样的问题类别上超越手工设计的方法。然而，先前的工作要么依赖非公开数据集，要么仅使用纯合成数据，这限制了其可重复性和在真实问题上的泛化能力。因此，该领域的进展一直受到缺乏大规模、真实世界、公开可用的预训练数据的制约。我们提出了BBO-Pile，这是首个包含超过50万条优化轨迹的开源数据集，这些轨迹在3095个不同的黑盒上使用不同优化器评估得到，是迄今为止该任务最大规模的公开数据集。利用这一数据集，我们训练了一系列不同规模的基础模型（参数从2M到80M，训练token从200M到2B），并研究了它们随计算量的扩展行为。我们的结果表明，大规模预训练是模仿黑盒优化方法的一种可行且有效的途径，为未来该方向的研究铺平了道路。

</details>


### [158] [Hinge Regression Trees and HRT-Boost: Newton-Optimized Oblique Learning for Compact Tabular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23422)
*Hongyi Li,Jun Xu,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出铰链回归树（HRT）框架，将倾斜决策树的每个分裂重新表述为两个线性预测器的非线性最小二乘问题，其最大/最小包络产生类ReLU表示能力。理论证明了HRT是通用逼近器且具有O(δ²)逼近率，并提出了HRT-Boost集成扩展。实验表明HRT在单树基线中极具竞争力，HRT-Boost优于强集成基线且模型更紧凑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习高质量的倾斜决策树因分裂优化的离散性和非凸性而面临重大挑战，现有方法难以有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出铰链回归树（HRT）框架，将每个倾斜分裂重新表述为两个线性预测器上的非线性最小二乘问题，其最大/最小包络赋予类ReLU表示能力。节点级优化可解释为阻尼牛顿法，并建立了回溯线搜索变体的节点目标单调递减性质。在此基础上提出HRT-Boost集成方法，将节点级牛顿更新与逐阶段函数梯度下降耦合。

Result: 理论证明了HRT是通用逼近器且具有O(δ²)的显式逼近率，HRT-Boost在平方损失下具有逐阶段经验风险降低保证。合成数据和真实世界基准实验表明，HRT与已有单树基线相比极具竞争力，HRT-Boost优于强集成基线且模型更紧凑。

Conclusion: HRT框架有效解决了倾斜决策树学习中的优化挑战，其理论基础和实验性能均表现出色，HRT-Boost在集成学习中实现了更优的精度-模型大小权衡。

Original Abstract: Learning high-quality oblique decision trees remains a significant challenge due to the discrete and non-convex nature of split optimization. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT) framework, which reframes each oblique split as a nonlinear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like representation capacity. We show that the resulting node-level optimization can be interpreted as a damped Newton method, and we establish the monotonic decrease of the node objective for its backtracking line-search variant. We establish, theoretically, that HRT is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate. Building upon this base learner, we propose HRT-Boost, a mathematically synergistic ensemble extension that couples node-level Newton updates with stage-wise functional gradient descent. We show that this ensemble construction admits a stage-wise empirical risk reduction guarantee under the squared loss. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that HRT is highly competitive with established single-tree baselines, and HRT-Boost compares favorably with strong ensemble baselines and often yields substantially more compact models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hongyi-Li-sz/HRT-Boost.

中文摘要翻译: 学习高质量的倾斜决策树仍是一个重大挑战，原因在于分裂优化的离散性和非凸性。我们提出了铰链回归树（HRT）框架，该框架将每个倾斜分裂重新表述为两个线性预测器上的非线性最小二乘问题，这两个预测器的最大/最小包络赋予了类ReLU的表示能力。我们证明由此产生的节点级优化可以解释为阻尼牛顿法，并为其回溯线搜索变体建立了节点目标的单调递减性质。我们从理论上证明了HRT是一个通用逼近器，具有显式的O(δ²)逼近率。在此基学习器的基础上，我们提出了HRT-Boost，一种数学上协同的集成扩展方法，它将节点级牛顿更新与逐阶段函数梯度下降相结合。我们证明，这种集成构造在平方损失下具有逐阶段经验风险降低保证。在合成数据和真实世界基准上的实验评估表明，HRT与已有的单树基线相比极具竞争力，而HRT-Boost与强集成基线相比表现优越，并且通常能产生更加紧凑的模型。代码已公开在https://github.com/Hongyi-Li-sz/HRT-Boost。

</details>


### [159] [Commutator-Induced Uncertainty in VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23449)
*Tahereh Dehdarirad,Michael Felsberg,Gabriel Eilertsen,Ziliang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种李群VAE框架，通过两阶段训练——先无约束训练并诊断非交换性，再引入变形稳定性约束——来显式建模潜在空间中的非交换结构，从而提升重构质量和解码器行为与潜在非交换性的一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对称感知VAE通过代数正则化强制交换性，这适用于交换变换群，但会抑制数据中内在的非交换结构。作者认为非交换性应当被显式诊断并反映在重构行为中，而非被正则化压制。

Method: 提出Lie Group VAE框架，分两阶段训练：第一阶段无结构约束训练，通过有限BCH偏差测量代数非交换性，通过重构顺序交换测试测量解码器顺序敏感性；第二阶段引入变形稳定性约束，配合数据驱动的校准常数，使解码器敏感性与代数非交换性对齐。

Result: 在dSprites、3DShapes、3DCars和CelebA上与beta-VAE、CLG-VAE、CFASL对比，该方法在合成基准上提升了重构质量，解码器行为与潜在非交换结构更一致；在CelebA上重构更忠实、因子遍历更明确，且展现出有意义的顺序依赖交互。

Conclusion: 非交换性不应被正则化消除，而应被显式诊断并建模；所提框架能有效对齐解码器敏感性与潜在非交换性，提升重构质量并保留数据内在的非交换结构。

Original Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) often struggle to represent non-commutative structure in learned latent spaces. Symmetry-aware VAEs commonly address this issue by enforcing commutativity through algebraic regularization, which is appropriate for commutative transformation groups but can suppress meaningful non-commutative structure when it is intrinsic to the data. We argue that non-commutativity should instead be explicitly diagnosed and reflected in reconstruction behavior. We introduce a Lie Group VAE framework that combines geometric and algebraic perspectives on uncertainty while separating discrete generative factors from continuous geometric transformations. In a first phase, the model is trained without structural constraints while algebraic non-commutativity is measured through finite Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff deviations and decoder order sensitivity is measured through reconstruction order-swap tests. These diagnostics reveal a scale mismatch between latent non-commutativity and reconstruction behavior under unconstrained training. In a second phase, we introduce a deformation-stability constraint with a data-driven calibration constant that aligns decoder sensitivity with algebraic non-commutativity. We evaluate the framework on dSprites, 3DShapes, 3DCars, and CelebA against generic and symmetry-aware baselines, including beta-VAE, CLG-VAE, and CFASL. Across synthetic benchmarks, the method improves reconstruction quality and yields decoder-level behavior more consistent with latent non-commutative structure. Qualitative analyses show clearer order-dependent latent compositions and more stable reconstructions. On CelebA, the model yields more faithful reconstructions and factor-specific latent traversals than CFASL, while also exhibiting meaningful order-dependent interactions between learned latent directions.

中文摘要翻译: 变分自编码器（VAE）在学习的潜在空间中往往难以表示非交换结构。对称感知VAE通常通过代数正则化强制交换性来解决这一问题，这种方法适用于交换变换群，但会抑制数据中固有的有意义的非交换结构。我们认为，非交换性应当被显式诊断并反映在重构行为中。我们提出了一种李群VAE框架，该框架在分离离散生成因子与连续几何变换的同时，结合了几何与代数视角下的不确定性。在第一阶段，模型在无结构约束下进行训练，同时通过有限Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff偏差测量代数非交换性，并通过重构顺序交换测试测量解码器顺序敏感性。这些诊断揭示了无约束训练下潜在非交换性与重构行为之间的尺度不匹配。在第二阶段，我们引入一种变形稳定性约束，配合数据驱动的校准常数，使解码器敏感性与代数非交换性对齐。我们在dSprites、3DShapes、3DCars和CelebA上，以beta-VAE、CLG-VAE和CFASL作为通用与对称感知基线进行评估。在合成基准上，该方法提升了重构质量，并使解码器层次的行为与潜在非交换结构更加一致。定性分析显示出更清晰的顺序依赖潜在组合与更稳定的重构。在CelebA上，该模型相比CFASL生成了更忠实的重构和因子特定的潜在遍历，同时在学习到的潜在方向之间展现出有意义的顺序依赖交互。

</details>


### [160] [Class-Dependent Hybrid Data Augmentation for Multiclass Migraine Classification under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23453)
*Elvin Somón,Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对先前的偏头痛分类研究进行了可重复性导向的再评估，纠正了数据泄漏和指标偏差问题。提出了临床驱动的亚型合并、类别依赖的混合增强策略以及保真度不对称概念，实验表明所提框架在宏平均F1上优于单独增强方法和无增强基线，最高达到0.914。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 先前的偏头痛分类研究存在数据泄漏和指标偏差等方法论缺陷，导致性能估计被高估。同时，现有数据增强方法未充分考虑类别间的样本量差异以及生成数据的保真度问题，需要更严谨和系统的方法来提升分类性能的鲁棒性。

Method: (i) 按照ICHD-3 §1.2.3标准对两种偏瘫亚型进行临床驱动的类别聚合；(ii) 提出类别依赖的混合增强策略，根据每类样本量分配不同的生成方法；(iii) 引入保真度不对称概念，提出比例约束增长替代完全的类别平衡。实验采用两阶段协议，在400名患者的七种偏头痛亚型数据集上进行，使用分层5折交叉验证和宏平均F1作为主要指标。

Result: 纠正方法缺陷后，基线宏平均F1从高估值降至0.71。所提框架在8个分类器上的平均宏平均F1为0.862，优于高斯Copula（0.836）、CTGAN（0.815）和无增强基线（0.801）。在FT-Transformer上使用比例增强达到峰值0.914。无增强的FT-Transformer基线为0.896，表明临床类别聚合贡献了大部分绝对提升。

Conclusion: 临床动机的类别聚合是提升性能的主导因素，所提框架的主要可量化贡献在于提高了跨分类器的平均鲁棒性，这凸显了问题公式化（problem formulation）在分类任务中的关键作用。

Original Abstract: We conducted a reproducibility-oriented re-evaluation of prior migraine classification studies, correcting for data leakage and metric bias. We then introduced (i) a clinically motivated aggregation of two hemiplegic subtypes following ICHD-3 §1.2.3, (ii) a class-dependent hybrid augmentation strategy that assigns generation methods based on per-class sample size, and (iii) the concept of fidelity asymmetry, motivating proportionally constrained growth as an alternative to full class balance. Experiments were performed on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes under a two-stage protocol, including the six-class configuration described above. Models were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with macro-averaged F1 as the primary metric.
  Correcting methodological flaws reduces previously inflated performance estimates, with the corrected macro-F1 baseline standing at 0.71. The proposed framework consistently outperformed individual augmenters in macro-F1 averaged across the eight evaluated classifiers (0.862 vs. 0.836 for Gaussian Copula, 0.815 for CTGAN, and 0.801 for the no-augmentation baseline), and achieved its peak result of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation. The no-augmentation FT-Transformer baseline (0.896) shows that, at the per-classifier ceiling, clinically motivated class aggregation accounts for most of the absolute improvement; the framework's principal measurable contribution is the gain in average robustness across classifiers, highlighting the dominant role of problem formulation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们对先前的偏头痛分类研究进行了可重复性导向的再评估，纠正了数据泄漏和指标偏差问题。随后引入：(i) 按照ICHD-3 §1.2.3标准对两种偏瘫亚型进行临床驱动的类别聚合，(ii) 一种类别依赖的混合增强策略，根据每类样本量分配生成方法，以及(iii) 保真度不对称概念，提出比例约束增长作为完全类别平衡的替代方案。实验在包含400名患者、涵盖七种偏头痛亚型的数据集上进行，采用两阶段协议，包括上述六类配置。模型使用分层5折交叉验证评估，以宏平均F1作为主要指标。纠正方法论缺陷可降低此前被高估的性能估计，修正后的宏平均F1基线为0.71。所提框架在8个评估分类器上的宏平均F1均值上始终优于单独增强方法（0.862对比高斯Copula的0.836、CTGAN的0.815以及无增强基线的0.801），并在FT-Transformer配合比例增强下达到峰值0.914。无增强的FT-Transformer基线（0.896）表明，在单分类器上限处，临床驱动的类别聚合贡献了大部分绝对提升；该框架的主要可量化贡献在于提高了跨分类器的平均鲁棒性，凸显了问题公式化的主导作用。

</details>


### [161] [Automated Random Embedding for Practical Bayesian Optimization with Unknown Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23473)
*Hong Qian,Xiang Shu,Xiang Xia,Xuhui Liu,Yangde Fu,Bei Liang,Huibin Wang,Liang Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态共享嵌入贝叶斯优化（DSEBO），一种针对有效维度未知的高维贝叶斯优化自动随机嵌入方法。DSEBO从低维子空间开始，在当前解初步收敛时自动切换到更高维子空间，动态决定下一子空间维度，并在子空间间共享查询解以实现更好的初始化。理论推导了遗憾界，实验表明该方法在优化遗憾和时间上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统随机嵌入方法需要预先知道任务的有效维度来选择子空间维度，但有效维度往往难以事先确定，且通过试错法估计会消耗大量资源。这影响了子空间维度选择和优化性能。

Method: 提出DSEBO方法：(1)从低维子空间开始优化；(2)当当前子空间中的解呈现初步收敛时，自动切换到更高维子空间；(3)基于不同子空间中解的质量动态决定下一子空间的维度；(4)在子空间间共享已查询的解，为新子空间提供更好的初始化。

Result: 理论推导了DSEBO的遗憾界，证明其能更好平衡逼近误差与优化误差。在变维度函数和真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，相比现有最优方法，跨不同子空间的交替优化在高维优化中在优化遗憾和时间上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: DSEBO通过动态调整子空间维度和跨子空间共享信息，有效解决了有效维度未知情况下高维贝叶斯优化的维度选择难题，在理论和实验上均验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely employed for optimizing complex black-box functions but struggles with the curse of dimensionality. Random embedding, as a dimension reduction strategy, simplifies tasks that possess the effective dimension by optimizing within a low-dimensional subspace. However, determining the effective dimension of a task in advance remains a significant challenge, which influences the selection of the subspace dimensionality and the optimization performance. Traditional methods use fixed subspace dimensions provided by experts or rely on trial and error to estimate subspace dimensions with resources consumed. To this end, this paper proposes an automated random embedding for high-dimensional Bayesian optimization with unknown effective dimension, called Dynamic Shared Embedding Bayesian Optimization (DSEBO). DSEBO starts with a low dimension and switches to a higher subspace if the solutions in the current subspace show preliminary convergence. DSEBO dynamically determines the dimension of the next subspace based on the quality of the solutions in different subspaces and shares the queried solutions with the new subspace for a better initialization. Theoretically, we derive a regret bound for DSEBO and demonstrate that DSEBO can better balance approximation and optimization errors. Extensive experiments on functions with dimensionality of varying magnitudes and real-world tasks with unknown effective dimensions reveal that, compared with state-of-the-art methods, alternating optimization across different subspaces results in significant improvements in high-dimensional optimization, both in terms of optimization regret and time.

中文摘要翻译: 贝叶斯优化被广泛用于优化复杂的黑箱函数，但其面临维度灾难的挑战。随机嵌入作为一种降维策略，通过在低维子空间内进行优化来简化具有有效维度的任务。然而，预先确定任务的有效维度仍然是一个重大挑战，它影响着子空间维度的选择和优化性能。传统方法使用专家提供的固定子空间维度，或依赖于试错法来估计子空间维度，这消耗了大量资源。为此，本文提出了一种针对有效维度未知的高维贝叶斯优化的自动化随机嵌入方法，称为动态共享嵌入贝叶斯优化（DSEBO）。DSEBO从低维子空间开始，当当前子空间中的解呈现初步收敛时，切换到更高的子空间。DSEBO基于不同子空间中解的质量动态决定下一子空间的维度，并与新子空间共享已查询的解以实现更好的初始化。理论上，我们推导了DSEBO的遗憾界，并证明了DSEBO能更好地平衡逼近误差与优化误差。在具有不同规模维度的函数以及有效维度未知的真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，与现有最优方法相比，跨不同子空间的交替优化在高维优化中在优化遗憾和时间上均有显著提升。

</details>


### [162] [CoSPlay: Cooperative Self-Play at Test-Time with Self-Generated Code and Unit Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23491)
*Zhangyi Hu,Chenhui Liu,Tian Huang,Jindong Li,Yang Yang,Jiemin Wu,Zining Zhong,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoSPlay 是一个无训练、无 Ground-Truth 单元测试的框架，通过代码与单元测试的协同自我博弈，在推理阶段迭代优化两者质量，显著提升 LLM 代码生成的 Best-of-N（BoN）性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的 RLVR 方法依赖昂贵的 Ground-Truth 单元测试进行训练，而 TTS 方法在缺乏 GT 时性能大幅下降。自生成单元测试往往含有噪声且与错误代码耦合，且无法在没有可靠代码的情况下验证自身质量。核心挑战在于如何同时提升代码与单元测试的质量。

Method: CoSPlay 首先探索多样化解法并识别潜在失败模式以生成具有区分力的单元测试思路；然后利用代码-单元测试执行矩阵的双向通过计数信号，迭代剪枝/修复弱代码、更新不可靠的单元测试，使两个池共同进化；最后当多个代码通过数相同时，从最大的输出一致性聚类中选择最终代码。

Result: 在四个基准上，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 的平均 BoN 从 22.1% 提升至 33.2%，单元测试准确率从 14.6% 提升至 78.3%，匹配或超越 RLVR 模型 CURE-7B；应用于 CURE-7B 进一步提升 BoN 5.7%；在不同基座模型上均有效，且在可比 token 预算下优于其他 GT-free TTS 方法。

Conclusion: CoSPlay 展示了一种无需任何 Ground-Truth 数据的可扩展推理策略，能够在不依赖 GT 单元测试的条件下，通过协同自我博弈实现具有竞争力的代码生成性能。

Original Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and Test-Time Scaling (TTS) have advanced LLM code generation through executable verification. Yet Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs) remain a bottleneck: SOTA RLVR methods require them for costly training, while existing TTS methods lose competitiveness without them. This motivates GT-free TTS, where existing methods directly use self-generated UTs to refine and select code candidates. Yet such UTs are often noisy or spuriously coupled with wrong code, and UT quality in turn cannot be validated without reliable code. The key challenge is therefore to jointly improve both. To this end, we present CoSPlay, a GT-free, training-free framework that jointly improves codes and UTs through cooperative self-play. It first explores diverse solution ideas and identifies their potential failure modes to produce discriminative UT ideas. It then uses bidirectional pass-count signals from the Code-UT execution matrix to iteratively prune or fix weak codes and refresh or replace unreliable UTs, letting the two pools co-evolve. Finally, when multiple codes remain tied at the highest pass count, it picks the final code from the largest output-consensus cluster, since correct codes agree on the same inputs while wrong codes diverge. Experiments on four challenging benchmarks show that CoSPlay on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct improves average BoN from 22.1% to 33.2% and UT accuracy from 14.6% to 78.3%, matching or surpassing the RLVR model CURE-7B. When applied to CURE-7B, it further improves BoN by 5.7%. CoSPlay also generalizes across diverse backbones and outperforms GT-free TTS baselines under comparable token budgets, with continued gains as the budget scales up. These results suggest a scalable inference strategy for competitive code generation without any GT data.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）和测试时扩展（TTS）通过可执行验证推动了LLM代码生成的发展。然而，地面真值单元测试（GT UTs）仍然是一个瓶颈：最先进的RLVR方法在成本高昂的训练中依赖它们，而现有的TTS方法在没有GT UTs的情况下会失去竞争力。这催生了无GT的TTS方法，现有方法直接使用自生成的UTs来优化和筛选代码候选。然而，这类UTs往往带有噪声或与错误代码虚假耦合，同时没有可靠代码也无法验证UT质量。因此，关键挑战在于同时改进两者。为此，我们提出CoSPlay，一个无GT、无训练的框架，通过协作式自我博弈联合改进代码和UTs。它首先探索多样化解法思路并识别其潜在失败模式，以生成具有区分力的UT思路。然后，它利用代码-UT执行矩阵中的双向通过计数信号，迭代剪枝或修复弱代码，刷新或替换不可靠的UTs，使两个池共同进化。最后，当多个代码在最高通过计数上持平，它从最大的输出一致性聚类中选择最终代码，因为正确代码在同一输入上一致，而错误代码则分化。在四个挑战性基准上的实验表明，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上的CoSPlay将平均BoN从22.1%提升至33.2%，UT准确率从14.6%提升至78.3%，匹配或超越了RLVR模型CURE-7B。当应用于CURE-7B时，它进一步将BoN提升了5.7%。CoSPlay还能在多种基座模型上泛化，并在可比token预算下优于无GT的TTS基线，且随着预算扩大持续获得增益。这些结果表明，这是一种无需任何GT数据即可实现具有竞争力代码生成的可扩展推理策略。

</details>


### [163] [VACE: Learning Geometrically Structured Representations for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23504)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Isaac Triguero,Julián Luengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VACE提出了一种基于速度一致性的自监督多元时间序列异常检测方法，通过无负样本、无合成异常的速度对齐通道嵌入，在嵌入空间中构建紧凑且方向一致的正常区域，在TSB-AD-M基准上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对比学习自监督方法通过成对采样启发式间接塑造嵌入空间，无法显式控制距离评分所需的几何结构（如正常表示的紧凑性和距离的方向意义），导致检测性能受限。

Method: VACE方法包括：(1) 通道感知编码器，通过速度一致性目标训练（无需负样本和合成异常），使正常轨迹局部平滑且对齐；(2) 测试时结合马氏距离位置分数和速度库方向分数，以乘法方式标记同时偏离分布和动态异常的点。

Result: 在TSB-AD-M基准上的严格评估中，VACE以更简单的方法显著优于那些在更大预算下训练的复杂方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过速度对齐的通道嵌入，无需负样本和合成异常即可构建方向一致的紧凑正常区域，为多元时间序列异常检测提供了一种简洁而有效的新范式。

Original Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is a critical task across a wide range of real-world applications, where abnormal behaviour is rare, labels are unavailable, and the cost of a miss is high. The central challenge is learning a characterisation of normality precise enough to flag deviations. Representation self-supervised learning, typically through contrastive approaches, addresses this by embedding temporal patches into a latent space where normality occupies a well-defined region, with anomalies detected by geometric deviation. However, contrastive approaches shape this space indirectly through pair-sampling heuristics, providing no explicit control over the geometric structure that distance-based scoring requires. This means how tightly normal representations are grouped, and whether distances are directionally meaningful. We present VACE (Velocity-Aligned Channel Embeddings), a self-supervised anomaly detection method that represents normality as a compact, directionally coherent region in the embedding space. To this end, VACE trains a channel-aware encoder through a velocity-consistency objective, with no negatives and no synthetic anomalies, so that normal trajectories are locally smooth and aligned. At test time, a Mahalanobis positional score and a velocity-bank directional score are combined multiplicatively, flagging points that are simultaneously off-distribution and dynamically atypical. Despite its simplicity, VACE achieves state-of-the-art performance on TSB-AD-M under rigorous evaluation, significantly outperforming more complex methods trained on substantially larger budgets.

中文摘要翻译: 多元时间序列中的异常检测是众多实际应用中的关键任务，其特点是异常行为罕见、标签不可获取且漏检代价高昂。核心挑战在于学习一种足够精确的正常性表征以标记偏差。表征自监督学习（通常通过对比方法）通过将时间片段嵌入到潜在空间中（其中正常性占据一个明确定义的区域），并通过几何偏差检测异常。然而，对比方法通过成对采样启发式间接塑造该空间，未能对基于距离评分所需的几何结构提供显式控制——即正常表示被分组的紧密程度以及距离是否具有方向意义。本文提出VACE（速度对齐通道嵌入，Velocity-Aligned Channel Embeddings），一种自监督异常检测方法，将正常性表示为嵌入空间中紧凑且方向一致的区域。为此，VACE通过速度一致性目标（无需负样本和合成异常）训练通道感知编码器，使得正常轨迹局部平滑且对齐。在测试阶段，马氏距离位置分数与速度库方向分数以乘法方式组合，标记同时偏离分布且动态异常的点。尽管方法简洁，VACE在TSB-AD-M基准的严格评估下取得了最先进的性能，显著优于在更大预算下训练的更为复杂的方法。

</details>


### [164] [Precise: SDE-Consistent Stochastic Sampling for RL Post-Training of Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23522)
*Jade Zou,Tao Huang,Weijie Kong,Junzhe Li,Yue Wu,Qi Tian,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jianwei Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Zhao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Precise，一种新的随机采样器，通过平衡探索与稳定性、保持SDE一致性，显著提升了流匹配生成器中在线强化学习的奖励优化效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在流匹配生成器中，在线强化学习需要将确定性采样轨迹转化为随机策略（使用SDE替代ODE），但现有采样器在小步数离散化时存在探索与稳定性的矛盾，以及离散化噪声过大或无法保证收敛到数据分布的问题。

Method: 将采样器设计分解为两个组件：1) 选择合适的随机探索量，通过分析探索与稳定性的权衡，推导出平衡二者的SDE调度；2) 忠实离散化SDE。提出Precise采样器，通过冻结干净隐变量后验均值的新型近似方法，使去噪轨迹保持SDE一致性，解决标准采样器中的过量噪声问题。

Result: 实验表明，Precise在RL奖励优化中更快更稳定，在PickScore、HPSv2.1等对齐指标上达到SOTA，同时训练时间减少13.1%-53.2%。

Conclusion: 采样器设计对RL在流匹配中的奖励优化至关重要，Precise通过平衡探索与稳定性并保持SDE一致性，实现了高效稳定的对齐性能提升。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective way to improve prompt alignment and perceptual quality in diffusion and flow-matching generators. A critical step for applying online RL to flow matching is turning the deterministic sampling trajectory into a stochastic policy, typically by replacing the reverse-time Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) with a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE). The stochastic sampler, controlling the exploration behavior and denoising dynamics, is thus part of the policy, and its design can significantly affect the reward optimization performance. We break down the sampler design into two interdependent components: choosing the right amount of stochastic exploration, and discretizing the resulting SDE faithfully at the small step counts used in RL. To address the first component, we analyze the inherent tension between exploration and stability in denoising and derive an SDE schedule that balances the two. Turning to the discretization challenge, we use a toy example to show that existing samplers can deviate from the flow-matching process, either by introducing excessive discretization noise or by relying on heuristic rules that do not guarantee convergence to the data distribution. To address these issues, we propose Precise, a new stochastic sampler that balances effective exploration with stability. Crucially, Precise keeps the denoising trajectory SDE-consistent through a novel approximation that freezes the clean-latent posterior mean, resolving the excess noise issue in standard samplers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this formulation leads to significantly faster and more stable reward optimization via reinforcement learning, achieving state-of-the-art alignment scores (e.g., PickScore, HPSv2.1) while requiring 13.1-53.2% less wall-clock training time to match the best in-domain performance of prior samplers.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习已成为提升扩散模型和流匹配生成器中提示对齐与感知质量的有效方法。将在线强化学习应用于流匹配的一个关键步骤是将确定性采样轨迹转化为随机策略，通常通过用随机微分方程替代逆向常微分方程来实现。控制探索行为和去噪动态的随机采样器是策略的一部分，其设计会显著影响奖励优化性能。我们将采样器设计分解为两个相互依赖的组成部分：选择合适的随机探索量，以及在强化学习使用的小步数下忠实地离散化所得到的随机微分方程。针对第一个问题，我们分析了去噪过程中探索与稳定性之间的固有张力，并推导出一种平衡二者的随机微分方程调度。针对离散化挑战，我们通过一个玩具示例证明现有采样器可能偏离流匹配过程，要么引入过多的离散化噪声，要么依赖无法保证收敛到数据分布的启发式规则。为解决这些问题，我们提出了Precise，一种平衡有效探索与稳定性的新型随机采样器。关键之处在于，Precise通过一种冻结干净隐变量后验均值的新型近似方法，使去噪轨迹保持随机微分方程一致性，解决了标准采样器中的过量噪声问题。大量实验表明，该公式通过强化学习实现了显著更快、更稳定的奖励优化，在达到先前采样器最佳域内性能的同时，训练时间减少了13.1%-53.2%，取得了最先进的对齐分数（如PickScore、HPSv2.1）。

</details>


### [165] [When One Point Is Not Enough: Addressing Ambiguous Instances in Dimensionality Reduction by Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23540)
*Diede P. M. van der Hoorn,Alessio Arleo,Fernando V. Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出降维可视化中一个被忽视的伪影来源——模糊实例（ambiguous instances），即高维空间中与多个互不相似邻域高度相似的实例，并提出基于图的复制嵌入方法来解决部分邻域嵌入问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 降维是一种有损过程，投影中存在视觉伪影。现有研究忽视了模糊实例这一伪影来源：这类实例在降维后只能被映射到单个点，导致其仅能表示部分邻域结构，造成信息丢失。

Method: 提出一种基于图的方法，识别高维空间中的模糊实例，并在投影中将其复制为多个点，每个副本分别放置在其对应的邻域中。该方法基于UMAP实现，但可推广到其他基于局部图的降维技术。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够揭示投影中先前被隐藏的邻域成员关系，减少部分邻域嵌入现象，并通过定量分析进一步支持了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 复制模糊实例的多点嵌入策略能有效缓解降维中的部分邻域嵌入问题，帮助用户更完整地理解高维数据的局部结构。

Original Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) methods are widely used to visualize high-dimensional data. One key task in DR-based analysis is discovering neighborhoods, which relies on analyzing the fine-grained local structure of a projection. However, DR is an inherently lossy process; no technique can perfectly preserve the high-dimensional relationships, and projections therefore contain visual artifacts. In this paper, we highlight a typically overlooked source of visual artifacts: ambiguous instances. These are instances that are highly similar to multiple mutually dissimilar neighborhoods in the high-dimensional space. Standard DR methods cannot faithfully project such instances, since each data instance is mapped to a single point in the visual space. As a result, such an instance is placed in only one of its neighborhoods (or in none at all), so only part of its neighborhood structure is represented. We call this distortion partial neighborhood embedding. In this paper, we introduce a graph-based approach that identifies ambiguous instances and replicates them as multiple points in the projection, placing each copy within its respective neighborhood. We use UMAP for our results, but our approach also generalizes to other local graph-based DR techniques, and we show that our approach reveals previously hidden neighborhood memberships in projections and reduces partial neighborhood embedding across multiple examples, and is further supported by quantitative analyses.

中文摘要翻译: 降维方法被广泛用于可视化高维数据。在基于降维的分析中，一个关键任务是发现邻域，这依赖于分析投影的精细局部结构。然而，降维本质上是一个有损过程；没有任何技术能够完美保留高维关系，因此投影中必然包含视觉伪影。在本文中，我们强调了一个常被忽视的视觉伪影来源：模糊实例。这些实例在高维空间中与多个互不相似的邻域高度相似。标准的降维方法无法忠实地投影这样的实例，因为每个数据实例在可视空间中被映射到单个点。因此，这样的实例被放置在其中一个邻域（或根本不放在任何邻域），导致只有其部分邻域结构得到表示。我们将这种失真称为部分邻域嵌入。本文提出了一种基于图的方法，该方法识别模糊实例并将其复制为投影中的多个点，将每个副本放置在其对应的邻域中。我们在UMAP上展示了结果，但我们的方法也可以推广到其他基于局部图的降维技术。我们通过多个示例表明，该方法能够揭示投影中先前被隐藏的邻域成员关系，减少部分邻域嵌入，并且定量分析进一步支持了该方法的有效性。

</details>


### [166] [MARS: Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23563)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Afsaneh M. Nejad,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARS方法，通过引入相对边际系数加权离散排名，解决标准CD图忽视模型性能差距大小的问题，实现更真实的统计比较。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准CD图仅依赖离散排名，忽略了模型之间性能差距的大小，存在"幅度盲视"问题，导致统计结果无法真实反映模型间的实际差异。

Method: 提出MARS方法，引入相对边际系数作为离散排名的权重。该系数基于最佳与最差性能者之间的距离对排名进行缩放，并通过动态投影处理边界情况，随后计算CD值。

Result: MARS能够更真实地统计表示模型性能差异，在广泛实验设置下提供更多关于方法实际表现的分析见解。

Conclusion: MARS通过量级感知的排名统计，克服了标准CD图的幅度盲视问题，为机器学习模型评估提供了更精确的统计比较工具。

Original Abstract: Comprehensive evaluation of machine learning models is the key to make sure that they perform as robustly and consistently as desired. In order to summarize the experimental results and pick a winner, Critical Difference (CD) diagrams are used. Standard CD diagrams rely on discrete ranks, discarding the magnitude of performance gaps between models, raising an issue which we call magnitude-blindness. In order to address this issue, we propose Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS) that incorporates a relative margin coefficient as a weight for the discrete ranks. This coefficient scales ranks based on the distance between the best and worst performers, with a dynamic projection to handle boundary cases. Followed by the calculation of a CD value, MARS results in a more realistic statistical representation of differences of model performances and more insights on how methods actually perform in vast and extensive experimental settings.

中文摘要翻译: 全面评估机器学习模型是确保其按预期稳健且一致地运行的关键。为了总结实验结果并选出优胜者，常使用关键差异（CD）图。标准CD图依赖于离散排名，忽略了模型之间性能差距的大小，这引发了一个我们称之为"幅度盲视"的问题。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了量级感知排名统计（MARS），它引入了一个相对边际系数作为离散排名的权重。该系数根据最佳与最差表现者之间的距离对排名进行缩放，并通过动态投影处理边界情况。在计算CD值之后，MARS能得到更真实的模型性能差异统计表示，并在广泛且深入的实验设置中提供更多关于方法实际表现的分析见解。

</details>


### [167] [Cost-Effective Model Evaluation with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23595)
*Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaEvaluator框架，通过元学习在参考模型池上获得可迁移初始化，实现无需标签、无需重训练即可评估任意新模型性能，显著降低成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有评估流程依赖昂贵的人工标注、重复微调或狭窄假设，无法跨模型族迁移，难以验证新模型在未见无标签数据上的可靠性。

Method: 基于元学习，在参考模型池上学习可迁移初始化，使新模型无需重新训练即可被准确评估，成本在模型池中分摊。

Result: 大量实验表明MetaEvaluator在显著降低成本的条件下产生稳定准确的性能估计，使无标签数据上的可扩展模型评估变得可行。

Conclusion: MetaEvaluator是首个能够对完全无标签数据集上的新模型进行评估的模型无关框架，实现了低成本、可扩展的模型评估。

Original Abstract: The rapid growth of machine learning has produced an ever-expanding ecosystem of models, making it increasingly challenging to verify the reliability of newly released models on unseen, unlabeled data. Conventional evaluation pipelines depend on expensive annotation, repeated fine-tuning, or narrow assumptions that fail to transfer across model families. We present MetaEvaluator, a cost-effective, model-agnostic framework for rapid, label-free assessment of unseen models spanning diverse architectures and modalities. MetaEvaluator leverages meta-learning over a pool of reference models to obtain a transferable initialization, enabling accurate evaluation of new models while amortizing cost across the pool and removing the need for per-model retraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first model-agnostic framework capable of evaluating new models on entirely unlabeled datasets. Extensive experiments show that MetaEvaluator produces stable and accurate performance estimates at substantially reduced cost compared to conventional approaches, making scalable benchmarking of emerging models on unlabeled data practical.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习的快速发展催生了一个不断扩展的模型生态系统，使得验证新发布模型在未见无标签数据上的可靠性变得越来越困难。传统的评估流程依赖昂贵的人工标注、重复微调或无法跨模型族迁移的狭窄假设。我们提出MetaEvaluator，一个成本高效、模型无关的框架，用于对跨越不同架构和模态的未见模型进行快速、无需标签的评估。MetaEvaluator利用元学习在参考模型池上获得可迁移初始化，从而能够准确评估新模型，同时将成本分摊到整个模型池中，消除了每次重新训练的需求。据我们所知，这是首个能够在完全无标签数据集上评估新模型的模型无关框架。大量实验表明，与常规方法相比，MetaEvaluator在显著降低成本的条件下产生稳定准确的性能估计，使得在无标签数据上对新兴模型进行可扩展的基准测试变得切实可行。

</details>


### [168] [Understanding Goal Generalisation in Sequential Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23565)
*Jason Ross Brown,Edward James Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习智能体在训练分布外环境中的目标导向泛化行为，提出“潜在策略梯度”方法，通过模拟训练过程中低维潜在变量的演化来预测智能体在分布外环境中的行为，并揭示了显著特征驱动泛化、早期学习目标持续影响后续目标等关键现象。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习智能体在训练分布外环境中常表现出非预期的目标导向行为，但现有研究缺乏对智能体如何基于训练历史泛化到新环境的原则性理解，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出潜在策略梯度（latent policy gradients）方法，通过模拟训练过程中低维潜在变量的演化（依据训练目标下能获得高回报的方式），并基于潜在变量到行为的简单映射模型，预测训练流程可能诱导出的分布外行为。

Result: 研究了超过100个顺序训练流程，在250多个分布外环境中评估行为，发现显著特征驱动泛化，早期学习目标会持续影响后续目标；潜在策略梯度方法在预测准确率上表现优异，能泛化到未见过的训练流程类型，且具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 分布外RL智能体行为虽然依赖于整个训练流程，但这种依赖关系存在可捕捉的内在结构，为从发展视角理解目标泛化奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents often exhibit unintended goal-directed behaviour outside their training distribution, but we currently lack a principled understanding of how such agents will generalise to novel environments based on their training history.
